Appendix A

Color Sorter Firmware

Colour-based product sorting conveyor, STM32F103C8T6

Enter steps through matches · Shift+Enter goes back

7,118 lines across 8 files Library and generated code excluded

What this listing contains

The whole of the application firmware for the sorting station: the main program and the three device drivers written for it. Files produced by STM32CubeMX and the STM32Cube HAL library are not reproduced, because none of them was written for this project.

The code appears exactly as it was compiled and loaded onto the board, with no edit of any kind. Its comments are written in Vietnamese without diacritics — the form in which the firmware was developed. They are kept rather than translated so that this listing matches the source on the device line for line, and they carry most of the design history: the failures observed on the rig, the measurement behind each constant, and the reason for each defensive check.

Click any line number to put that line in the address bar, so a specific line can be cited or shared. Copy on each file header takes the whole file as plain text with straight quotation marks intact, which is the form a compiler accepts — a word processor that substitutes curly quotation marks will make the same code fail to build.

47/* ==========================================================================
48 * KIEN TRUC: HAI KHOI DOC LAP CHAY SONG SONG TRONG SUPERLOOP
49 * ==========================================================================
50 *
51 * KHOI A - CAM BIEN + DIEU KHIEN NGUON (chay tu luc CAP NGUON, VO DIEU KIEN)
52 * gy33_quet() : doc TCS34725 (GY-33) LIEN TUC, phat SU KIEN "XANH DUONG"
53 * ir_quet() : doc E18-D80NK LIEN TUC - CHI de HIEN THI va DEM
54 * nut_quet() : doc START/STOP - CHI dong/cat RELAY MOTOR, khong gi khac
55 *
56 * KHOI B - CO CAU GAT (may trang thai gat_task)
57 * Nghe DUY NHAT MOT dau vao: su kien XANH DUONG do khoi A phat ra.
58 *
59 * ⚠⚠ LIEN DONG BAT BIEN (yeu cau bat buoc cua he):
60 * Khoi B KHONG CO bat ky duong tin hieu nao tu E18 va tu NUT NHAN.
61 * Bien g_ir_vat TUYET DOI khong duoc xuat hien ben trong gat_task().
62 * LY DO KY THUAT: day tin hieu E18 chay song song day dong luc motor DC 24V
63 * nen bi kich gia thuong xuyen. O ban cu, E18 la diem VAO cua chu trinh phan
64 * loai => moi lan nhieu kich gia la mot lan servo gat oan ("servo giat lien
65 * tuc"). Cat han duong nay thi nhieu tren PA0 chi lam sai BO DEM hien thi,
66 * khong the lam co cau chap hanh chuyen dong.
67 *
68 * ★★ TRINH TU SUA 14/08/2026 - HOME DOI VE 90 DO, XANH CO CHANG NANG CANH.
69 *
70 * TRINH TU PHOI XANH (3 chang - dung theo yeu cau van hanh moi):
71 * GY-33 ket luan XANH DUONG XAC_NHAN_XANH lan LIEN TIEP
72 * -> [G_NANG] servo truot 90 -> 180 do (nang canh len), cho "den noi"
73 * -> [G_CHO_TROI] servo GIU NGUYEN 180 do trong TRE_GAT_XANH_MS (2s)
74 * (= thoi gian phoi troi tu cam bien toi canh gat;
75 * ⚠ dem TU LUC TOI 180 do, KHONG phai tu luc thay mau)
76 * -> [G_DANG_GAT] servo truot 180 -> 0 do (gat phoi ra), cho "den noi"
77 * -> [G_GIU] dung yen tai 0 do trong GIU_XANH_MS (1s)
78 * -> [G_VE_HOME] servo truot 0 -> 90 do (ve home), cho bao "den noi"
79 * -> [G_KHOA] CHI vu trang lai khi DU CA HAI dieu kien:
80 * (1) GY-33 da doc ra KHAC XANH it nhat MOT lan
81 * (phoi da troi ra khoi tam nhin cam bien)
82 * (2) da qua NGHI_SAU_GAT_MS ke tu luc ve toi home
83 *
84 * TRINH TU PHOI DO (2 chang - khong co chang nang canh):
85 * GY-33 ket luan MAU KHAC du XAC_NHAN_DO lan + on dinh TRE_ON_DINH_DO_MS
86 * -> [G_NANG] goc cho = 90 = dung home nen DI QUA NGAY (khong di)
87 * -> [G_CHO_TROI] TRE_BAO_DO_MS = 0 nen cung di qua ngay
88 * -> [G_DANG_GAT] servo truot 90 -> 0 do NGAY LAP TUC
89 * -> [G_GIU] dung yen tai 0 do trong GIU_BAO_DO_MS (5s)
90 * -> [G_VE_HOME] servo truot 0 -> 90 do (ve home)
91 * -> [G_KHOA] giong tren
92 *
93 * Servo hoat dong DOC LAP voi bang tai: gat ke ca khi motor dang dung.
94 * ========================================================================== */

96/* ★★★★★ CHE DO DO MAU - THEM 14/08/2026 (mot cong tac DUY NHAT) ★★★★★
97 ==========================================================================
98 Bat len de DO LAI NGUONG MAU sau khi lam man che anh sang. Khi = 1:
99 1) In LIEN TUC so lieu mau ra UART TX (PA9): C | r g b | b-r b-g | ket luan.
100 Cam FT232: PA9 -> RX cua FT232, GND -> GND. Terminal 115200 8N1.
101 ⚠ Ban NoUART khong nhan duoc phim, nen day la kenh MOT CHIEU: chi doc.
102 2) KHOA CO CAU GAT (g_tu_dong = 0): servo nam yen o home 90 do trong suot
103 qua trinh do. Khong gat oan vao tay, va log khong bi cac dong cua may
104 trang thai gat chen ngang.
105 Bang tai / nut START-STOP / LED 7 doan VAN chay binh thuong - do duoc ca
106 luc bang tai dang chay.
107 
108 ★★ SUA 14/08/2026 (lan 3): TACH LAM HAI CONG TAC DOC LAP.
109 Ban dau mot cong tac lam ca hai viec, nen muon chay thu servo la mat luon
110 log - dung luc can nhat, vi phai NHIN SO trong khi motor chay moi biet
111 nhieu co lam d_br nhay hay khong. Nay tach ra:
112 CHE_DO_DO_MAU - in so lieu (khong anh huong servo)
113 CHE_DO_KHOA_SERVO - khoa co cau gat (khong anh huong viec in)
114 BA GIAI DOAN dung nhu sau:
115 do nguong : DO_MAU=1 KHOA_SERVO=1
116 chay thu co so : DO_MAU=1 KHOA_SERVO=0
117 chay that : DO_MAU=0 KHOA_SERVO=0 <== buoc dang toi
118 
119 ★★ 14/08/2026 (lan 2): DA TAT (1 -> 0). KHONG phai vi da do xong nguong,
120 ma vi CHE_DO_BANG_LOG thay the: bang log moi in DU CA so lieu mau lan
121 encoder tren cung mot dong, cot thang hang de quay video. De ca hai cung
122 bat thi hai luong in chen nhau, cot le het.
123 ⚠ Muon quay lai che do do nguong mau (5 dong/giay, nhieu chi tiet hon,
124 co ca kenh tho): dat lai DO_MAU = 1 VA dat CHE_DO_BANG_LOG = 0. */
125#define CHE_DO_DO_MAU 0u
126 
127/* ⚠⚠ DAT LAI VE 0 KHI CHAY THAT, neu khong servo se KHONG BAO GIO gat.
128 Trieu chung neu quen: cam bien nhan dung mau, log day du, den PC13 nhay
129 deu, nhung servo im hoan toan.
130 ★ 14/08/2026: DA MO KHOA (1 -> 0). Trieu chung tren da xay ra that - mau
131 nhan dung het nhung servo im, vi co nay con dang de 1 tu buoc do nguong.
132 g_tu_dong = 0 lam gat_task() thoat ngay dong dau, khong bao gio chay toi
133 may trang thai. KHONG phai loi nguong mau. */
134#define CHE_DO_KHOA_SERVO 0u

136/* Gian cach in mot dong so lieu o che do do (ms). O gain/ATIME mac dinh mot

146/* ★ SO LAN THU TU DO NEN luc khoi dong, va gian cach giua hai lan (ms).

148 xau nhat = 5 x 2s = 10s, chi xay ra khi that su co van de - luc do dung
149 10 giay de nguoi van hanh bo vat ra la rat dang. */

153/* ★★★★★ HOC LAI NEN SAU KHI MOTOR DA CHAY - THEM 14/08/2026 (lan 5) ★★★★★
154 ==========================================================================
155 LOI CO HE THONG, DA DO DUOC BANG SO qua ba lan khoi dong khac nhau:
156 nen luc motor DUNG (do 14/08) : b-r = +28
157 nen luc motor CHAY (do 14/08) : b-r = +32
158 moc hoc duoc luc khoi dong (log sau) : b-r = +34
159 TCS_HocSanToi() chi chay o cuoi main() truoc vong lap, ma luc do motor
160 LUON DUNG (MOTOR_TAT o dau main() la yeu cau an toan, khong doi duoc).
161 Nghia la moc nen luon duoc hoc o MOT dieu kien chieu sang, roi he lam viec
162 o mot dieu kien KHAC. Sai so ay nam lai vinh vien trong moi nguong, vi ca
163 nguong xanh lan vung nen deu suy ra tu moc.
164 
165 CACH CHUA: hoc LAI mot lan nua, ngay sau khi motor da chay va bang tai
166 thuc su trong. Luc do dieu kien chieu sang dung bang luc lam viec.
167 
168 ⚠ BA CHOT AN TOAN de khong bao gio hoc nham mot cai PHOI thanh nen:
169 1) Chi dem nhung lan doc DA DUOC KET LUAN LA TRONG. Ma "TRONG" nghia la
170 van mau da nam trong vung nen hien tai, nen phep hoc lai chi co the
171 TINH CHINH trong vung do (+-TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR), TUYET DOI khong the
172 nhay sang ngoi len phoi. Phoi do (+16..+24) va phoi xanh (+39 tro
173 len) deu KHONG duoc ket luan TRONG nen bi loai tu dau.
174 2) Phai DUNG YEN: hai lan doc lien tiep lech qua 2 don vi la dem lai tu
175 dau. Phoi dang troi qua khong bao gio dat duoc.
176 3) CHI MOT LAN moi lan cap nguon. Khong co chuyen moc bi hoc di hoc lai
177 troi dan theo thoi gian - viec bam theo cham da co TCS_NEN_BAM lo. */
178#define HOC_LAI_SO_LAN 15u /* ~2.3s bang tai trong, on dinh */
179 
180/* Loc nhieu IR E18-D80NK: lay mau moi 10ms, can 3 mau giong nhau lien tiep
181 (30ms) moi doi trang thai - khong tin 1 canh don le. */
182#define IR_MAU_MS 10u
183/* ★ 3 -> 6 mau (60ms). LY DO: motor DC 24V dong ngat qua relay phat xung
184 nhieu manh; day tin hieu IR chay song song day dong luc rat de bi kich
185 gia. Moi lan kich gia la mot chu trinh phan loai chay oan => servo gat
186 lung tung ("giat lien tuc"). Vat that che tia IR lau hon 60ms rat nhieu
187 nen tang nguong nay khong lam bo sot phoi. */
188#define IR_SO_MAU 6u

192/* ★ GOC NGHI = GOC SERVO VE NGAY KHI CAP NGUON.
193 Servo_Init nap do rong xung TRUOC khi bat PWM nen xung dau tien phat ra
194 da dung goc nay - servo di thang ve day, khong quat sang cho khac.
195 LUU Y: GOC_NGHI_DO va GOC_GAT_DO PHAI KHAC NHAU.
196 
197 ★★★ SUA 14/08/2026: 180 -> 90 (yeu cau nguoi dung: home la 90 do).
198 HE QUA DA TINH DEN, doc truoc khi go loi:
199 1) Xung home gio la 1500us (giua dai 1000-2000us) thay vi 2000us. Day la
200 diem GIUA tam co khi cua MG996R nen cang AN TOAN hon ban cu - het han
201 moi rui ro "ep chan co" da ghi o servo_mg996r.h.
202 2) ⚠ CANH GAT PHAI DUOC LAP LAI TREN TRUC: truoc day canh nghi o 180 do,
203 nay nghi o 90 do. Neu giu nguyen vi tri lap cu thi canh se nghi o
204 giua duong bang tai va CHAN PHOI. Thao canh, dua servo ve home (xung
205 1500us) roi lap lai canh dung vi tri "khong chan phoi".
206 MG996R 25 rang => 14.4 do/rang, khong lech duoc dung 90 do bang cach
207 lech rang - phai lap lai cho tu te.
208 3) Hanh trinh moi chang gio chi con 90 do (500us) thay vi 180 do, tru
209 chang gat 180->0 cua phoi xanh van la toan dai. Thoi gian truot tu
210 tinh lai o bang duoi phan TOC DO SERVO. */
211#define GOC_NGHI_DO 90u /* goc servo luc nghi (home) - sua 14/08/2026 */
212 
213/* ★★★ THEM 14/08/2026: GOC CHO cua chu trinh XANH ("nang canh len").
214 Khi GY-33 xac nhan phoi XANH, servo KHONG dung yen o home nua ma truot
215 len 180 do TRUOC, roi moi dung o do dem TRE_GAT_XANH_MS cho phoi troi
216 toi, sau do gat mot mach 180 -> 0 do.
217 Y NGHIA VAT LY: nang canh gat len khoi mat bang tai de phoi xanh troi vao
218 dung tam, roi quet TOAN DAI 180 do de hat phoi ra - luc gat co ca quang
219 duong lay da chu khong chi 90 do.
220 ⚠ Chu trinh DO KHONG dung goc nay (goc cho cua DO = home, xem gat_task). */
221#define GOC_CHO_XANH_DO 180u /* goc servo cho phoi xanh troi toi */
222/* ★ SUA 03/08/2026: 90 -> 0. Hanh trinh gat gio la TOAN DAI 180 do.
223 He qua da tinh den:
224 - Xung phai di 1000us (thay vi 500us). Voi SERVO_BUOC_US=20us moi
225 SERVO_CHU_KY_MS=10ms => ~500ms moi lan truot, thay vi ~250ms.
226 Khong can sua timing: may trang thai CHO Servo_DenNoi(), khong doan
227 thoi gian, va TIMEOUT_GAT_MS (15s) con thua rat nhieu.
228 - Mot chu ky gat tron ven dai them ~500ms (di + ve). Neu phoi di sat
229 nhau thi tang khoang cach giua chung.
230 - Quet toan dai keo dong dinh cao hon o MG996R - nguon servo phai
231 du khoe, neu thay MCU reset luc gat thi do sut ap, khong phai code. */
232#define GOC_GAT_DO 0u /* goc servo luc gat */
233 
234/* ★★★ CHAN O KHAU BIEN DICH - THEM 31/07/2026.
235 ⚠ LOI DA GAP THAT: co luc GOC_NGHI_DO bi dat = 190. Servo_DatGoc() KEP
236 moi gia tri > 180 ve 180, nen servo van chay toi dung 2000us y het nhu
237 180 - tuc la SUA XONG KHONG CO TAC DUNG GI. Nhung cac dong thong bao lai
238 in thang macro ra nen terminal bao "truot 190 do -> 90 do", lam nguoi
239 van hanh tuong da doi duoc goc. Mat thoi gian truy oan.
240 Tu nay sai nguong se KHONG BIEN DICH DUOC, khong con am tham nua.
241 ⚠ Muon canh gat ra XA HON 2000us thi KHONG duoc nang so nay - phai:
242 (1) do lai chan co that cua servo roi nang SERVO_XUNG_MAX_US, HOAC
243 (2) lech mot rang khop tren truc servo (MG996R 25 rang = 14.4 do/rang). */
244#if (GOC_NGHI_DO > 180u) || (GOC_GAT_DO > 180u)
245#error "GOC_NGHI_DO / GOC_GAT_DO phai <= 180 - Servo_DatGoc() se kep am tham"
246#endif
247#if (GOC_NGHI_DO == GOC_GAT_DO)
248#error "GOC_NGHI_DO va GOC_GAT_DO PHAI KHAC NHAU - canh gat se khong di chuyen"
249#endif
250/* ★ Chan cung cho goc CHO cua chu trinh XANH (them 14/08/2026) */
251#if (GOC_CHO_XANH_DO > 180u)
252#error "GOC_CHO_XANH_DO phai <= 180 - Servo_DatGoc() se kep am tham"
253#endif
254#if (GOC_CHO_XANH_DO == GOC_GAT_DO)
255#error "GOC_CHO_XANH_DO trung GOC_GAT_DO - chang gat XANH se khong di chuyen"
256#endif
257 
258/* TRE VAN CHUYEN: tu luc XAC NHAN xanh duong den luc bat dau truot ra goc
259 gat. Trong suot khoang nay servo NAM YEN o GOC_NGHI_DO.
260 Y NGHIA VAT LY: thoi gian phoi troi tu tam nhin cam bien mau toi canh gat.
261 Chinh so nay khi thay canh gat danh HUT phia sau phoi (tang) hoac danh
262 TRUOC mat phoi (giam). */
263/* ★ LICH SU CHINH 03/08/2026: 2000 -> 1500 -> 2000 (tra lai 2s).
264 Do la TRE tu luc XAC NHAN xanh den luc BAT DAU truot, KHONG bao gom
265 thoi gian truot (~500ms cho hanh trinh 180->0 do). Tong tu luc nhan
266 tin hieu den luc canh gat toi noi la ~2.5s.
267 Neu canh gat danh TRUOC mat phoi thi tang; danh HUT phia sau thi giam. */
268/* ★★ SUA 14/08/2026 - MOC DEM DA DOI, DOC KY TRUOC KHI CHINH SO NAY.
269 Nguoi dung da chot: 2 giay duoc dem TU LUC SERVO TOI 180 DO, khong phai
270 tu luc xac nhan mau. Nghia la tong thoi gian tu luc thay phoi xanh den
271 luc BAT DAU gat gio la:
272 ~400ms (truot 90 -> 180 do o BUOC_XANH_NANG = 25us/khung)
273 + 2000ms (dung yen tai 180 do)
274 = ~2400ms, roi cong ~800ms nua cho chang gat 180 -> 0 do
275 => canh gat cham phoi vao khoang ~3.2s sau khi cam bien ket luan xanh,
276 CHAM HON ban cu ~400ms.
277 ⚠ VI VAY neu chay thu thay canh gat danh HUT phia sau phoi (phoi da troi
278 qua roi canh moi toi), cho sua DAU TIEN la HA con so nay xuong ~1550,
279 KHONG phai tang toc do servo. */
280#define TRE_GAT_XANH_MS 2000u
281 
282/* Giu yen tai GOC_GAT_DO bao lau roi moi tra ve home (ms).
283 Nghi o day de servo DUNG HAN roi moi doi chieu - doi chieu ngay luc con
284 quan tinh chinh la cai lam servo GIAT va rut dong dot bien. */
285#define GIU_XANH_MS 1000u
286 
287/* ==================== CHU TRINH BAO PHOI DO (them 03/08/2026) =============
288 Yeu cau (cap nhat 14/08/2026): thay phoi DO thi servo tu HOME 90 do ve
289 GOC_BAO_DO = 0 do NGAY LAP TUC, giu 5 giay roi ve home 90 do.
290 Chu trinh DO KHONG di qua 180 do - chi chu trinh XANH moi nang canh len.
291 
292 "DO" o day lay bang TCS_MAU_KHAC, tuc MOI vat khong phai xanh duong.
293 Da xac nhan voi nguoi dung: day chuyen chi co hai loai phoi do/xanh nen
294 khong can them lop nhan dien DO rieng. ⚠ He qua: phoi trang / xanh la /
295 vat la roi vao bang tai cung se kich chu trinh nay.
296 
297 ⚠⚠ LICH SU GOC: 90 -> 0 (doi ngay 03/08/2026 theo yeu cau).
298 Luc dat 90 do, nguoi dung da xac nhan goc do CHI LA BAO HIEU, canh gat
299 KHONG cham vao phoi - vi vay moi dat TRE_BAO_DO_MS = 0 (phoi khong phai
300 troi di dau ca).
301 NAY GOC_BAO_DO = 0 = DUNG BANG GOC_GAT_DO cua phoi XANH, tuc la VI TRI
302 GAT THAT SU. Sau ban 14/08/2026, hai chu trinh khac nhau o CA DUONG DI
303 LAN THOI GIAN:
304 XANH: 90 -> 180 (nang canh) -> cho 2000ms -> gat ve 0 -> giu 1000ms
305 -> ve 90
306 DO : 90 -> 0 NGAY -> giu 5000ms -> ve 90
307 HE QUA PHAI LUONG TRUOC: canh gat ra vi tri gat ngay khi phoi DO con o
308 cho cam bien, roi NAM YEN o do 5 giay. Phoi do mat khoang 2 giay de troi
309 toi canh gat (do chinh la y nghia cua TRE_GAT_XANH_MS), luc do canh van
310 dang o 0 do => PHOI DO RAT CO THE BI GAT RA LUON.
311 Neu chay thu thay dung nhu vay ma KHONG muon the, co hai cach:
312 (1) Dat TRE_BAO_DO_MS = 0 nhung ha GIU_BAO_DO_MS xuong duoi thoi gian
313 phoi troi toi canh gat (< ~1500ms), de canh ve home truoc khi phoi
314 toi noi; HOAC
315 (2) Tra GOC_BAO_DO ve 90 (hoac mot goc khac khong chan duong phoi). */
316#define GOC_BAO_DO 0u /* goc servo khi bao phoi DO (cu: 90) */
317#define TRE_BAO_DO_MS 0u /* 0 = truot ngay, khong cho phoi troi */
318#define GIU_BAO_DO_MS 5000u /* giu tai GOC_BAO_DO 5 giay roi ve home */
319 
320#if (GOC_BAO_DO > 180u)
321#error "GOC_BAO_DO phai <= 180 - Servo_DatGoc() se kep am tham"
322#endif
323#if (GOC_BAO_DO == GOC_NGHI_DO)
324#error "GOC_BAO_DO trung GOC_NGHI_DO - canh gat se khong di chuyen"
325#endif
326 
327/* So lan doc MAU KHAC LIEN TIEP moi kich chu trinh bao do.
328 ⚠⚠ RUI RO DA BIET voi con so 2 - doc truoc khi go loi "phoi xanh bi bo
329 sot": luc phoi XANH vua troi VAO tam nhin, cam bien thay MOT PHAN phoi va
330 MOT PHAN nen => van mau nam giua nen (b-r=+8) va xanh (b-r=+34), rat co
331 the ra MAU KHAC vai lan doc lien tiep TRUOC khi du dieu kien xanh.
332 Neu hai lan doc chuyen tiep do du kich chu trinh bao do thi:
333 - servo ve 90 do va bi khoa 5 giay
334 - phoi XANH that di qua trong luc khoa => BI BO SOT hoan toan
335 Nguoi dung da chon 2 (03/08/2026). Neu gap trieu chung tren thi NANG con
336 so nay len 3 hoac 4 - do la cho sua DAU TIEN, khong phai dong o dau khac.
337 Chu trinh do khong cham vao phoi nen nang len khong mat gi.
338 
339 ★ SUA 04/08/2026: 2 -> 1 theo yeu cau "quet RED mot lan la du".
340 ⚠ LUU Y THAT: doi so nay KHONG lam do nhanh len chut nao. Dieu kien kich do
341 la HAI VE AND: so lan doc VA thoi gian on dinh (TRE_ON_DINH_DO_MS ngay
342 duoi). Voi chu ky doc ~20-25ms thi 2 lan doc chi ton ~45ms, trong khi cua
343 so on dinh la 250ms - ve thu hai chan truoc tu lau. Muon do nhanh hon thi
344 phai ha TRE_ON_DINH_DO_MS chu khong phai so nay.
345 Ha ve 1 van an toan vi tinh LIEN TUC da duoc bao dam o cho khac: chuoi bi
346 dut mot lan la g_do_lien_tiep ve 0 va dong ho tinh lai tu dau. */
347#define XAC_NHAN_DO 1u
348 
349/* ★★★★ SUA 04/08/2026 - LOI THAT: "phoi XANH cung chay chu trinh DO" ★★★★
350 ==========================================================================
351 TRIEU CHUNG: bat ke phoi xanh hay do, servo deu ra 0 do roi 5 giay sau moi
352 ve home - tuc la LUON chay chu trinh DO, chu trinh XANH khong bao gio toi
353 luot.
354 
355 NGUYEN NHAN - mot cuoc dua khong can suc trong G_SAN_SANG:
356 XANH: can 2 lan doc XANH_DUONG lien tiep, roi CHO 2000ms moi truot
357 DO : can 2 lan doc MAU_KHAC lien tiep, truot NGAY (tre = 0)
358 Luc phoi XANH vua troi VAO tam nhin, cam bien thay mot phan phoi + mot
359 phan nen => van mau nam GIUA nen (b-r=-10) va xanh (b-r=+34) => ra
360 MAU_KHAC vai lan doc. Chi can 2 lan la DO kich ngay, servo ra 0 do va
361 khoa 5 giay; phoi xanh that di qua trong luc khoa => khong bao gio duoc
362 xet. Cuoc dua nay DO luon thang vi no khong phai cho gi ca.
363 
364 CACH CHUA: bat DO phai thay MAU_KHAC LIEN TUC du mot KHOANG THOI GIAN
365 truoc khi duoc kich. Trong khoang do, nhanh XANH van duoc xet TRUOC moi
366 vong lap nen phoi xanh kip thang. Chuyen tiep mep phoi chi keo dai vai
367 chuc ms nen bi loai het.
368 
369 ⚠ DUNG THOI GIAN THAT chu KHONG dem so lan doc: toc do vong lap thay doi
370 theo tinh huong (ban NoUART chay nhanh hon han ban co UART vi trinh bien
371 dich da xoa toan bo khoi xu ly lenh), nen dem "so lan doc" khong con la
372 thuoc do on dinh nua.
373 ⚠ DANH DOI: phoi DO nam trong tam nhin ngan hon so nay se bi bo qua. Bang
374 tai hien tai cho phoi qua tam nhin khoang 0.5-1s nen 400ms con an toan;
375 neu chay bang tai NHANH HON nhieu thi phai ha so nay xuong. */
376/* ★ SUA 04/08/2026: 400 -> 250 theo yeu cau "do dang cham qua".
377 DAY MOI LA CON SO QUYET DINH TOC DO PHAN UNG CUA DO (khong phai
378 XAC_NHAN_DO). Do gio kich o moc 250ms thay vi 400ms.
379 ⚠⚠ DAY LA NOI DOI RUI RO CO Y: 250ms van gap ~10 lan chu ky doc nen loai
380 duoc nhieu don le, NHUNG neu chuyen tiep mep phoi XANH keo dai hon 250ms
381 thi loi "phoi xanh chay chu trinh do" SE QUAY LAI.
382 DAU HIEU: phoi xanh ra log "PHOI DO" va servo giu 5 giay thay vi 1 giay.
383 PHUONG AN DA CHOT NEU TAI PHAT (04/08/2026): KHONG nang lai 400, ma cho
384 XANH CAT NGANG chu trinh DO dang chay - kha thi vi ca hai chu trinh cung
385 ra 0 do nen servo khong phai doi chieu, chi doi thoi gian giu (5s -> 1s).
386 Luc do phai thiet ke lai thoi diem canh gat cham phoi, can so lieu chay
387 thu that moi lam dung duoc. */
388#define TRE_ON_DINH_DO_MS 250u
389 
390/* ==================== TOC DO SERVO THEO TUNG CHANG (04/08/2026) ===========
391 So us do rong xung doi moi khung PWM 20ms. Ca dai 180 do = 1000us, nen:
392 25us -> 40 khung -> ~800ms (EM, da chay thu xac nhan het giat)
393 40us -> 25 khung -> ~500ms (NHANH, co giat nhe khi phai keo nguoc tai)
394 
395 VI SAO PHAI CHIA THEO CHANG: chay 40us thi servo bi lenh 360 do/giay, sat
396 kha nang thuc cua MG996R o 6V (~430 do/giay khong tai) - het bien ngay khi
397 co tai canh gat, nen chieu phai KEO NGUOC (0 -> 180, ve home) bi giat.
398 Chieu di gat (180 -> 0) duoc trong luc/quan tinh giup nen van bam kip.
399 
400 BANG TOC DO (nguoi dung chot 04/08/2026):
401 XANH di gat : EM - cu g?t nh? nhang, khong giat
402 XANH ve home : EM
403 DO di 0 do : NHANH - bao hieu nen can dut khoat
404 DO ve home : EM - do da giu 5 giay roi, ve tu tu khong sao
405 
406 ⚠ CHANG "XANH DI GAT" GIO MAT 800ms THAY VI 500ms, tuc canh gat toi vi tri
407 gat CHAM HON 300ms. TRE_GAT_XANH_MS van de nguyen 2000 (con so nay do nguoi
408 dung tu chot, khong tu y doi). Neu chay thu thay canh danh HUT phia sau
409 phoi xanh thi HA TRE_GAT_XANH_MS xuong ~1700 la bu lai dung. */
410#define BUOC_EM 25u /* ~800ms ca dai 180 do */
411#define BUOC_NHANH 40u /* ~500ms ca dai 180 do */
412 
413/* ★★ THEM 14/08/2026: BUOC_XANH_NANG - toc do chang MOI 90 -> 180 do.
414 Nguoi dung chot chay EM. LY DO KY THUAT (khong phai chon bua): day dung la
415 chieu PHAI KEO NGUOC TAI (nang canh gat len), tuc chieu da tung lo ra loi
416 "giat nhe" khi chay 40us - xem khoi giai thich o SERVO_BUOC_US trong
417 servo_mg996r.h. Chang nay chi 90 do (500us) nen o 25us/khung mat ~400ms.
418 
419 BANG THOI GIAN THUC TE SAU KHI HOME DOI VE 90 DO:
420 XANH nang 90 -> 180 : 500us / 25us = 20 khung = ~400ms
421 XANH gat 180 -> 0 : 1000us / 25us = 40 khung = ~800ms (toan dai)
422 XANH ve 0 -> 90 : 500us / 25us = 20 khung = ~400ms
423 DO di 90 -> 0 : 500us / 40us = 13 khung = ~260ms (nhanh)
424 DO ve 0 -> 90 : 500us / 25us = 20 khung = ~400ms
425 ⚠ Chang DO di gio chi con 260ms (truoc la ~500ms cho 180->0) - bao hieu
426 dut khoat hon han, dung y do ban dau cua BUOC_DO_DI. */
427#define BUOC_XANH_NANG BUOC_EM
428#define BUOC_XANH_DI BUOC_EM
429#define BUOC_XANH_VE BUOC_EM
430#define BUOC_DO_DI BUOC_NHANH
431#define BUOC_DO_VE BUOC_EM

433/* ★★ SO LAN DOC XANH DUONG LIEN TIEP moi duoc coi la CHAC va cho gat.
434 Moi lan doc ton mot chu ky tich phan (154ms) cong ~20ms cua so phuc vu,
435 nen 2 lan doi hoi phoi nam trong tam nhin cam bien khoang 350ms LIEN TUC.
436 Muc dich: chan mot lan doc don le bi sai do nhieu tu motor 24V danh vao

442 2 = <== DANG DUNG (sua 03/08/2026: ha tu 3 xuong 2)

448 ung cham han: phoi phai nam trong tam nhin du 3 chu ky LIEN TIEP, bang tai
449 chay thi kho dat va de bo sot. Cac moc "~175ms / ~350ms / ~520ms" ghi o
450 ban goc tinh theo ATIME = 0xC0 (154ms/lan doc) - KHONG con dung voi ATIME

454#define XAC_NHAN_XANH 2u

456/* ★★★ BIEN AN TOAN TANG UNG DUNG - SUA LOI "SERVO GAT OAN KHI CHUA CO PHOI".

461 NGUYEN NHAN GOC (do duoc tu chinh rig):
462 - Nen do duoc C=7856, VUOT TCS_NGUONG_NEN_TOI(2000) => driver ket luan
463 "NEN SANG" => san toi ha ve muc chan nhieu (982). Buoc "co vat hay
464 khong" cua driver TU DO LUON TRA VE CO VAT. Khong con mot bang chung

466 - Chot chan d_bg >= -20 KHONG chan duoc gi: nen cua rig nay da o -15,
467 tuc la da lot san qua dieu kien do.

469 cuc mong: nen = +31 | nguong xanh = +36 => CHI 5 DON VI.
470 Nhieu 5 don vi tren d_br la ra XANH GIA -> servo gat oan.
471

474 ra ngay.

476 CACH CHUA - BA LOP DOC LAP (khong lop nao dung tin hieu E18):
477 Lop 1: bien an toan RIENG cua ung dung, CHAT HON nguong cua driver.
478 Phai co d_br >= nen + BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN moi tinh la xanh "chac".
479 Tu dong bam theo nen (ke ca khi nen tu bam doi moc).

482 khong con kich duoc.
483 Lop 3: canh len (edge) - truoc do phai thay bang tai TRONG on dinh.
484 Nen dung yen mai mai se khong bao gio tu kich duoc.

486 ⚠ CHINH SO NAY THE NAO:
487 Do that tren rig: nen +31, phoi xanh that ~+40 => tong khoang cach 9 don vi.
488 Dat 7 la nam gan phia phoi, uu tien CHONG GAT OAN.
489 - Van con gat oan -> TANG (8, 9). Toi da la (xanh_that - nen).
490 - Bo sot phoi that -> GIAM (6, 5). Toi thieu la TCS_BIEN_TREN_NEN (5).
491 Go lenh 'w' de DO chinh xac dai nhieu cua nen roi hay chinh con so nay. */
492/* ★★★ SUA 01/08/2026: 7 -> 12. LAM THEO SO DO, khong phai phong doan.
493 Chinh lenh 'w' tren rig da tinh ra: "Phai TANG BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN len it
494 nhat 11 (dang 7)" vi nen do duoc cham nguong co bien 30/30 lan.

503 BIEN (b-r tut ve ~+8), phai chinh CO KHI dua cam bien lai gan cho phoi
504 di qua o vung C = 12000..18000 - DUNG ha con so nay xuong. */
505/* ★★★★ RESET 14/08/2026: 12 -> 7 (tra ve gia tri THIET KE GOC).
506 So 12 duoc tinh tu phien do 01/08/2026 tren rig CHUA CO MAN CHE ("nen do

508 dung cai nguon gay troi nen ma con so 12 sinh ra de chong - nen giu 12

515 PHOI DO : b-r = -7

548#define BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN 6

561/* Relay kich muc CAO (jumper High Level Trigger tren module) */

563#define MOTOR_TAT() HAL_GPIO_WritePin(RELAY_MOTOR_GPIO_Port, RELAY_MOTOR_Pin, GPIO_PIN_RESET)
564 
565/* Nut nhan: POLLING + loc mau, KHONG dung EXTI.
566 Ly do: moi truong co motor DC 24V, xung nhieu tu motor tung kich nham EXTI
567 lam motor tu tat - loi da gap that o khoi Motor_Start_Stop. */
568#define BTN_MAU_MS 10u /* lay mau moi 10ms */
569/* ★ 5 -> 10 -> 20 mau (200ms). DA XAC NHAN THUC TE: voi 10 mau (100ms) nhieu
570 tu motor VAN kich duoc nham nut STOP - nghia la no giu chan o muc CAO
571 LIEN TUC hon 100ms, khong phai xung nhon. Nguoi bam nut binh thuong giu
572 200-300ms nen nguong nay van bat duoc moi lan bam that.

574 BAT BUOC phai sua phan cung (tro keo xuong ngoai + tu 104), xem lenh 'j'

576#define BTN_SO_MAU 20u
577#define BTN_KHOA_MS 1000u /* khoa 1s giua 2 lenh - bao ve tiep diem */
578 
579/* ★★ CUA SO CAM LUC MOI CAP NGUON.

581 moi dieu khien duoc. Nguyen nhan: module nut 3 chan co nguon rieng, luc
582 moi co dien ngo ra cua no vong len muc CAO vai chuc ms truoc khi on dinh.
583 Bo loc 5 mau (50ms) khong du de loai, nen firmware nhan nham la co nguoi
584 bam START.
585 Hai lop chan:
586 1) Trong BTN_KHOI_DONG_MS dau tien sau khi cap nguon: KHONG nhan lenh nao.
587 2) Sau do van phai THAY NUT O MUC THAP it nhat mot lan roi moi cong nhan
588 lan bam dau tien (co giu_* khoi tao = 1). Nut dang bi giu / dang chap

590#define BTN_KHOI_DONG_MS 1500u

592/* Encoder JGB37-545: hop so 1:44, encoder 11 xung/vong truc dong co,
593 TIM4 dem 4 canh (TI12) => 11 x 4 x 44 = 1936 xung / 1 vong truc ra. */
594#define ENCODER_GEAR 44u
595#define ENCODER_PPR 11u
596#define ENCODER_X4 4u
597#define XUNG_MOI_VONG (ENCODER_PPR * ENCODER_X4 * ENCODER_GEAR)
598#define CUA_SO_RPM_MS 100u /* cua so do toc do */

600/* ★★★★★ KHOI DOC ENCODER KHI HOAT DONG - THEM 14/08/2026 ★★★★★
601 ==========================================================================
602 LY DO PHAI THEM: TIM4 va encoder_quet() DA CO SAN va van chay dung, nhung
603 cho DUY NHAT in gia tri ra la lenh 'e' go qua UART. Ban NoUART tat han
604 duong nhan (uart_doc_lenh() luon tra 0) nen trinh bien dich XOA CA khoi
605 switch(c) => so lieu encoder duoc tinh moi 20ms roi vut di, khong ai nhin
606 thay. Khoi nay mo lai duong ra bang cach IN DINH KY, khong can go phim.
607 
608 BON DAI LUONG in ra:
609 xung - so xung tich luy (dem 4 canh), co dau theo chieu quay
610 vong - so vong TRUC RA (da qua hop so 1:44)
611 rpm - toc do truc ra, do theo cua so thoi gian THUC
612 mm/s - toc do dai cua mat bang tai + quang duong da chay

614 ⚠ KHOI NAY CHI DOC VA IN. No khong ghi vao mot bien trang thai nao cua
615 khoi gat / khoi mau / khoi hien thi => KHONG THE gay hoi quy. Nghi no gay
616 loi thi dat CHE_DO_BANG_LOG = 0, dich lai, loi con nghia la cho khac.
617 
618 ★★ NANG CAP 14/08/2026 (lan 2) - GOP THANH MOT BANG LOG DUY NHAT.
619 ==========================================================================
620 Muc dich: QUAY MAN HINH lam video bao ve. Mot dong = mot khoanh khac cua
621 ca he thong, cac cot thang hang, cu DASH_SO_DONG_TD dong in lai tieu de:
622 
623 t(s) | encoder (xung/vong/rpm/mm-s) | cam bien mau | servo | dem | motor
624 
625 ⚠⚠ DA TAT CHE_DO_DO_MAU (1 -> 0) KEM THEO. Ly do BAT BUOC: hai nguon in
626 chay song song thi dong cua chung xen ke nhau, cot le tung tung, quay
627 video khong dung duoc. Toan bo so lieu mau da co mat trong bang nay roi.
628 Muon quay lai che do do nguong mau chi tiet thi dao nguoc: DO_MAU = 1 va
629 BANG_LOG = 0.
630 Cac dong SU KIEN (PHOI XANH, nut START/STOP, canh bao) VAN GIU - chung
631 thua thot va chinh la cai lam video de hieu. */
632#define CHE_DO_BANG_LOG 1u
633 
634/* Gian cach in mot dong bang log (ms).
635 500ms = 2 dong/giay: du nhanh de thay phoi troi qua tam nhin (~0.5-1s) va
636 thay so dem nhay, nhung nguoi xem video van kip doc tung dong.
637 Mot phep do mau ton ~154ms nen 500ms la ~3 phep do/dong - so lieu tuoi.
638 Nhanh hon: 200. Cham hon (de doc hon nhung de LOT phoi): 1000. */
639#define DASH_CHU_KY_MS 500u
640 
641/* Cu bao nhieu dong thi in lai dong tieu de cot. 20 dong x 500ms = 10 giay
642 moi lan - vua du de nguoi xem video tua toi doan nao cung thay ten cot. */
643#define DASH_SO_DONG_TD 20u
644 
645/* ⚠⚠ SO NAY PHAI DO THAT ROI SUA - hien dang la GIA DINH.
646 Duong kinh PULY KEO (con lan chu dan mat bang), tinh bang mm, do phan
647 TIEP XUC voi day bang tai. Sai so nay chi lam sai cot mm/s va mm; cot
648 xung / vong / rpm KHONG phu thuoc no nen van dung tuyet doi. */
649#define ENC_DUONG_KINH_MM 30u
650 
651/* Chu vi x10 (0.1mm). 3142 = pi x1000, chia 100 de ra don vi 0.1mm.
652 D=30mm -> 942 (= 94.2mm moi vong truc ra). */
653#define ENC_CHU_VI_X10 ((3142u * ENC_DUONG_KINH_MM) / 100u)
654 
655/* He so doi (xung / ms) -> rpm x10, LAM TRON thay vi cat cut:
656 rpm_x10 = xung * 600000 / (dt_ms * XUNG_MOI_VONG)
657 = xung * ENC_HS_RPMX10 / dt_ms
658 Voi 1936 xung/vong -> 310. Sai so 0.03%. */
659#define ENC_HS_RPMX10 ((600000u + (XUNG_MOI_VONG / 2u)) / XUNG_MOI_VONG)
660 
661/* Motor DANG CHAY (relay dong) ma bo dem KHONG NHUC MOT XUNG trong khoang
662 nay => bao dong. 1.5s: dai hon moi qua do khoi dong cua JGB37 (~200ms)
663 nhung van bat duoc su co gan nhu tuc thi. */
664#define ENC_KET_CANH_BAO_MS 1500u
665#define ENC_BAO_KET_LAP_MS 5000u /* gian cach in lai canh bao ket */
666 
667/* Chong ket co cau chap hanh: mot buoc CO CHUYEN DONG treo qua lau (servo
668 khong bao "den noi" vi ket co khi / mat nguon servo) thi danh ve home va
669 vu trang lai, khong de may trang thai chet cung. */
670#define TIMEOUT_GAT_MS 15000u
671 
672/* ★★★ SO LAN THU KET NOI CAM BIEN MAU LUC KHOI DONG.
673 ⚠⚠ SUA 31/07/2026 - LOI DA GAP: "LED PC13 nhay rat nhanh lien tuc, servo
674 dung im khong ve home, nut START chet".
675 NGUYEN NHAN: ban cu cho cam bien bang mot vong "while (kiem_tra_ket_noi()
676 == 0)" KHONG CO LOI THOAT. Cam bien chua bat tay duoc thi firmware KET
677 VINH VIEN o do va KHONG BAO GIO vao vong lap chinh. Ma gat_task() nam SAU
678 vong nay => toan bo trinh tu phan loai khong chay mot nhip nao, du logic
679 viet dung. Trong lucodo chi con hai phim 'k' va 'p' con tac dung.
680 CACH CHUA: thu dung so lan nay roi VAN VAO vong lap chinh voi co
681 g_cam_bien_ok = 0. Vong lap chinh goi TCS_Doc() moi vong va gy33_quet()
682 da xu ly san truong hop doc that bai, nen cam bien cam lai luc nao la he
683 chay tiep luc do - khong can khoi dong lai board.
684 Nho vay yeu cau van hanh so 1 (cap nguon -> servo ve home, motor DUNG,
685 nut START song) duoc bao dam VO DIEU KIEN, khong phu thuoc cam bien mau.
686 Moi lan thu ton ~2s cong thoi gian kiem tra, 3 lan la du de nguoi dung
687 kip cam lai day ma khong phai cho lau. */
688#define KHOI_DONG_SO_LAN_THU 3u
689 
690/* ★ Gian cach TOI THIEU giua hai lan in canh bao "mat lien lac cam bien".
691 Xem giai thich tai cho dung trong vong lap chinh. 3 giay du de nguoi van
692 hanh biet he dang loi ma khong lam ngap terminal. */
693#define MAT_LIEN_LAC_IN_MS 3000u
694/* ========================================================================== */
695 
696/* ==================== HANG DOI THONG BAO (event queue) ====================
697 ⚠ TAI SAO PHAI CO:
698 Mot phep do mau ton tron mot chu ky tich phan (154ms). Trong suot thoi gian
699 do driver ngoi cho co AVALID va tra quyen dieu khien ve cho phuc_vu_trong_
700 khi_do(). GY-33 gio quet LIEN TUC nen trang thai "dang cho I2C" chiem gan
701 het thoi gian CPU => nut nhan va IR BAT BUOC phai duoc quet ben trong vong
702 cho do, neu khong bo loc 20 mau x 10ms = 200ms cua nut se khong bao gio
703 dem du (thuc te se mat 3-4 giay moi nhan mot lan bam).
704 NHUNG ir_quet()/nut_quet() ban cu co IN THANG ra UART, ma HAL_UART_Transmit
705 chan toi 200ms => goi trong vong cho se lam HET HAN AVALID (472ms) truoc
706 khi cam bien do xong => phep do that bai.
707 CACH GIAI: hai ham do khong in nua, chi DAT CO su kien (thao tac vai chu
708 ky may). Vong lap chinh xa hang doi va in ra khi rong. */
709#define SK_IR_VAT_DEN 0x01u
710#define SK_IR_VAT_DI 0x02u
711#define SK_NUT_START 0x04u
712#define SK_NUT_STOP 0x08u
713 
714/* Cac buoc cua CO CAU GAT (khoi B). Dau vao DUY NHAT: su kien xanh duong. */
715typedef enum
716{
717 G_SAN_SANG = 0, /* da vu trang - cho GY-33 xac nhan XANH DUONG */
718 G_NANG, /* ★14/08: servo truot HOME 90 -> goc cho (XANH: 180) */
719 G_CHO_TROI, /* servo GIU tai goc cho, dem g_tre_ms (phoi troi) */
720 G_DANG_GAT, /* servo dang truot goc cho -> goc gat (0 do) */
721 G_GIU, /* dung yen tai 0 do, dem g_giu_ms */
722 G_VE_HOME, /* servo dang truot 0 -> 90 do (ve home) */
723 G_KHOA /* cho phoi roi khoi tam nhin + het NGHI_SAU_GAT_MS */
724} Gat_t;

757uint8_t g_tu_dong = 1u;

766uint8_t g_goc_cho = GOC_CHO_XANH_DO; /* ★14/08: goc DUNG CHO phoi

774const char *g_ten_ct = "XANH"; /* ten chu trinh - chi de in log */
775uint32_t g_dem_bao_do; /* so lan da chay chu trinh bao do */
776uint8_t g_trong_lien_tiep; /* so lan doc TRONG lien tiep */

783 XAC_NHAN_XANH=2 doi hoi phoi nam trong tam nhin ~350ms lien tuc
784 (2 x 154ms tich phan + 2 x 20ms cua so phuc vu). Bang tai chay nhanh la
785 phoi troi qua truoc khi du so lan => BO SOT PHOI.
786 g_xanh_max : chuoi xanh DAI NHAT tung dat duoc. Neu chay ca buoi ma con
787 so nay khong bao gio toi XAC_NHAN_XANH thi PHAI ha nguong
788 xac nhan xuong (hoac cho bang tai chay cham lai).
789 g_dem_hut : so lan chuoi xanh DUT GIUA CHUNG truoc khi du. Moi lan tang
790 la mot phoi xanh RAT CO THE da bi bo sot. */
791uint8_t g_xanh_max;
792uint32_t g_dem_hut;
793uint32_t g_dem_gat; /* so lan da gat (= so phoi xanh duong) */
794uint32_t g_dem_khac; /* so lan ket luan MAU KHAC (cho di thang) */
795uint32_t g_dem_bo_qua; /* thay xanh nhung dang khoa -> khong gat */
796uint32_t g_moc_ve_home; /* thoi diem servo ve toi home lan gan nhat */

798uint8_t g_mat_lien_lac;/* 1 = dang mat lien lac I2C voi cam bien mau */
799uint32_t g_moc_in_mat; /* moc lan in canh bao mat lien lac gan nhat */

802 volatile vi duoc ghi tu ben trong vong cho I2C (qua ham phuc vu). */
803volatile uint8_t g_su_kien;
804

808uint32_t g_so_lan_stop; /* dem so lan nhan STOP hop le */

814 vi chieu quay cua bang tai co dinh - dau am chi noi len thu tu hai day
815 A/B dang nguoc, khong phai loi. */

817int32_t g_mm_s; /* toc do dai mat bang tai (mm/giay) */

836 
837/* Gui 1 chuoi ra USART1 (PA9 = TX) -> FT232 -> terminal PC (115200 8N1) */
838static void uart_in(const char *s)
839{
840 /* ★★★★★ SUA 14/08/2026 - HAN THOI GIAN PHAI THEO DO DAI CHUOI ★★★★★
841 ==========================================================================
842 LOI DA GAP THAT, VA DA LAM MAT NHIEU LUOT NAP DE TRUY:
843 Han cu la 200ms CO DINH. HAL_UART_Transmit lay moc thoi gian MOT LAN o
844 dau ham roi kiem tra tich luy cho CA CHUOI, nen 200ms la ngan sach cho
845 TOAN BO chuoi chu khong phai cho tung byte.
846 O 115200 8N1 thi 200ms chi phat duoc ~2300 ky tu. Ma in_tro_giup() in
847 1882 ky tu trong MOT lan goi = ~163ms - chi con 37ms du. Chi can mot
848 ngat chen vao (TIM3 cua servo moi 20ms, SysTick moi 1ms) la vuot han va
849 HAM TRA VE GIUA CHUNG, cat cut chuoi.
850 TRIEU CHUNG THAT (log 14/08/2026): banner khoi dong bi mat mot mang, hai
851 chuoi khac nhau dinh vao nhau thanh "LEanh sang la den LED doi qua". Va
852 dong bi nuot dung la dong ">> SERVO DANG BI KHOA" - dong duy nhat noi
853 duoc vi sao servo khong chay. Mat may luot nap chi vi cho nay.
854 
855 NAY: han = 50ms co dinh + 1ms cho moi 8 ky tu.
856 8 ky tu/ms la RONG RAI so voi toc do that 11.5 ky tu/ms o 115200, nen
857 luon du gap ~1.4 lan; 50ms dau la du cho cac chuoi ngan.
858 60 ky tu -> 57ms (can ~5ms)
859 1882 ky tu -> 285ms (can ~163ms)
860 ⚠ Van co han (khong dung HAL_MAX_DELAY) de mot loi phan cung UART khong
861 lam treo vinh vien vong lap chinh. */
862 uint16_t n = (uint16_t)strlen(s);
863 
864 HAL_UART_Transmit(&huart1, (uint8_t *)s, n, (uint32_t)(50u + (n / 8u)));
865}
866 
867/* ★★ QUET CAM BIEN IR E18-D80NK (PA0, active-low) voi loc 60ms.
868 ⚠⚠ HAM NAY CHI CAP NHAT SO LIEU HIEN THI.
869 Ket qua (g_ir_vat, g_ir_so_lan_vat) KHONG duoc phep dieu khien bat cu co
870 cau chap hanh nao. Day la lien dong bat bien cua he - xem giai thich o
871 dau file. Neu ban them tinh nang moi, TUYET DOI khong duoc doc g_ir_vat
872 ben trong gat_task().
873 KHONG IN RA UART o day: ham duoc goi ca tu ben trong vong cho I2C, ma
874 HAL_UART_Transmit chan toi 200ms se lam hong phep do mau. Chi dat co,
875 vong lap chinh se in. */
876static void ir_quet(uint32_t now)
877{
878 static uint32_t tick_mau = 0;
879 static uint8_t dem = 0;
880 uint8_t muc_moi;
881 
882 if ((now - tick_mau) < IR_MAU_MS) { return; }
883 tick_mau = now;
884 
885 /* Open collector + pull-up: khong vat = CAO, co vat = keo xuong THAP */
886 muc_moi = (HAL_GPIO_ReadPin(IR_E18_GPIO_Port, IR_E18_Pin) == GPIO_PIN_RESET) ? 1u : 0u;
887 
888 if (muc_moi == g_ir_vat) { dem = 0; return; } /* khong doi -> thoi */
889 if (++dem < IR_SO_MAU) { return; } /* chua du 6 mau -> cho */
890 dem = 0;
891 g_ir_vat = muc_moi;
892 
893 if (g_ir_vat)
894 {
895 g_ir_so_lan_vat++;
896 g_su_kien |= SK_IR_VAT_DEN;
897 }
898 else
899 {
900 g_su_kien |= SK_IR_VAT_DI;
901 }
902}
903 
904/* ===================== MOTOR BANG TAI + NUT NHAN ========================= */
905 
906/* ★★ QUET 2 NUT START/STOP bang POLLING co loc mau.
907 Quy tac: START chi BAT, STOP chi TAT; nhan ca hai => UU TIEN STOP.
908 ⚠ TAM DIEU KHIEN: hai nut nay CHI dong/cat RELAY MOTOR. Chung KHONG
909 dung/chay khoi gat - servo lam viec doc lap voi bang tai.
910 KHONG IN RA UART o day (goi tu trong vong cho I2C) - chi dat co. */
911static void nut_quet(uint32_t now)
912{
913 static uint32_t tick_mau = 0;
914 static uint32_t tick_khoa = 0;
915 static uint8_t dem_start = 0, dem_stop = 0;
916 /* ★ Khoi tao = 1: PHAI thay nut o muc THAP mot lan roi moi cong nhan lan
917 bam dau tien. Nho vay ngo ra vong cao luc module nut moi co dien khong
918 bi hieu la co nguoi bam START. */
919 static uint8_t giu_start = 1, giu_stop = 1;
920 /* ★ Chot "da qua cua so khoi dong". Xem giai thich ngay duoi. */
921 static uint8_t qua_khoi_dong = 0;
922 uint8_t muc_start, muc_stop;
923 
924 /* ★ Cam tuyet doi trong cua so khoi dong - luc nay moi thu con dang on ap.
925 ⚠ Dung CHOT MOT CHIEU chu KHONG so sanh thang "now < BTN_KHOI_DONG_MS":
926 HAL_GetTick() la uint32_t, tran ve 0 sau ~49.7 ngay chay lien tuc. Voi
927 phep so sanh cu, dung luc tran nut START/STOP se bi cam them 1.5 giay
928 mot cach vo co - dung vao mot he bang tai thi day la loi mat lenh dung.
929 Chot chi bat len MOT LAN roi khong bao gio xet lai. */
930 if (qua_khoi_dong == 0u)
931 {
932 if (now < BTN_KHOI_DONG_MS) { return; }
933 qua_khoi_dong = 1u;
934 }
935 
936 if ((now - tick_mau) < BTN_MAU_MS) { return; }
937 tick_mau = now;
938 
939 /* Module nut 3 chan, tro keo xuong => nhan = muc CAO */
940 muc_start = (HAL_GPIO_ReadPin(BTN_START_GPIO_Port, BTN_START_Pin) == GPIO_PIN_SET) ? 1u : 0u;
941 muc_stop = (HAL_GPIO_ReadPin(BTN_STOP_GPIO_Port, BTN_STOP_Pin) == GPIO_PIN_SET) ? 1u : 0u;
942 
943 /* Dem so mau CAO lien tiep; nha nut thi xoa co giu */
944 if (muc_start) { if (dem_start < BTN_SO_MAU) { dem_start++; } }
945 else { dem_start = 0; giu_start = 0; }
946 if (muc_stop) { if (dem_stop < BTN_SO_MAU) { dem_stop++; } }
947 else { dem_stop = 0; giu_stop = 0; }
948 
949 /* ⚠ KHONG co bat ky duong nao khoa duoc nut vat ly. Nut START/STOP la
950 dau vao dieu khien DUY NHAT cua bang tai va phai song vo dieu kien. */
951 
952 /* Con trong cua so khoa thi bo qua (lan dau sau boot khong bi chan) */
953 if ((tick_khoa != 0u) && ((now - tick_khoa) < BTN_KHOA_MS)) { return; }
954 
955 /* --- STOP xet TRUOC START: nhan ca hai thi dung, khong chay --- */
956 if ((dem_stop >= BTN_SO_MAU) && (giu_stop == 0u))
957 {
958 giu_stop = 1u;
959 if (g_motor_chay)
960 {
961 MOTOR_TAT();
962 g_motor_chay = 0u;
963 g_so_lan_stop++;
964 tick_khoa = now;
965 g_su_kien |= SK_NUT_STOP;
966 }
967 return; /* lenh trung trang thai: khong ton khoa */
968 }
969 
970 if ((dem_start >= BTN_SO_MAU) && (giu_start == 0u))
971 {
972 giu_start = 1u;
973 if (g_motor_chay == 0u)
974 {
975 MOTOR_BAT();
976 g_motor_chay = 1u;
977 g_so_lan_start++;
978 tick_khoa = now;
979 g_su_kien |= SK_NUT_START;
980 }
981 }
982}
983 
984/* Xa hang doi thong bao - CHI goi tu VONG LAP CHINH, khong bao gio goi tu
985 ben trong vong cho I2C (vi ham nay in ra UART, chan toi 200ms). */
986static void xa_su_kien(void)
987{
988 uint8_t sk = g_su_kien;
989 
990 if (sk == 0u) { return; }
991 g_su_kien = 0u; /* xoa truoc khi in - khong mat su kien moi */
992 
993 if (sk & SK_NUT_STOP)
994 {
995 uart_in("\r\n>> NUT STOP (PA2): bang tai DUNG\r\n"
996 " Neu ban KHONG he bam nut ma van thay dong nay => nhieu tu\r\n"
997 " motor dang kich nham PA2. Xem huong dan chong nhieu.\r\n");
998 }
999 if (sk & SK_NUT_START) { uart_in("\r\n>> NUT START: bang tai CHAY\r\n"); }
1000 /* IR chi la thong tin tham khao - ghi ro de nguoi van hanh khong hieu
1001 nham rang no dang dieu khien servo. */
1002 if (sk & SK_IR_VAT_DEN) { uart_in(" [IR] vat den (chi dem, KHONG kich gat)\r\n"); }
1003 if (sk & SK_IR_VAT_DI) { uart_in(" [IR] vat di (chi dem, KHONG kich gat)\r\n"); }
1004}
1005 
1006/* Doc encoder TIM4: quang duong tich luy + toc do truc ra.
1007 TIM4 dem 16-bit tu quay vong, nen phai cong don theo HIEU so voi lan truoc. */
1008static void encoder_quet(uint32_t now)
1009{
1010 static uint32_t tick = 0;
1011 static uint16_t cnt_truoc = 0;
1012 static uint8_t lan_dau = 1;
1013 uint16_t cnt_nay;
1014 int16_t delta;
1015 
1016 cnt_nay = (uint16_t)__HAL_TIM_GET_COUNTER(&htim4);
1017 
1018 if (lan_dau)
1019 {
1020 cnt_truoc = cnt_nay;
1021 lan_dau = 0u;
1022 tick = now;
1023 return;
1024 }
1025 
1026 /* Ep ve int16 => tu xu ly dung ca truong hop bo dem tran qua 0 */
1027 delta = (int16_t)(cnt_nay - cnt_truoc);
1028 cnt_truoc = cnt_nay;
1029 g_encoder_cnt += delta;
1030 
1031 /* ★★ SUA 14/08/2026 - CHONG TRAN int32.
1032 Ban cu: g_vong_x100 = (g_encoder_cnt * 100) / XUNG_MOI_VONG.
1033 Phep nhan 100 tran khi g_encoder_cnt > 21.4 trieu, tuc ~11000 vong truc
1034 ra = ~4 gio chay lien tuc o 46 rpm. Tran xong so vong NHAY SANG AM va
1035 ca mm/s lan quang duong thanh rac - loi rat kho doan vi phai chay lau
1036 moi thay. Chia trong ra truoc roi moi nhan phan du thi khong bao gio
1037 tran, ket qua y het. */
1038 {
1039 int32_t vong_tron = g_encoder_cnt / (int32_t)XUNG_MOI_VONG;
1040 int32_t phan_du = g_encoder_cnt % (int32_t)XUNG_MOI_VONG;
1041 int32_t v;
1042 
1043 g_vong_x100 = (vong_tron * 100) + ((phan_du * 100) / (int32_t)XUNG_MOI_VONG);
1044 
1045 v = g_vong_x100;
1046 if (v < 0) { v = -v; }
1047 g_quang_duong_mm = (v * (int32_t)ENC_CHU_VI_X10) / 1000;
1048 }
1049 
1050 if ((now - tick) >= CUA_SO_RPM_MS)
1051 {
1052 static int32_t cnt_moc = 0;
1053 int32_t xung = g_encoder_cnt - cnt_moc;
1054 uint32_t dt = now - tick;
1055 
1056 cnt_moc = g_encoder_cnt;
1057 tick = now;
1058 
1059 /* ★★ SUA 14/08/2026 - CHIA THEO THOI GIAN THUC, KHONG THEO DANH NGHIA.
1060 Ban cu dung hang so (60000/CUA_SO_RPM_MS) = 600, tuc DINH NINH cua so
1061 dung 100ms. Nhung encoder_quet() duoc goi tu vong lap chinh VA tu
1062 phuc_vu_trong_khi_do(); mot phep do mau ton 154ms nen khoang cach
1063 thuc te giua hai lan vao day la 100-180ms. Chia cho 100 trong khi da
1064 gom xung cua 180ms => RPM BAO CAO HON THUC. (Chieu sai: cang cham
1065 nhip vong lap thi so cang phong to.) Lay dt that thi so dung o moi
1066 nhip vong lap, ke ca khi bus I2C loi lam vong lap quay rat nhanh. */
1067 if (xung < 0) { xung = -xung; }
1068 if (dt == 0u) { dt = 1u; }
1069 
1070 g_rpm_x10 = (xung * (int32_t)ENC_HS_RPMX10) / (int32_t)dt;
1071 g_rpm = g_rpm_x10 / 10; /* giu bien cu cho cac cho dang dung */
1072 
1073 /* mm/s = (rpm_x10 / 10) x (chu_vi_x10 / 10) / 60 = tich / 6000 */
1074 g_mm_s = (g_rpm_x10 * (int32_t)ENC_CHU_VI_X10) / 6000;
1075 }
1076}
1077 
1078#if (CHE_DO_BANG_LOG != 0u)
1079/* ==========================================================================
1080 ★★★ BANG LOG TONG HOP - THEM 14/08/2026, MO RONG tu khoi doc encoder ★★★
1081 ==========================================================================
1082 MOT dong = MOT khoanh khac cua CA he thong. Dung de quay man hinh lam
1083 video bao ve: chi mot nguon in duy nhat nen cot khong bao gio le.
1084 
1085 Goi tu VONG LAP CHINH. ⚠ TUYET DOI khong goi tu phuc_vu_trong_khi_do():
1086 ham nay in UART, ma uart_in() chan toi 200ms - du de lam HET HAN co
1087 AVALID (472ms) cua cam bien => phep do mau dang do se that bai.
1088 
1089 BA VIEC:
1090 1) In tieu de cot moi DASH_SO_DONG_TD dong.
1091 2) In mot dong so lieu moi DASH_CHU_KY_MS.
1092 3) GIAM SAT: relay dang dong ma bo dem khong nhuc mot xung qua
1093 ENC_KET_CANH_BAO_MS => canh bao kem ba nguyen nhan theo thu tu kiem.
1094 
1095 ⚠ Viec (3) CHI CANH BAO, KHONG cat relay. Co y: bang tai dung dot ngot
1096 giua chu trinh gat co the ket phoi vao canh gat. Muon no tu cat motor thi
1097 phai thiet ke them trinh tu dung an toan - bao lai truoc khi lam.
1098 
1099 ⚠ CA KHOI CHI DOC BIEN. Khong ghi vao mot bien trang thai nao cua khoi
1100 gat / khoi mau / khoi hien thi => khong the gay hoi quy. */
1101 
1102/* Ten mau NGAN (TCS_TenMau() tra ve chuoi 30 ky tu, qua dai cho mot cot). */
1103static const char *ten_mau_ngan(TCS_Mau_t m)
1104{
1105 switch (m)
1106 {
1107 case TCS_XANH_DUONG: return "XANH ";
1108 case TCS_MAU_KHAC: return "KHAC ";
1109 case TCS_KHONG_RO: return "BAOHOA";
1110 default: return "TRONG ";
1111 }
1112}
1113 
1114/* Ten buoc gat NGAN - ten_buoc() day du dai toi 35 ky tu. */
1115static const char *ten_buoc_ngan(Gat_t b)
1116{
1117 switch (b)
1118 {
1119 case G_SAN_SANG: return "SANSANG";
1120 case G_NANG: return "NANG ";
1121 case G_CHO_TROI: return "CHOTROI";
1122 case G_DANG_GAT: return "DANGGAT";
1123 case G_GIU: return "GIU ";
1124 case G_VE_HOME: return "VEHOME ";
1125 default: return "KHOA ";
1126 }
1127}
1128 
1129/* Goc servo DANG PHAT ra (khong phai goc dich): suy nguoc tu do rong xung
1130 trong thanh ghi CCR. Ban nay khong co co SERVO_DAO_CHIEU nen anh xa
1131 thang: 1000us = 0 do ... 2000us = 180 do. */
1132static uint8_t servo_goc_hien_tai(void)
1133{
1134 uint16_t us = Servo_XungHienTai();
1135 
1136 if (us <= SERVO_XUNG_MIN_US) { return 0u; }
1137 if (us >= SERVO_XUNG_MAX_US) { return 180u; }
1138 
1139 return (uint8_t)(((uint32_t)(us - SERVO_XUNG_MIN_US) * 180u) /
1140 (uint32_t)(SERVO_XUNG_MAX_US - SERVO_XUNG_MIN_US));
1141}
1142 
1143/* ==== Dong tieu de cot ====================================================
1144 ⚠ DO RONG TUNG O PHAI KHOP TUYET DOI voi format cua bang_log_in(), neu
1145 khong thi quay video ra bang le cot - loi nhin thay ngay va rat kho chiu.
1146 Khoa so do o day de sau nay sua khoi phai dem lai bang tay:
1147 
1148 o 1 t(s) : 9 | o 2 encoder : 28 | o 3 mau : 36 |
1149 o 4 servo : 13 | o 5 bo dem : 9 | o 6 motor: con lai
1150 
1151 Cong thuc o 2: %8ld(xung) + sp + %7s(vong) + sp + %3ld.%ld(rpm) + sp
1152 + %4ld(mm/s) + sp = 8+1+7+1+5+1+4+1 = 28.
1153 Cong thuc o 3: sp + %4u + sp + %3u x3 + sp + %+5d + sp + %+4d + sp
1154 + %s(6 ky tu co dinh) + sp = 36.
1155 ⚠ ten_mau_ngan() phai luon dai DUNG 6 ky tu, ten_buoc_ngan() DUNG 7 -
1156 da dem san bang cach dem khoang trang o hai ham tren. Them ten moi thi
1157 phai dem lai.
1158 
1159 Chia lam ba lan goi uart_in() theo quy tac "CHIA NHO" o khoi
1160 CHE_DO_DO_MAU: mot chuoi qua dai co the bi cat giua chung vi han
1161 thoi gian cua HAL_UART_Transmit. */
1162static void dash_tieu_de(void)
1163{
1164 char b[200];
1165 
1166 uart_in("\r\n"
1167 " t(s) | xung vong rpm mm/s "
1168 "| C r g b b-r b-g mau "
1169 "| goc buoc | DO/XANH | MOTOR\r\n");
1170 uart_in("---------+----------------------------"
1171 "+------------------------------------"
1172 "+-------------+---------+------\r\n");
1173 snprintf(b, sizeof(b),
1174 " (r,g,b = ti le chuan hoa, r+g+b=255. GAT khi b-r >= %+d, %u lan lien tiep.)\r\n",
1175 (int)(TCS_LayDbrNen() + BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN), (unsigned)XAC_NHAN_XANH);
1176 uart_in(b);
1177}
1178 
1179static void bang_log_in(uint32_t now)
1180{
1181 static uint8_t lan_dau = 1u;
1182 static uint32_t tick_in = 0u; /* moc in dinh ky */
1183 static uint32_t tick_ket = 0u; /* moc lan cuoi CON THAY xung */
1184 static uint32_t tick_bao = 0u; /* moc in lai canh bao ket */
1185 static int32_t cnt_ket = 0; /* so xung tai tick_ket */
1186 static uint8_t dem_dong = 0u; /* dem de in lai tieu de cot */
1187 char b[200];
1188 char s_vong[16]; /* so vong da dung san co dau */
1189 int32_t v;
1190 const char *dau;
1191 
1192 if (lan_dau)
1193 {
1194 lan_dau = 0u;
1195 tick_in = now;
1196 tick_ket = now;
1197 tick_bao = now;
1198 cnt_ket = g_encoder_cnt;
1199 dem_dong = 0u;
1200 dash_tieu_de();
1201 }
1202 
1203 /* ---------- 1) GIAM SAT KET ---------- */
1204 if (g_encoder_cnt != cnt_ket)
1205 {
1206 cnt_ket = g_encoder_cnt; /* con xung -> lam tuoi moc */
1207 tick_ket = now;
1208 if (g_enc_ket != 0u)
1209 {
1210 g_enc_ket = 0u;
1211 uart_in(" [ENC] Da co xung tro lai - truc dong co dang quay.\r\n");
1212 }
1213 }
1214 else if (g_motor_chay == 0u)
1215 {
1216 tick_ket = now; /* motor dung thi dung im la DUNG */
1217 }
1218 else if ((g_enc_ket == 0u) && ((now - tick_ket) >= ENC_KET_CANH_BAO_MS))
1219 {
1220 g_enc_ket = 1u;
1221 if (g_enc_so_lan_ket < 0xFFFFFFFFu) { g_enc_so_lan_ket++; }
1222 tick_bao = now - ENC_BAO_KET_LAP_MS; /* ep in ngay lan dau */
1223 }
1224 else
1225 {
1226 /* dang ket va chua toi han in lai - khong lam gi */
1227 }
1228 
1229 if ((g_enc_ket != 0u) && ((now - tick_bao) >= ENC_BAO_KET_LAP_MS))
1230 {
1231 tick_bao = now;
1232 uart_in("\r\n[!] ENCODER KHONG DEM trong khi RELAY DANG DONG.\r\n"
1233 " Ba nguyen nhan, kiem tra theo dung thu tu nay:\r\n"
1234 " 1) Bang tai/truc bi KET hoac tuot day dai - motor khong quay\r\n"
1235 " that. Nghe tieng motor: co u u ma khong quay = ket co khi.\r\n");
1236 uart_in(" 2) Encoder chua co dien hoac chua dau tin hieu: day encoder\r\n"
1237 " cua JGB37 can rieng +5V va GND, hai day A/B vao PB6/PB7.\r\n"
1238 " Dau hieu: xung dung dung 0 tu luc cap nguon toi gio.\r\n"
1239 " 3) Relay dong nhung 24V khong len - do dien ap 2 dau motor.\r\n");
1240 }
1241 
1242 /* ---------- 2) IN TIEU DE CO CHU KY ---------- */
1243 if ((now - tick_in) < DASH_CHU_KY_MS) { return; }
1244 tick_in = now;
1245 
1246 if (dem_dong >= (uint8_t)DASH_SO_DONG_TD)
1247 {
1248 dem_dong = 0u;
1249 dash_tieu_de(); /* nhac lai ten cot + nguong gat dang dung */
1250 }
1251 dem_dong++;
1252 
1253 /* ---------- 3) MOT DONG SO LIEU ---------- */
1254 /* ⚠ SO VONG PHAI DUNG SAN THANH CHUOI TRUOC, khong nhet thang vao format
1255 chinh. Hai ly do:
1256 1) "%ld.%02ld" voi so am cho ra "-1.-50" (dau am in ra HAI lan).
1257 2) Neu de "%s%3ld.%02ld" thi o nay rong 6 hay 7 ky tu TUY dau am co
1258 hay khong => ca phan con lai cua dong bi day lech mot cot. Dung
1259 %7s voi chuoi da co dau thi be rong CO DINH.
1260 So xung AM chi co nghia la hai day A/B dau nguoc - van dung tri so. */
1261 v = g_vong_x100;
1262 dau = "";
1263 if (v < 0) { v = -v; dau = "-"; }
1264 snprintf(s_vong, sizeof(s_vong), "%s%ld.%02ld",
1265 dau, (long)(v / 100), (long)(v % 100));
1266 
1267 /* ⚠ MOT LAN snprintf DUY NHAT cho ca dong. Neu chia lam nhieu lan
1268 uart_in() thi giua hai lan goi co the bi mot dong SU KIEN (PHOI XANH,
1269 nut bam) chen vao giua - dong so lieu se bi be doi. */
1270 snprintf(b, sizeof(b),
1271 " %4lu.%lu |%8ld %7s %3ld.%ld %4ld "
1272 "| %4u %3u %3u %3u %+5d %+4d %s "
1273 "| %3u %s | %2lu / %2lu | %s%s\r\n",
1274 /* --- thoi gian tu luc cap nguon --- */
1275 (unsigned long)(now / 1000u), (unsigned long)((now % 1000u) / 100u),
1276 /* --- encoder / bang tai --- */
1277 (long)g_encoder_cnt,
1278 s_vong,
1279 (long)(g_rpm_x10 / 10), (long)(g_rpm_x10 % 10),
1280 (long)g_mm_s,
1281 /* --- cam bien mau --- */
1282 (unsigned)g_kq.raw_c, (unsigned)g_kq.r, (unsigned)g_kq.g,
1283 (unsigned)g_kq.b, (int)g_kq.d_br, (int)g_kq.d_bg,
1284 ten_mau_ngan(g_kq.mau),
1285 /* --- co cau gat --- */
1286 (unsigned)servo_goc_hien_tai(), ten_buoc_ngan(g_gat_buoc),
1287 /* --- hai bo dem san pham (dung so DAY DU, khong % 10 nhu LED) --- */
1288 (unsigned long)g_dem_bao_do, (unsigned long)g_dem_gat,
1289 /* --- bang tai --- */
1290 (g_motor_chay != 0u) ? "CHAY" : "DUNG",
1291 (g_cam_bien_ok != 0u) ? "" : " [MAT I2C]");
1292 uart_in(b);
1293}
1294#endif /* CHE_DO_BANG_LOG */
1295 
1296/* ★★ BAN NoUART: ham nay LUON tra ve 0.
1297 Duong nhan da bi tat han o USER CODE BEGIN 2 (UART_MODE_TX + PA10 pull-up),
1298 nen phan cung khong the nhan byte nao. Tra ve 0 ngay tu day de:
1299 - khoi doc thanh ghi cua mot bo thu da tat
1300 - moi khoi `switch (c)` ben duoi thanh KHONG BAO GIO chay, ke ca hai vong
1301 `while (uart_doc_lenh() == 0u)` trong lenh 'c' va 'd' - hai vong do neu
1302 vao duoc se treo VINH VIEN vi khong con gi go phim duoc nua.
1303 Than ham goc duoc giu lai trong khoi `#if 0` ngay duoi de con doi chieu khi
1304 can dung lai ban co UART. Dung `#if 0` chu KHONG de thanh mot ham tinh
1305 khong ai goi - AC5 se bao warning #177-D "declared but never referenced",
1306 ma quy uoc cua du an la phai 0 Error 0 Warning. */
1307static uint8_t uart_doc_lenh(void)
1308{
1309 return 0u;
1310}
1311 
1312#if 0 /* ===== BAN GOC, chi de tham khao - xem khoi comment o tren ===== */
1313static uint8_t uart_doc_lenh_ban_cu(void)

1317 __HAL_UART_CLEAR_OREFLAG(&huart1);
1318 }
1319 if (__HAL_UART_GET_FLAG(&huart1, UART_FLAG_RXNE))
1320 {
1321 return (uint8_t)(huart1.Instance->DR & 0xFFu);
1322 }
1323 return 0u;

1327/* In 1 dong ket qua kiem tra: [so] ten ... OK / LOI + goi y sua */
1328static void in_dong_kt(int stt, const char *ten, uint8_t ok, const char *chi_tiet)
1329{
1330 char b[180];
1331 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "[%d] %-26s : %s %s\r\n", stt, ten, ok ? "OK " : "LOI", chi_tiet);
1332 uart_in(b);
1333}

1335/* Quet bus I2C va in danh sach dia chi tim duoc.
1336 ⚠ CONG CU QUAN TRONG NHAT khi cam bien khong noi chuyen duoc: phan biet
1337 duoc "dut day" (khong co dia chi nao) voi "cam nham cap chan GY-33"
1338 (thay 0x5A cua MCU phu thay vi 0x29 cua chip TCS34725). */
1339static void quet_bus_va_in(void)
1340{
1341 char b[140];
1342 uint8_t i;
1343 uint8_t thay_29 = 0u, thay_5a = 0u;
1344 
1345 uart_in("\r\n--- QUET BUS I2C2 (PB10=SCL, PB11=SDA) ---\r\n");
1346 TCS_QuetBus(&g_cd);
1347 
1348 if (g_cd.so_tb == 0u)
1349 {
1350 uart_in(" KHONG co thiet bi nao tra loi.\r\n"
1351 " -> Kiem tra: GY-33 co dien 3.3V chua, GND CHUNG voi STM32,\r\n"
1352 " chan SCL cua GY-33 vao PB10, SDA vao PB11 (rat de cam\r\n"
1353 " nguoc 2 day nay), va tro keo len 4.7k tu SCL/SDA len 3.3V\r\n"
1354 " neu module KHONG co san tro.\r\n");
1355 return;
1356 }
1357 
1358 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " Tim thay %u thiet bi:\r\n", (unsigned)g_cd.so_tb);
1359 uart_in(b);
1360 for (i = 0u; (i < g_cd.so_tb) && (i < 8u); i++)
1361 {
1362 const char *ghi_chu = "";
1363 if (g_cd.dia_chi[i] == TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT)
1364 {
1365 ghi_chu = "<== chip TCS34725 - DUNG CAI NAY";
1366 thay_29 = 1u;
1367 }
1368 if (g_cd.dia_chi[i] == TCS_DIA_CHI_GY33)
1369 {
1370 ghi_chu = "<== MCU phu cua GY-33 (cap chan CT/DR)";
1371 thay_5a = 1u;
1372 }
1373 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " 0x%02X %s\r\n", (unsigned)g_cd.dia_chi[i], ghi_chu);
1374 uart_in(b);
1375 }
1376 
1377 /* GY-33 co HAI duong I2C: SCL/SDA di thang vao chip 0x29, con CT/DR di vao
1378 MCU phu 0x5A. Chi ket luan duoc khi biet dang thay dia chi nao. */
1379 if ((thay_29 == 0u) && thay_5a)
1380 {
1381 uart_in(" [!] CHI thay MCU phu (0x5A), KHONG thay chip TCS34725 (0x29).\r\n"
1382 " Dang cam nham vao cap chan CT/DR cua GY-33.\r\n"
1383 " => Chuyen 2 day sang cap chan ghi SCL va SDA tren module.\r\n");
1384 }
1385 else if (thay_29 && thay_5a)
1386 {
1387 uart_in(" [!] Thay CA HAI dia chi => MCU phu cua GY-33 dang cung nam\r\n"
1388 " tren bus nay. Firmware van chay duoc, nhung neu bo dem\r\n"
1389 " 'cuu bus' (go 't') tang deu thi chinh no dang tranh bus:\r\n"
1390 " luc do phai cat/nhac chan MCU phu ra khoi bus.\r\n");
1391 }
1392}
1393 
1394/* Chay tu kiem tra ket noi va in bao cao ra terminal */
1395static uint8_t kiem_tra_ket_noi(void)
1396{
1397 char b[190];
1398 
1399 uart_in("\r\n--- KIEM TRA KET NOI TCS34725 ---\r\n");
1400 TCS_KiemTraKetNoi(&g_cd);
1401 
1402 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "So thiet bi tren bus=%u | ID=0x%02X | C=%u R=%u G=%u B=%u (tran %u)\r\n",
1403 (unsigned)g_cd.so_tb, (unsigned)g_cd.id, (unsigned)g_cd.c,
1404 (unsigned)g_cd.r, (unsigned)g_cd.g, (unsigned)g_cd.b,
1405 (unsigned)g_cd.tran);
1406 uart_in(b);
1407 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "Gain=%s | Tich phan=%ums (ATIME=0x%02X)\r\n",
1408 TCS_TenGain(TCS_LayGain()), (unsigned)TCS_ThoiGianTichPhan_ms(),
1409 (unsigned)TCS_LayTichPhan());
1410 uart_in(b);
1411 
1412 in_dong_kt(1, "Bus I2C2 - dia chi 0x29", g_cd.ok_bus,
1413 g_cd.ok_bus ? "chip TCS34725 tra ACK"
1414 : "-> go 'p' quet bus (co the dang cam nham CT/DR)");
1415 in_dong_kt(2, "Thanh ghi ID (0x44/0x4D)", g_cd.ok_id,
1416 g_cd.ok_id ? "dung chip TCS34725"
1417 : "-> co thiet bi o 0x29 nhung khong phai TCS34725");
1418 in_dong_kt(3, "Doc duoc so lieu moi", g_cd.ok_doc,
1419 g_cd.ok_doc ? "AVALID len, doc du 4 kenh"
1420 : "-> chip khong hoan tat chu ky do (kiem tra nguon 3.3V)");
1421 in_dong_kt(4, "Muc sang", g_cd.ok_sang,
1422 g_cd.ok_sang ? "du sang"
1423 : "-> qua toi: bat den module (lenh 'l'), tang gain ('a')");
1424 in_dong_kt(5, "Khong bao hoa", g_cd.ok_khong_bh,
1425 g_cd.ok_khong_bh ? "con cho de phoi sang hon"
1426 : "-> qua sang: ha gain ('a') hoac ha tich phan ('n')");
1427 in_dong_kt(6, "Tach duoc 3 kenh", g_cd.ok_tach_mau,
1428 g_cd.ok_tach_mau ? "R,G,B khac nhau"
1429 : "-> 3 kenh dinh nhau: cam bien bi che kin / du lieu rac");
1430 
1431 if (g_cd.du_dieu_kien)
1432 {
1433 if (g_cd.ok_sang && g_cd.ok_khong_bh && g_cd.ok_tach_mau)
1434 {
1435 uart_in("=> KET NOI TOT. San sang quet mau.\r\n");
1436 }
1437 else
1438 {
1439 uart_in("=> Bus + chip OK, muc 4-5-6 chi la CANH BAO (phu thuoc vat dang\r\n"
1440 " dat truoc cam bien). Van quet duoc; go 'b' de do lai nen.\r\n");
1441 }
1442 }
1443 else
1444 {
1445 uart_in("=> CHUA DAT. Sua day theo goi y o tren roi go 'k' de kiem tra lai.\r\n");
1446 quet_bus_va_in();
1447 }
1448 return g_cd.du_dieu_kien;
1449}

1484/* ★★ HAM PHUC VU - driver cam bien goi lien tuc trong suot 154ms cho do xong.
1485 Khong co no thi ca he thong dung hinh moi lan do mau: servo dung giua
1486 hanh trinh (GIAT rat ro), nut nhan bi bo sot, IR khong duoc quet.
1487 PHAI ngan va TUYET DOI khong duoc goi lai ham TCS_* nao (de quy). */
1488static void phuc_vu_trong_khi_do(void)
1489{
1490 /* ★★ BAY GIO PHAI QUET CA NUT VA IR O DAY.
1491 Ban cu chi truot servo + doc encoder, vi nut/IR duoc quet du o vong lap
1492 chinh - luc do GY-33 chi doc khi co chu trinh phan loai.
1493 GY-33 gio QUET LIEN TUC nen he gan nhu luc nao cung dang nam trong vong
1494 cho AVALID nay. Neu khong quet nut o day thi bo loc 20 mau x 10ms cua
1495 nut se mat 3-4 giay moi dem du => bam START/STOP nhu khong an.
1496 AN TOAN: ca bon ham duoi day deu KHONG in UART va KHONG goi TCS_* nao
1497 (ir_quet/nut_quet chi dat co su kien, vong lap chinh moi in). */
1498 uint32_t t = HAL_GetTick();
1499 
1500 Servo_Task();
1501 encoder_quet(t);
1502 nut_quet(t); /* START/STOP phai an lien tay ke ca khi dang do mau */
1503 ir_quet(t); /* chi cap nhat bo dem hien thi */
1504}

1506/* In dong tieu de cot cho che do quet lien tuc */

1508 ==========================================================================
1509 LY DO: log 14/08/2026 cho thay chuoi mau DA DU DIEU KIEN GAT (nen +35,
1510 nguong gat +41, phoi xanh cho +61 roi +59 - hai lan lien tiep vuot nguong)
1511 ma KHONG co dong "===== PHOI XANH =====" nao, servo dung im. Nghia la
1512 nghen nam BEN TRONG gat_task(), nhung bang do chi in so lieu CAM BIEN nen
1513 khong nhin thay duoc gi ben trong khoi gat - phai doan.
1514 Hai kha nang khong the phan biet duoc tu log cu:
1515 (a) g_tu_dong == 0 -> gat_task() thoat ngay dong dau
1516 (b) may trang thai ket o G_KHOA (chua vu trang lai duoc)
1517 Nay in thang bon con so quyet dinh vao tieu de bang. Nhin mot cai la biet,
1518 khong phai doan them lan nao nua.
1519 ⚠ ten_buoc() dinh nghia MAI TAN DUOI (dong ~1500) nen phai khai bao truoc
1520 o day, neu khong AC5 bao loi #20 "identifier undefined". */

1570 Hai so nay cho biet ngay firmware dang lay moc o dau. */

1597 khong phai chinh nguong. */

1600
