1618/* Doc cam bien 1 lan va in 1 dong ket qua */
1619static void doc_va_in(void)
1620{
1621 char dong[190];
1622 
1623 g_cam_bien_ok = TCS_Doc(&g_kq);
1624 g_so_lan_doc++;
1625 
1626 if (g_cam_bien_ok == 0u)
1627 {
1628 uart_in("!! MAT LIEN LAC I2C - driver da tu cuu bus nhung van khong doc duoc.\r\n"
1629 " Go 'p' de quet bus, hoac 'x' de cuu bus lai bang tay.\r\n");
1630 return;
1631 }
1632 snprintf(dong, sizeof(dong),
1633 " %5u | %3u %3u %3u | %+4d %+4d | %-7s | %s%s\r\n",
1634 (unsigned)g_kq.raw_c,
1635 g_kq.r, g_kq.g, g_kq.b, g_kq.d_br, g_kq.d_bg,
1636 g_ir_vat ? "CO VAT" : "TRONG", TCS_TenMau(g_kq.mau),
1637 g_kq.bao_hoa ? " [BAO HOA]" : "");
1638 uart_in(dong);
1639}
1640 
1641/* In 1 dong tu ket qua DA CO SAN trong g_kq - KHONG doc lai cam bien.
1642 Dung o che do quet lien tuc ('q'): vong lap chinh da doc mot lan bang
1643 gy33_quet() roi, doc them lan nua chi ton them 154ms cho khong.
1644 Cot IR o day thuan tuy la thong tin tham khao, khong tham gia dieu khien. */
1645static void in_dong_quet(void)
1646{
1647 char dong[190];
1648 
1649 /* ★★ THEM 14/08/2026: IN CA SO DEM THO r/g/b, khong chi so da chuan hoa.
1650 LY DO THAT: dong log "C=2 | 0 0 254 | +254 +254" ngay 14/08/2026 khong
1651 the chan doan duoc bang mat - phai giai nguoc he phuong trinh chuan hoa
1652 moi suy ra kenh B tho phai ~510 trong khi C chi co 2, tu do moi biet la
1653 BYTE HONG chu khong phai thieu sang. Neu luc do in san so tho thi nhin
1654 mot cai la thay ngay. Ba cot tho la thu DUY NHAT cho biet cam bien that
1655 su dang thu duoc bao nhieu photon. */
1656 snprintf(dong, sizeof(dong),
1657 " %5u | %5u %5u %5u | %3u %3u %3u | %+4d %+4d | %-7s | %s%s\r\n",
1658 (unsigned)g_kq.raw_c,
1659 (unsigned)g_kq.raw_r, (unsigned)g_kq.raw_g, (unsigned)g_kq.raw_b,
1660 g_kq.r, g_kq.g, g_kq.b, g_kq.d_br, g_kq.d_bg,
1661 g_ir_vat ? "CO VAT" : "TRONG", TCS_TenMau(g_kq.mau),
1662 g_kq.bao_hoa ? " [BAO HOA]" : "");
1663 uart_in(dong);
1664}
1665 
1666#if (CHE_DO_DO_MAU != 0u)
1667/* ★★ IN SO LIEU O CHE DO DO MAU - co TIET CHE theo thoi gian.
1668 Khac in_dong_quet() o cho: khong in moi vong lap (se ra ~190 dong/giay o
1669 gain/ATIME mac dinh, khong doc noi va lam nghen UART), ma DO_MAU_CHU_KY_MS
1670 moi in mot dong, va cu DO_MAU_SO_DONG_TD dong lai in lai tieu de cot.
1671 Dung lai g_kq da co san - KHONG doc lai cam bien. */
1672static void do_mau_in(uint32_t now)
1673{
1674 static uint32_t tick = 0u;
1675 static uint8_t dem = 0u;
1676 static uint8_t lan_dau = 1u;
1677 
1678 if (lan_dau) /* in tieu de ngay dong dau tien */
1679 {
1680 lan_dau = 0u;
1681 dem = 0u;
1682 in_tieu_de_cot();
1683 }
1684 
1685 if ((now - tick) < DO_MAU_CHU_KY_MS) { return; }
1686 tick = now;
1687 
1688 if (dem >= (uint8_t)DO_MAU_SO_DONG_TD)
1689 {
1690 dem = 0u;
1691 in_tieu_de_cot(); /* nhac lai nguong + muc nen dang bam */
1692 }
1693 dem++;
1694 
1695 in_dong_quet();
1696}
1697#endif /* CHE_DO_DO_MAU */
1698 
1699/* ★★ HOC LAI NEN MOT LAN sau khi motor da chay - xem HOC_LAI_SO_LAN.
1700 Goi tu vong lap chinh, NGAY SAU gy33_quet() (can g_kq da moi).
1701 ⚠ KHONG duoc goi tu trong vong cho I2C: ham nay co the goi TCS_HocSanToi()
1702 von ton mot chu ky tich phan (154ms). */
1703static void hoc_lai_nen_khi_chay(void)
1704{
1705 static uint8_t da_hoc = 0u; /* mot lan duy nhat moi lan cap nguon */
1706 static uint8_t dem = 0u;
1707 static int16_t br_truoc = 0;
1708 
1709 if (da_hoc != 0u) { return; }
1710 if (g_motor_chay == 0u) { dem = 0u; return; } /* chi hoc khi dang chay */
1711 
1712 /* CHOT 1: chi dem lan doc da ket luan TRONG (xem giai thich o HOC_LAI_*) */
1713 if (g_kq.mau != TCS_TRONG)
1714 {
1715 dem = 0u;
1716 return;
1717 }
1718 
1719 /* CHOT 2: phai dung yen - lech qua 2 don vi la dem lai tu dau */
1720 if (dem > 0u)
1721 {
1722 int16_t lech = (int16_t)(g_kq.d_br - br_truoc);
1723 
1724 if (lech < 0) { lech = (int16_t)(-lech); }
1725 if (lech > (int16_t)TCS_NEN_BAM_DAO_DONG) { dem = 0u; }
1726 }
1727 br_truoc = g_kq.d_br;
1728 if (dem < 255u) { dem++; }
1729 
1730 if (dem >= (uint8_t)HOC_LAI_SO_LAN)
1731 {
1732 TCS_KetQua_t k;
1733 char b[190];
1734 int16_t br_cu = TCS_LayDbrNen();
1735 
1736 da_hoc = 1u; /* CHOT 3: chi mot lan */
1737 
1738 if (TCS_HocSanToi(&k) != 0u)
1739 {
1740 int16_t nbr, nbg;
1741 
1742 TCS_LayNguongXanh(&nbr, &nbg);
1743 snprintf(b, sizeof(b),
1744 "\r\n>> HOC LAI NEN voi MOTOR DANG CHAY: b-r %+d -> %+d (b-g %+d)\r\n",
1745 br_cu, TCS_LayDbrNen(), TCS_LayDbgNen());
1746 uart_in(b);
1747 snprintf(b, sizeof(b),
1748 " Nguong moi: XANH khi b-r >= %+d | GAT khi b-r >= %+d | TRONG %+d..%+d\r\n",
1749 nbr, (int)(TCS_LayDbrNen() + BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN),
1750 (int)(TCS_LayDbrNen() - TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR),
1751 (int)(TCS_LayDbrNen() + TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR));
1752 uart_in(b);
1753 }
1754 else
1755 {
1756 uart_in("\r\n>> Hoc lai nen THAT BAI (loi I2C) - giu moc cu.\r\n");
1757 }
1758 }
1759}
1760 
1761/* ======================================================================== */
1762/* KHOI A - QUET GY-33 LIEN TUC (nguon kich DUY NHAT cua he) */
1763/* ======================================================================== */
1764 
1765/* ★★ Doc cam bien mau MOT chu ky va cap nhat cac co trang thai cua he.
1766 Duoc goi LIEN TUC tu vong lap chinh, KHONG phu thuoc:
1767 - trang thai bang tai (motor chay hay dung)
1768 - cam bien IR E18
1769 - vi tri cua servo
1770 Tra ve 1 = doc duoc, 0 = loi I2C.
1771 
1772 Hai co ma khoi B doc:
1773 g_xanh_lien_tiep : so lan doc XANH DUONG LIEN TIEP (reset ve 0 khi
1774 doc ra mau khac). >= XAC_NHAN_XANH la du chac.
1775 g_da_roi_xanh : bat len khi doc ra KHAC xanh - dung lam dieu kien
1776 vu trang lai sau mot lan gat. */
1777/* ★ LOP 1 cua bo chong gat oan: ngoai viec driver ket luan XANH DUONG, con
1778 BAT BUOC d_br phai vuot NEN it nhat BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN don vi.
1779 Doc nen bang TCS_LayDbrNen() (khong phai hang so bake) nen bien nay TU
1780 DONG bam theo moc nen, ke ca khi "nen tu bam" doi moc giua chung. */
1781static uint8_t la_xanh_chac(const TCS_KetQua_t *k)
1782{
1783 int16_t muc_toi_thieu;
1784 
1785 if (k->mau != TCS_XANH_DUONG) { return 0u; }
1786 
1787 muc_toi_thieu = (int16_t)(TCS_LayDbrNen() + BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN);
1788 return (uint8_t)((k->d_br >= muc_toi_thieu) ? 1u : 0u);
1789}
1790 
1791static uint8_t gy33_quet(void)
1792{
1793 TCS_KetQua_t k;
1794 
1795 if (TCS_Doc(&k) == 0u)
1796 {
1797 g_cam_bien_ok = 0u;
1798 return 0u; /* loi I2C: GIU NGUYEN cac bo dem lien
1799 tiep - mot lan mat lien lac khong duoc
1800 tinh la bang chung cua bat cu dieu gi */
1801 }
1802 
1803 g_kq = k;
1804 g_cam_bien_ok = 1u;
1805 g_so_lan_doc++;
1806 
1807 /* Dai dao dong cua d_br - so lieu chan doan cho lenh 't' va 'w'.
1808 Neu g_br_max cham toi nguong xanh trong luc bang tai TRONG thi chinh
1809 cai nen dang tu kich - phai tang BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN. */
1810 if (k.d_br < g_br_min) { g_br_min = k.d_br; }
1811 if (k.d_br > g_br_max) { g_br_max = k.d_br; }
1812 
1813 /* --- LOP 1 + LOP 2: bien an toan, roi dem so lan LIEN TIEP --- */
1814 if (la_xanh_chac(&k))
1815 {
1816 if (g_xanh_lien_tiep < 255u) { g_xanh_lien_tiep++; }
1817 if (g_xanh_lien_tiep > g_xanh_max) { g_xanh_max = g_xanh_lien_tiep; }
1818 }
1819 else
1820 {
1821 /* Chuoi xanh vua DUT. Neu dut khi chua du xac nhan thi rat co the mot
1822 phoi xanh vua troi qua ma khong kip ket luan - dem lai de biet co
1823 phai dang dat XAC_NHAN_XANH cao qua so voi toc do bang tai khong. */
1824 if ((g_xanh_lien_tiep > 0u) && (g_xanh_lien_tiep < XAC_NHAN_XANH))
1825 {
1826 g_dem_hut++;
1827 }
1828 if (k.mau == TCS_XANH_DUONG)
1829 {
1830 /* Driver bao xanh nhung KHONG du bien => coi nhu chua ket luan duoc.
1831 Dem lai: con so nay lon nghia la BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN dang dat cao qua
1832 so voi phoi that (bo sot phoi), hoac nen dang troi len. */
1833 g_dem_xanh_yeu++;
1834 }
1835 g_xanh_lien_tiep = 0u;
1836 }
1837 
1838 /* --- LOP 3: chi TRONG (bang tai rong) moi duoc coi la "da roi xanh" ---
1839 ⚠ Truoc day chi can "khac xanh" la du, nhung MAU KHAC cung thoa dieu
1840 kien do => mot vat khong phai xanh nam giua hai lan doc xanh gia van
1841 mo duong cho lan gat ke tiep. Bat buoc phai la TRONG thi canh len moi
1842 that su co nghia: bang tai PHAI trong tro lai roi moi nhan phoi moi. */
1843 if (k.mau == TCS_TRONG)
1844 {
1845 if (g_trong_lien_tiep < 255u) { g_trong_lien_tiep++; }
1846 if (g_trong_lien_tiep >= XAC_NHAN_TRONG) { g_da_roi_xanh = 1u; }
1847 }
1848 else
1849 {
1850 g_trong_lien_tiep = 0u;
1851 if (k.mau == TCS_MAU_KHAC) { g_dem_khac++; }
1852 }
1853 
1854 /* --- Dem MAU KHAC LIEN TIEP cho chu trinh BAO DO (them 03/08/2026).
1855 Dem rieng, khong dung chung g_dem_khac vi con so kia dem TONG so lan
1856 doc chu khong phai chuoi lien tiep. */
1857 if (k.mau == TCS_MAU_KHAC)
1858 {
1859 /* Ghi lai thoi diem BAT DAU chuoi - chuoi bi dut thi lan sau tinh lai
1860 tu dau. Chinh cho nay chan duoc chuyen tiep mep phoi xanh: no chi
1861 keo dai vai chuc ms nen khong bao gio du TRE_ON_DINH_DO_MS. */
1862 if (g_do_lien_tiep == 0u) { g_do_moc = HAL_GetTick(); }
1863 if (g_do_lien_tiep < 255u) { g_do_lien_tiep++; }
1864 }
1865 else
1866 {
1867 g_do_lien_tiep = 0u;
1868 }
1869 return 1u;
1870}
1871 
1872/* ======================================================================== */
1873/* KHOI B - MAY TRANG THAI CO CAU GAT (chi nghe GY-33) */
1874/* ======================================================================== */
1875 
1876static const char *ten_buoc(Gat_t b)
1877{
1878 switch (b)
1879 {
1880 case G_SAN_SANG: return "SAN SANG (cho GY-33 bao xanh duong)";
1881 case G_NANG: return "SERVO DANG NANG CANH RA GOC CHO";
1882 case G_CHO_TROI: return "CHO PHOI TROI TOI CANH GAT";
1883 case G_DANG_GAT: return "SERVO DANG RA GOC GAT";
1884 case G_GIU: return "GIU TAI GOC GAT";
1885 case G_VE_HOME: return "SERVO DANG VE HOME";
1886 default: return "KHOA (cho phoi roi + het nghi)";
1887 }
1888}
1889 
1890static void doi_buoc(Gat_t buoc_moi)
1891{
1892 g_gat_buoc = buoc_moi;
1893}
1894 
1895/* Dua co cau ve trang thai an toan roi BAT PHAI qua buoc KHOA - con cau
1896 phai thay phoi roi khoi tam nhin + het thoi gian nghi moi gat tiep. */
1897static void gat_ve_an_toan(void)
1898{
1899 Servo_DatTocDo(BUOC_EM); /* ve home theo duong an toan: luon di EM */
1900 Servo_DatGoc(GOC_NGHI_DO);
1901 g_xanh_lien_tiep = 0u;
1902 g_do_lien_tiep = 0u;
1903 g_trong_lien_tiep = 0u;
1904 g_da_roi_xanh = 0u;
1905 g_moc_ve_home = HAL_GetTick();
1906 doi_buoc(G_KHOA);
1907}
1908 
1909/* ★★ VAO TRANG THAI KHOA - dung luc KHOI DONG va sau cac lenh HIEU CHINH
1910 ('b'/'c'/'d'/'w'/'0'/'a'/'n'/'y').
1911 ⚠ CO Y KHONG vao thang G_SAN_SANG. Day chinh la LOP 3 (canh len) chong
1912 loi "vua cap nguon servo da gat": he BUOC phai quan sat duoc bang tai
1913 TRONG on dinh XAC_NHAN_TRONG lan lien tiep roi moi tu vu trang. Mot cai
1914 nen dung yen - du van mau cua no co nam sat nguong den dau - cung khong
1915 bao gio tu kich duoc mot lan gat nao. */
1916static void gat_vu_trang(void)
1917{
1918 Servo_DatTocDo(BUOC_EM); /* ve home theo duong an toan: luon di EM */
1919 Servo_DatGoc(GOC_NGHI_DO);
1920 g_xanh_lien_tiep = 0u;
1921 g_do_lien_tiep = 0u;
1922 g_trong_lien_tiep = 0u;
1923 g_da_roi_xanh = 0u;
1924 g_moc_ve_home = HAL_GetTick();
1925 doi_buoc(G_KHOA);
1926}
1927 
1928/* ★★★★ LED PC13 BAO TRANG THAI - KENH CHAN DOAN DUY NHAT CUA BAN NoUART
1929 ==========================================================================
1930 Ban nay khong go lenh duoc nua, va binh thuong chay khong cam FT232, nen
1931 LED la cach duy nhat biet he dang lam gi. Thay cho "nhip tim" cu (toggle
1932 moi vong lap - nhay khong deu, khong mang thong tin gi).
1933 
1934 ⚠ PC13 tren Blue Pill la ACTIVE-LOW: GPIO_PIN_RESET = SANG, SET = TAT.
1935 
1936 QUY UOC (uu tien tu tren xuong - trang thai xau hon thi de len tren):
1937 Nhay RAT NHANH (5 lan/giay) : MAT LIEN LAC I2C voi cam bien mau
1938 SANG LIEN TUC : dang chay chu trinh gat (xanh HOAC do)
1939 Nhay NGAN moi 1 giay : bang tai DANG CHAY, san sang nhan phoi
1940 Nhay NGAN moi 3 giay : bang tai DANG DUNG, cho nut START
1941 
1942 Dung `now % chu_ky` nen khong can bien trang thai rieng, va khong bi troi
1943 nhip du vong lap chay nhanh cham khac nhau. */
1944static void led_trang_thai(uint32_t now)
1945{
1946 uint32_t chu_ky;
1947 uint32_t sang;
1948 
1949 if (g_mat_lien_lac != 0u)
1950 {
1951 chu_ky = 200u; sang = 100u; /* nhay rat nhanh */
1952 }
1953 else if ((g_gat_buoc != G_SAN_SANG) && (g_gat_buoc != G_KHOA))
1954 {
1955 chu_ky = 1u; sang = 1u; /* now%1 = 0 < 1 => SANG LIEN */
1956 }
1957 else if (g_motor_chay != 0u)
1958 {
1959 chu_ky = 1000u; sang = 80u; /* nhay ngan moi 1 giay */
1960 }
1961 else
1962 {
1963 chu_ky = 3000u; sang = 80u; /* nhay ngan moi 3 giay */
1964 }
1965 
1966 HAL_GPIO_WritePin(LED_GPIO_Port, LED_Pin,
1967 ((now % chu_ky) < sang) ? GPIO_PIN_RESET : GPIO_PIN_SET);
1968}
1969 
1970/* ★★ Chay 1 nhip cua co cau gat. Goi lien tuc trong vong lap chinh.
1971 
1972 ⚠⚠ KIEM TRA LIEN DONG khi doc/sua ham nay:
1973 Trong toan bo ham KHONG duoc xuat hien g_ir_vat, IR_E18_Pin, g_motor_chay,
1974 BTN_START_Pin hay BTN_STOP_Pin. Dau vao hop le DUY NHAT la g_xanh_lien_tiep
1975 va g_da_roi_xanh - ca hai do gy33_quet() sinh ra. */
1976static void gat_task(void)
1977{
1978 static uint32_t moc = 0; /* moc thoi gian cua buoc hien tai */
1979 uint32_t now = HAL_GetTick();
1980 char b[150];
1981 
1982 if (g_tu_dong == 0u) /* dang tam dung bang lenh 's' */
1983 {
1984 return;
1985 }
1986 
1987 /* Chong ket CO CAU CHAP HANH: chi giam sat cac buoc CO CHUYEN DONG hoac
1988 CO DEM GIO. G_SAN_SANG va G_KHOA duoc phep o lai vo han (bang tai co
1989 the trong ca chuc phut) nen KHONG bi timeout. */
1990 if ((g_gat_buoc != G_SAN_SANG) && (g_gat_buoc != G_KHOA) &&
1991 ((now - moc) > TIMEOUT_GAT_MS))
1992 {
1993 uart_in("!! TIMEOUT co cau gat - servo khong bao 'den noi'.\r\n"
1994 " Kiem tra nguon 6V cua servo va co khi canh gat co bi ket.\r\n"
1995 " Danh servo ve home va vu trang lai.\r\n");
1996 gat_ve_an_toan();
1997 moc = now;
1998 return;
1999 }
2000 
2001 switch (g_gat_buoc)
2002 {
2003 /* ---- 1. SAN SANG: cho GY-33 xac nhan XANH DUONG ---- */
2004 case G_SAN_SANG:
2005 if (g_xanh_lien_tiep >= XAC_NHAN_XANH)
2006 {
2007 g_dem_gat++;
2008 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "\r\n===== PHOI XANH #%lu: xac nhan %u lan lien tiep =====\r\n",
2009 (unsigned long)g_dem_gat, (unsigned)g_xanh_lien_tiep);
2010 uart_in(b);
2011 /* In DU BANG CHUNG da dung de quyet dinh - de khi gat oan con truy
2012 duoc no gat vi con so nao. */
2013 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " b-r=%+d (nen %+d, can >= %+d) b-g=%+d C=%u\r\n",
2014 g_kq.d_br, TCS_LayDbrNen(),
2015 (int)(TCS_LayDbrNen() + BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN),
2016 g_kq.d_bg, (unsigned)g_kq.raw_c);
2017 uart_in(b);
2018 /* Nap tham so cho chu trinh XANH roi dung chung cac trang thai giua.
2019 ★14/08: chu trinh XANH co them chang NANG CANH 90 -> 180 do. */
2020 g_goc_cho = GOC_CHO_XANH_DO;
2021 g_buoc_nang = BUOC_XANH_NANG;
2022 g_goc_dich = GOC_GAT_DO;
2023 g_tre_ms = TRE_GAT_XANH_MS;
2024 g_giu_ms = GIU_XANH_MS;
2025 g_buoc_di = BUOC_XANH_DI;
2026 g_buoc_ve = BUOC_XANH_VE;
2027 g_ten_ct = "XANH";
2028 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), ">> SERVO [XANH]: nang canh %u do -> %u do (buoc %uus)\r\n",
2029 (unsigned)GOC_NGHI_DO, (unsigned)g_goc_cho,
2030 (unsigned)g_buoc_nang);
2031 uart_in(b);
2032 Servo_DatTocDo(g_buoc_nang); /* PHAI dat TRUOC Servo_DatGoc */
2033 Servo_DatGoc(g_goc_cho);
2034 doi_buoc(G_NANG);
2035 moc = HAL_GetTick(); /* lay lai tick: uart_in vua ton thoi gian */
2036 }
2037 /* ---- PHOI DO (= MAU KHAC): bao hieu, KHONG cham vao phoi ----
2038 Xet SAU xanh trong cung mot lan goi, nen mot lan doc da ket luan
2039 xanh thi khong bao gio roi vao day. Chu trinh do khong co tre van
2040 chuyen vi 90 do chi la bao hieu. */
2041 else if ((g_do_lien_tiep >= XAC_NHAN_DO) &&
2042 ((now - g_do_moc) >= TRE_ON_DINH_DO_MS))
2043 {
2044 g_dem_bao_do++;
2045 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "\r\n===== PHOI DO #%lu: %u lan lien tiep, on dinh %lums =====\r\n",
2046 (unsigned long)g_dem_bao_do, (unsigned)g_do_lien_tiep,
2047 (unsigned long)(now - g_do_moc));
2048 uart_in(b);
2049 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " b-r=%+d b-g=%+d C=%u (nguong co vat %u)\r\n",
2050 g_kq.d_br, g_kq.d_bg, (unsigned)g_kq.raw_c,
2051 (unsigned)TCS_LayNguongCoVat());
2052 uart_in(b);
2053 /* ★14/08: chu trinh DO KHONG nang canh. Dat goc cho = GOC_NGHI_DO
2054 thi trang thai G_NANG di qua NGAY (servo dang dung o home nen
2055 Servo_DenNoi() dung ngay lan goi dau), roi g_tre_ms = 0 lam
2056 G_CHO_TROI cung di qua ngay => servo ra 0 do TUC THI, dung y do
2057 "bao hieu dut khoat" cua chu trinh DO. */
2058 g_goc_cho = GOC_NGHI_DO;
2059 g_buoc_nang = BUOC_DO_DI; /* khong dung toi - chang dai 0 do */
2060 g_goc_dich = GOC_BAO_DO;
2061 g_tre_ms = TRE_BAO_DO_MS;
2062 g_giu_ms = GIU_BAO_DO_MS;
2063 g_buoc_di = BUOC_DO_DI;
2064 g_buoc_ve = BUOC_DO_VE;
2065 g_ten_ct = "DO";
2066 Servo_DatTocDo(g_buoc_nang);
2067 Servo_DatGoc(g_goc_cho); /* = home, servo khong di chuyen */
2068 doi_buoc(G_NANG);
2069 moc = HAL_GetTick();
2070 }
2071 break;
2072 
2073 /* ---- 1b. NANG CANH: servo truot HOME -> g_goc_cho (them 14/08/2026) --
2074 XANH: 90 -> 180 do, mat ~400ms.
2075 DO : g_goc_cho = 90 = dung home nen Servo_DenNoi() dung ngay, di
2076 qua trong cung mot lan goi ke tiep - khong ton them thoi gian.
2077 ⚠ Trang thai nay NAM TRONG dien giam sat TIMEOUT_GAT_MS (vi khac
2078 G_SAN_SANG va G_KHOA) nen ket co khi van bat duoc. */
2079 case G_NANG:
2080 if (Servo_DenNoi())
2081 {
2082 if (g_tre_ms > 0u)
2083 {
2084 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), ">> SERVO [%s]: da toi %u do (xung %uus), giu yen %lums cho phoi troi\r\n",
2085 g_ten_ct, (unsigned)Servo_GocDich(),
2086 (unsigned)Servo_XungHienTai(), (unsigned long)g_tre_ms);
2087 uart_in(b);
2088 }
2089 doi_buoc(G_CHO_TROI);
2090 /* ★ MOC 2 GIAY BAT DAU DEM TU DAY - tuc tu luc servo TOI 180 do,
2091 dung nhu nguoi dung da chot (khong tinh tu luc xac nhan mau). */
2092 moc = HAL_GetTick();
2093 }
2094 break;
2095 
2096 /* ---- 2. CHO TROI: servo NAM YEN o g_goc_cho dung g_tre_ms ----
2097 XANH: nam yen o 180 do (goc cho) du 2 giay cho phoi troi toi canh gat.
2098 DO : g_tre_ms = 0 nen di thang qua day ngay lan goi ke tiep.
2099 ⚠ Truoc 14/08/2026 cho nay nam yen o HOME; nay HOME va GOC CHO la hai
2100 goc KHAC NHAU o chu trinh XANH nen phai in g_goc_cho, khong duoc in
2101 GOC_NGHI_DO - in sai la mot lan truy oan nua. */
2102 case G_CHO_TROI:
2103 if ((now - moc) >= g_tre_ms)
2104 {
2105 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), ">> SERVO [%s]: truot %u do -> %u do (buoc %uus)\r\n",
2106 g_ten_ct, (unsigned)g_goc_cho, (unsigned)g_goc_dich,
2107 (unsigned)g_buoc_di);
2108 uart_in(b);
2109 Servo_DatTocDo(g_buoc_di); /* PHAI dat TRUOC Servo_DatGoc */
2110 Servo_DatGoc(g_goc_dich);
2111 doi_buoc(G_DANG_GAT);
2112 moc = HAL_GetTick();
2113 }
2114 break;
2115 
2116 /* ---- 3. Servo dang truot ra goc gat ---- */
2117 case G_DANG_GAT:
2118 if (Servo_DenNoi())
2119 {
2120 /* In XUNG THAT dang phat kem theo goc: neu mot ngay nao do goc va
2121 xung khong khop nhau nua thi nhin la thay ngay, khong phai doan. */
2122 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), ">> SERVO [%s]: da toi %u do (xung %uus), giu yen %lums\r\n",
2123 g_ten_ct, (unsigned)Servo_GocDich(), (unsigned)Servo_XungHienTai(),
2124 (unsigned long)g_giu_ms);
2125 uart_in(b);
2126 doi_buoc(G_GIU);
2127 moc = HAL_GetTick();
2128 }
2129 break;
2130 
2131 /* ---- 4. Giu yen tai goc gat ----
2132 Nghi o day cho servo DUNG HAN roi moi doi chieu. Doi chieu ngay luc
2133 con quan tinh chinh la cai lam servo giat va rut dong dot bien. */
2134 case G_GIU:
2135 if ((now - moc) >= g_giu_ms)
2136 {
2137 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), ">> SERVO [%s]: ve HOME - truot %u do -> %u do (buoc %uus)\r\n",
2138 g_ten_ct, (unsigned)g_goc_dich, (unsigned)GOC_NGHI_DO,
2139 (unsigned)g_buoc_ve);
2140 uart_in(b);
2141 Servo_DatTocDo(g_buoc_ve); /* PHAI dat TRUOC Servo_DatGoc */
2142 Servo_DatGoc(GOC_NGHI_DO);
2143 doi_buoc(G_VE_HOME);
2144 moc = HAL_GetTick();
2145 }
2146 break;
2147 
2148 /* ---- 5. Servo dang truot ve home ---- */
2149 case G_VE_HOME:
2150 if (Servo_DenNoi())
2151 {
2152 /* ★ Chi bat dau dem NGHI_SAU_GAT_MS TU LUC NAY - khong phai tu luc
2153 bat dau gat. Va xoa g_da_roi_xanh de BAT BUOC phai co it nhat mot
2154 lan doc "khac xanh" MOI (sau khi ve home) thi moi vu trang lai. */
2155 g_da_roi_xanh = 0u;
2156 g_xanh_lien_tiep = 0u;
2157 g_trong_lien_tiep = 0u;
2158 g_moc_ve_home = HAL_GetTick();
2159 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), ">> XONG. Da gat: %lu | Mau khac cho qua: %lu | Bo qua: %lu\r\n",
2160 (unsigned long)g_dem_gat, (unsigned long)g_dem_khac,
2161 (unsigned long)g_dem_bo_qua);
2162 uart_in(b);
2163 doi_buoc(G_KHOA);
2164 moc = g_moc_ve_home;
2165 }
2166 break;
2167 
2168 /* ---- 6. KHOA: chong gat lap lai tren CUNG MOT phoi ----
2169 Can DU CA HAI dieu kien moi vu trang lai:
2170 (1) g_da_roi_xanh : GY-33 da doc ra KHAC xanh it nhat mot lan
2171 => phoi that su da troi ra khoi tam nhin
2172 (2) het NGHI_SAU_GAT_MS ke tu luc servo ve toi home
2173 Thieu (1) thi mot phoi xanh dung yen truoc cam bien se bi gat lap di
2174 lap lai. Thieu (2) thi so doc nhap nhay o ranh gioi nguong co the cho
2175 gat hai lan lien nhau tren cung mot phoi. */
2176 default:
2177 {
2178 /* Dem so PHOI bi bo qua, KHONG phai so lan doc. Khong co co chot nay
2179 thi vong lap chay ~6 lan/giay se lam bo dem nhay vo nghia. */
2180 static uint8_t da_dem_bo_qua = 0u;
2181 
2182 if (g_xanh_lien_tiep >= XAC_NHAN_XANH)
2183 {
2184 if (da_dem_bo_qua == 0u)
2185 {
2186 da_dem_bo_qua = 1u;
2187 g_dem_bo_qua++;
2188 uart_in(" [!] Thay XANH nhung dang KHOA -> khong gat.\r\n"
2189 " Neu day la phoi MOI bi bo sot: cac phoi dang di qua\r\n"
2190 " gan nhau hon NGHI_SAU_GAT_MS, hay gian chung ra.\r\n");
2191 }
2192 }
2193 else
2194 {
2195 da_dem_bo_qua = 0u; /* het xanh -> san sang dem phoi ke tiep */
2196 }
2197 
2198 /* g_da_roi_xanh chi bat len khi da doc TRONG du XAC_NHAN_TRONG lan
2199 lien tiep (xem gy33_quet) => bang tai that su da rong tro lai. */
2200 if (g_da_roi_xanh && ((now - g_moc_ve_home) >= NGHI_SAU_GAT_MS))
2201 {
2202 da_dem_bo_qua = 0u;
2203 doi_buoc(G_SAN_SANG);
2204 moc = now;
2205 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " >> Bang tai TRONG (b-r=%+d, nen %+d) - SAN SANG nhan phoi.\r\n",
2206 g_kq.d_br, TCS_LayDbrNen());
2207 uart_in(b);
2208 }
2209 break;
2210 }
2211 }
2212}
2213 
2214/* USER CODE END 0 */
2215 
2216/**
2217 * @brief The application entry point.
2218 * @retval int
2219 */
2220int main(void)
2221{
2222 
2223 /* USER CODE BEGIN 1 */
2224 
2225 /* USER CODE END 1 */
2226 
2227 /* MCU Configuration--------------------------------------------------------*/
2228 
2229 /* Reset of all peripherals, Initializes the Flash interface and the Systick. */
2230 HAL_Init();
2231 
2232 /* USER CODE BEGIN Init */
2233 
2234 /* USER CODE END Init */
2235 
2236 /* Configure the system clock */
2237 SystemClock_Config();
2238 
2239 /* USER CODE BEGIN SysInit */
2240 
2241 /* USER CODE END SysInit */
2242 
2243 /* Initialize all configured peripherals */
2244 MX_GPIO_Init();
2245 MX_I2C2_Init();
2246 MX_TIM3_Init();
2247 MX_TIM4_Init();
2248 MX_USART1_UART_Init();
2249 /* USER CODE BEGIN 2 */
2250 /* ★★★ HAI VIEC NAY PHAI LA VIEC DAU TIEN, TRUOC CA BANNER.
2251 ==========================================================================
2252 LOI DA GAP: Servo_Init nam SAU banner. In banner o 115200 ton ~95ms, cong
2253 HAL_Delay(50) cua relay nua => trong ~150ms dau tien chan PA6 KHONG PHAT
2254 XUNG PWM nao ca. Servo RC khong co xung thi khong giu vi tri: no tha troi
2255 hoac giu nguyen cho cu tu lan chay truoc, roi moi GIAT ve home khi xung
2256 bat dau co. Nhin ra ngoai dung la "servo quay loan luc cap nguon".
2257 
2258 Dat Servo_Init NGAY DAY thi xung 1500us (=90 do, home moi tu 14/08/2026)
2259 duoc phat ra tu nhip
2260 dau tien sau khi ngoai vi san sang - servo di THANG ve home, khong quat
2261 sang cho khac. Servo_Init cung co y nap do rong xung TRUOC khi bat PWM
2262 (xem servo_mg996r.c) nen xung dau tien da dung goc roi.
2263 
2264 MOTOR_TAT cung dua len day vi ly do tuong tu: cat relay som nhat co the,
2265 khong de bang tai chay mot nhip nao truoc khi firmware kip kiem soat. */
2266 Servo_Init(&htim3, TIM_CHANNEL_1, GOC_NGHI_DO);
2267 MOTOR_TAT();
2268 g_motor_chay = 0u;
2269 
2270 /* ★★★ KHOI HIEN THI SO DEM - 2 IC 74HC595 noi tiep, 2 LED 7 doan 1 chu so.
2271 ==========================================================================
2272 Dat NGAY SAU Servo_Init/MOTOR_TAT va TRUOC banner, vi hai ly do:
2273 1) Hai den phai hien "0" ngay tu dau. Neu de sau phan do cam bien (co
2274 the ton toi 3 x 2s khi cam bien chua cam day) thi trong suot thoi
2275 gian do hai den van con giu noi dung RAC cua lan chay truoc - nhin
2276 ra ngoai giong het nhu he bi treo.
2277 2) LED595_Init() chi cham vao PB12/PB13/PB15 va SPI2 - ba chan va mot
2278 ngoai vi ma KHONG mot khoi nao khac trong du an nay dung toi. Dat o
2279 dau hay cuoi deu khong anh huong gi, nen chon cho an toan nhat.
2280 ⚠ PHAI goi SAU MX_GPIO_Init() (da chay o tren) vi ham nay ghi vao
2281 GPIOB->CRH; goi truoc thi MX_GPIO_Init se de len mat.

2483 /* ★★★★ SUA 14/08/2026 - KHONG dung TCS_DoNhayToiDa() nua.
2484 LOI DA GAP (am tham, phat hien khi doc lai code 14/08/2026):
2485 TCS_DoNhayToiDa() ep CUNG gain = 60x bang so 3 viet thang trong ham,
2486 BO QUA TCS_GAIN_MAC_DINH. Nghia la du header ghi "gain mac dinh = 1x
2487 (RESET 03/08/2026)" thi may van chay 60x - hai con so noi hai dang.
2488 Bat cu ai sua TCS_GAIN_MAC_DINH cung se thay "sua xong khong co tac
2489 dung gi", dung kieu bay da tung mat thoi gian truy o GOC_NGHI_DO.
2490 
2491 NAY nap DUNG hai hang so mac dinh trong tcs34725.h:
2492 gain = TCS_GAIN_MAC_DINH = 0 -> 1x (mac dinh cua chip)
2493 ATIME = TCS_ATIME_MAC_DINH = 0xFF -> 2.4ms, tran dem 1024
2494 Nguoi dung chot: "cu dua ve mac dinh roi hieu chinh len tu tu".
2495 ⚠ DU DOAN PHAI KIEM CHUNG BANG SO DO: o 1x + 2.4ms VA co man che, C
2496 nhieu kha nang chi con vai chuc don vi - qua nho, van mau se rat nhieu.
2497 DAU HIEU: cot C duoi ~50, va b-r/b-g nhay lung tung +-10 giua cac dong
2498 lien tiep du khong dong vao gi. Luc do nang len theo THU TU NAY:
2499 1) ATIME 0xFF -> 0xD5 (103ms, tran 44032) <== nang cai nay TRUOC
2500 2) gain 1x -> 4x -> 16x <== roi moi toi gain
2501 Nang ATIME truoc vi no tang tin hieu ma KHONG khuech dai nhieu; tang
2502 gain thi khuech dai ca hai. Doi bang cach sua TCS_ATIME_MAC_DINH /
2503 TCS_GAIN_MAC_DINH trong tcs34725.h roi nap lai.
2504 ★ DA KIEM CHUNG 14/08/2026: du doan tren DUNG. O 1x+2.4ms co man che
2505 doc duoc C = 2 (tran 1024). Da nang len 60x + 0xC0 (154ms) => C = 7568,
2506 dung bang 2 x 64 x 60 = 7680 nhu tinh toan. Hai hang so trong
2507 tcs34725.h gio la 3 (60x) va 0xC0. */
2508 (void)TCS_DatGain(TCS_GAIN_MAC_DINH);
2509 (void)TCS_DatTichPhan(TCS_ATIME_MAC_DINH);
2510 TCS_ChongBaoHoa();

2717

2720 break;

2726 break;

2790 tong_bg += k.d_bg;

2917 case 'j': case 'J':
2918 {

2921 va nhin cot %CAO. Chan nao %CAO nhay len khi motor chay = chan do
2922 dang bi nhieu. Day la so lieu de quyet dinh sua phan cung o dau.
2923 ⚠ nut_quet() VAN duoc goi trong vong do nay nen nut START/STOP
2924 that su an duoc trong luc dang do. */
2925 uint32_t n = 0, cao_ir = 0, cao_start = 0, cao_stop = 0;
2926 uint32_t t_in = HAL_GetTick();
2927 char b[190];
2928 
2929 uart_in("\r\n>> DO NHIEU CHAN DAU VAO. AN NUT START VAT LY de bat\r\n"

2931 uart_in(" PA0 IR: binh thuong CAO (khong vat). PA1/PA2 nut: binh\r\n"

2935 {
2936 if (uart_doc_lenh() != 0u) { break; }
2937 
2938 if (HAL_GPIO_ReadPin(IR_E18_GPIO_Port, IR_E18_Pin) == GPIO_PIN_SET) { cao_ir++; }
2939 if (HAL_GPIO_ReadPin(BTN_START_GPIO_Port, BTN_START_Pin) == GPIO_PIN_SET) { cao_start++; }
2940 if (HAL_GPIO_ReadPin(BTN_STOP_GPIO_Port, BTN_STOP_Pin) == GPIO_PIN_SET) { cao_stop++; }
2941 n++;
2942 
2943 nut_quet(HAL_GetTick()); /* nut vat ly VAN song trong luc do */
2944 Servo_Task();
2945 
2946 if ((HAL_GetTick() - t_in) >= 500u) /* in moi 0.5 giay */
2947 {
2948 t_in = HAL_GetTick();
2949 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " %5lu mau | PA0 IR:%3lu%% | PA1 START:%3lu%% | PA2 STOP:%3lu%% | motor:%s\r\n",
2950 (unsigned long)n,
2951 (unsigned long)((cao_ir * 100u) / n),
2952 (unsigned long)((cao_start * 100u) / n),
2953 (unsigned long)((cao_stop * 100u) / n),
2954 g_motor_chay ? "CHAY" : "dung");
2955 uart_in(b);
2956 n = 0; cao_ir = 0; cao_start = 0; cao_stop = 0;
2957 }

2965 case 'w': case 'W':

2973 Tu do biet phai dat BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN bao nhieu la du. */

2976 int16_t bg_min = 32767, bg_max = -32768;

2990 nen, nbr, (int)(nen + BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN));

2999 n++;
3000 if (k.d_br < br_min) { br_min = k.d_br; }
3001 if (k.d_br > br_max) { br_max = k.d_br; }
3002 if (k.d_bg < bg_min) { bg_min = k.d_bg; }
3003 if (k.d_bg > bg_max) { bg_max = k.d_bg; }
3004 if (k.d_br >= nbr) { cham_driver++; }
3005 if (k.d_br >= (int16_t)(nen + BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN)) { cham_bien++; }
3006 if (k.mau == TCS_TRONG) { so_trong++; }
3007 else if (k.mau == TCS_XANH_DUONG) { so_xanh++; }
3008 else { so_khac++; }
3009 }
3010

3012 {

3015 gat_vu_trang();
3016 break;
3017 }

3038 (unsigned)cham_bien);
3039 uart_in(b);
3040 snprintf(b, sizeof(b), " Phai TANG BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN len it nhat %d (dang %d).\r\n",
3041 (int)(br_max - nen + 2), (int)BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN);
3042 uart_in(b);

3214 {

3234 
3235 /* ★★ KHOI A - GY-33 QUET LIEN TUC, VO DIEU KIEN, MOI VONG LAP.
3236 Day la NGUON KICH DUY NHAT cua co cau gat. Khong phu thuoc nut nhan,
3237 khong phu thuoc motor, khong phu thuoc E18.
3238 Mot lan doc ton ~154ms (mot chu ky tich phan); trong suot thoi gian do
3239 driver goi phuc_vu_trong_khi_do() lien tuc nen servo van truot deu,
3240 encoder van dem, va nut START/STOP van an lien tay. */
3241 if (gy33_quet() == 0u)
3242 {
3243 /* ★★ HAN CHE TAN SUAT IN - SUA 31/07/2026.
3244 ⚠ LOI LO RA SAU KHI BO VONG CHO CHAN: truoc day cam bien loi thi
3245 firmware ket o buoc khoi dong, khong bao gio toi day. Nay he VAN
3246 chay tiep, nen nhanh nay chay MOI VONG LAP khi cam bien mat lien
3247 lac. In day du moi vong se:
3248 - do chu lien tuc, terminal khong doc noi - dung luc can no nhat
3249 de chan doan;
3250 - moi lan in ~130 ky tu o 115200 chan ~11ms, lam cham han vong
3251 lap va do dai chu ky quet nut / IR / servo.
3252 Chi in LAN DAU va sau do moi MAT_LIEN_LAC_IN_MS mot lan. */
3253 uint32_t nay = HAL_GetTick();
3254 if ((g_mat_lien_lac == 0u) ||
3255 ((nay - g_moc_in_mat) >= MAT_LIEN_LAC_IN_MS))
3256 {
3257 g_mat_lien_lac = 1u;
3258 g_moc_in_mat = nay;
3259 uart_in("!! MAT LIEN LAC I2C voi GY-33 - driver da tu cuu bus nhung van\r\n"
3260 " khong doc duoc. Go 'p' de quet bus, 'x' de cuu bus bang tay.\r\n");
3261 }
3262 }
3263 else
3264 {
3265 /* Bao PHUC HOI - de nguoi van hanh biet luc nao cam lai day dat. */
3266 if (g_mat_lien_lac)
3267 {
3268 g_mat_lien_lac = 0u;
3269 uart_in(">> DA CO LAI LIEN LAC voi cam bien mau - he tiep tuc binh thuong.\r\n");
3270 }
3271 if (g_dang_quet)
3272 {
3273 in_dong_quet(); /* che do xem so lieu lien tuc ('q') */
3274 }
3275#if (CHE_DO_DO_MAU != 0u)
3276 /* ⚠ Phai goi HAL_GetTick() o day: bien 'nay' thuoc nhanh IF ben tren
3277 (nhanh mat lien lac), khong nhin thay duoc tu nhanh ELSE nay. */
3278 do_mau_in(HAL_GetTick()); /* ★14/08: che do DO MAU, co tiet che */
3279#endif
3280 /* ★14/08 lan 5: chinh lai moc nen theo dieu kien LAM VIEC THAT (motor
3281 dang chay). Dat o day vi can g_kq vua duoc gy33_quet() cap nhat. */
3282 hoc_lai_nen_khi_chay();
3283 }
3284 
3285 /* ★★ KHOI B - co cau gat. Dau vao DUY NHAT la ket qua cua khoi A. */
3286 gat_task();
3287 
3288 /* ★★ KHOI C - HIEN THI SO DEM ra 2 LED 7 doan qua 2 IC 74HC595.
3289 ==========================================================================
3290 HAI BO DEM DOC LAP, KHONG phai mot so hai chu so:
3291 den board #1 = g_dem_bao_do (so phoi DO da bao hieu)
3292 den board #2 = g_dem_gat (so phoi XANH DUONG da gat)
3293 Moi den chay 0 -> 9 roi QUAY VE 0. Khong co nho (carry) sang den kia:
3294 phoi do thu 10 di qua thi den do ve 0 con den xanh DUNG YEN. Vi vay o
3295 day chi co phep chia lay DU (% 10) - nam ben trong LED595_DatSo() -
3296 va tuyet doi khong co phep / 10 nao de tinh hang chuc.
3297 
3298 ⚠ TAI SAO GOI O DAY chu khong nhet vao trong gat_task():
3299 gat_task() la may trang thai da duoc soi ky nhieu lan, moi dong deu co
3300 ly do. Chen them viec hien thi vao giua cac nhanh chi lam no kho doc va
3301 de gay hoi quy. Dat o day thi viec hien thi la mot khoi DOC LAP: no chi
3302 DOC hai bien dem, khong ghi vao bat cu bien trang thai nao cua khoi gat.
3303 Vong lap chinh quay ~5-6 lan/giay nen do tre toi da ~180ms - mat thuong
3304 khong phan biet duoc, ma phoi thi con dang troi tren bang tai.
3305 
3306 LED595_DatSo() tu so sanh voi gia tri dang hien va chi day ra SPI khi
3307 that su doi, nen goi moi vong lap khong ton gi.
3308 LED595_Task() day lai toan bo noi dung moi 500ms. Day la BAO HIEM cho
3309 viec 74HC595 chay 5V nhung nhan tin hieu 3.3V (V_IH = 3.5V - xem khung
3310 canh bao trong tai lieu dau day): neu mot xung bi nhieu lam sai ghi
3311 dich thi cham nhat sau 500ms den tu hien lai dung, khong phai reset. */
3312 LED595_DatSo((uint8_t)(g_dem_bao_do % 10u), (uint8_t)(g_dem_gat % 10u));
3313 LED595_Task(HAL_GetTick());
3314 
3315 /* In cac thong bao IR / nut nhan da xep hang trong luc cho I2C.
3316 PHAI o vong lap chinh: uart_in chan toi 200ms, goi trong vong cho se
3317 lam het han AVALID cua cam bien. */
3318 xa_su_kien();
3319 
3320#if (CHE_DO_BANG_LOG != 0u)
3321 /* ★★ KHOI D - BANG LOG TONG HOP (them 14/08/2026).
3322 Dat o day, SAU xa_su_kien(), vi hai ly do:
3323 1) Ham nay in UART nen phai nam o vong lap chinh, tuyet doi khong
3324 duoc goi tu phuc_vu_trong_khi_do() (se lam het han AVALID).
3325 2) SAU xa_su_kien() thi cac dong su kien (PHOI XANH, nut START/STOP)
3326 duoc in TRUOC dong so lieu cua cung nhip => doc log theo dung thu
3327 tu nhan qua: thay dong ">>> PHOI XANH" roi moi thay dong co
3328 buoc = DANGGAT.
3329 Ban than phep DEM xung van chay trong vong cho I2C (encoder_quet o
3330 cuoi vong lap) - khoi nay chi lay ket qua ra man hinh, khong dem. */
3331 bang_log_in(HAL_GetTick());
3332#endif
3333 
3334 led_trang_thai(HAL_GetTick()); /* thay cho nhip tim - xem quy uoc o ham */
3335 
3336 /* Cua so phuc vu 20ms. Binh thuong gy33_quet() phia tren da phuc vu du
3337 roi, nhung khi bus I2C loi no tra ve NGAY LAP TUC - luc do vong lap
3338 se quay rat nhanh va day moi la cho duy nhat con lai bao dam nut nhan,
3339 IR, encoder va servo van duoc goi deu dan. Khong dung HAL_Delay. */
3340 {
3341 uint32_t t0 = HAL_GetTick();
3342 while ((HAL_GetTick() - t0) < 20u)
3343 {
3344 uint32_t t = HAL_GetTick();
3345 ir_quet(t);
3346 nut_quet(t);
3347 encoder_quet(t);
3348 Servo_Task();
3349 }
3350 }
3351 }
3352 /* USER CODE END 3 */
3353}

Colour driver interface, thresholds and calibration constants

1/**
2 ******************************************************************************
3 * @file tcs34725.h
4 * @brief Driver cam bien mau TCS34725 (I2C) cho STM32F103C8T6
5 * -> BAN "CHI NHAN XANH DUONG" (blue = co tin hieu)
6 *
7 * KHAC HAN TCS3200: TCS34725 la cam bien SO. Ben trong co 4 bo do (Clear,
8 * Red, Green, Blue), moi bo la mot BO DEM 16-BIT dem so photon trong mot
9 * KHOANG TICH PHAN. Het khoang tich phan, ca 4 kenh cung chot gia tri MOT
10 * LUC roi nam trong thanh ghi, MCU chi viec doc ra qua I2C.
11 *
12 * ====================================================================
13 * NGUYEN LY PHAN LOAI (ban nay) - QUAN TRONG:
14 * ====================================================================
15 * CHI CO 3 KET LUAN:
16 * TCS_TRONG - khong co vat truoc cam bien (kenh CLEAR duoi san toi)
17 * TCS_XANH_DUONG - co vat va vat mau XANH DUONG => RA TIN HIEU
18 * TCS_MAU_KHAC - co vat nhung KHONG phai xanh duong => phan loai ra
19 * (them TCS_KHONG_RO khi bao hoa - so lieu khong tin duoc)
20 *
21 * Cach nhan xanh duong: chuan hoa 3 kenh ve TONG = 255 (bo do sang di, chi
22 * con THANH PHAN MAU => KHONG phu thuoc khoang cach), roi doi HAI dieu kien:
23 * d_br = b - r >= nguong_br (xanh duong hut do rat manh)
24 * d_bg = b - g >= nguong_bg (b phai la kenh LON NHAT, tach voi
25 * nen xanh la / vat xanh la)
26 * Ca hai cung dung => XANH DUONG. Thieu mot => MAU KHAC.
27 * Vi sao 2 dieu kien: neu chi xet b-r thi vat XANH LA cung cho b-r duong nhe
28 * va se bi bao nham thanh xanh duong. Dieu kien b-g chan viec do.
29 *
30 * Vi du van mau sau chuan hoa (r+g+b = 255):
31 * Xanh duong dam : r= 57 g= 78 b=120 -> d_br=+63 d_bg=+42 => XANH
32 * Xanh duong nhat: r= 73 g= 82 b=100 -> d_br=+27 d_bg=+18 => XANH
33 * Nen xanh la : r= 83 g= 84 b= 88 -> d_br= +5 d_bg= +4 => KHAC
34 * Vang : r=110 g= 95 b= 50 -> d_br=-60 => KHAC
35 * Do : r=140 g= 60 b= 55 -> d_br=-85 => KHAC
36 * Trang : r= 85 g= 85 b= 85 -> d_br= 0 => KHAC
37 *
38 * ====================================================================
39 * LAP DAT: CAM BIEN DAT NGANG, VAT CACH 10-15 cm
40 * ====================================================================
41 * Day la khoang cach RAT XA voi module GY-33 (chi co 4 den LED trang nho).
42 * Anh sang phan xa ve giam theo ~1/d^2: o 12cm chi con vai % so voi 2cm.
43 * Vi vay ban nay dat mac dinh o DO NHAY TOI DA:
44 * GAIN = 60x (muc cao nhat cua chip)
45 * ATIME = 0xC0 = 154 ms (dai nhat thong dung, tran dem 65535)
46 * Doi lai moi phep do mat ~154ms x TCS_SO_MAU_LOC (~0.5 s) - chap nhan duoc
47 * cho chuong trinh TEST. Khi ghep vao bang tai chay that, neu phoi di nhanh
48 * thi ha ATIME xuong 0xD5 (103ms) va giam TCS_SO_MAU_LOC ve 1.
49 *
50 * ⚠ HAI HAN CHE VAT LY o 10-15cm - phai biet truoc:
51 * 1) Vat mau TOI / DEN phan xa rat it, kenh CLEAR co the khong vuot noi
52 * san toi => bi coi la "TRONG". Cach chua: ha san toi (lenh 'b' do lai
53 * luc trong, hoac lenh 's' dat tay), hoac gan them 1 LED trang roi
54 * thang vao vat.
55 * 2) Anh sang phong (den tuyp / den tran) chieu vao vat cung bi tinh vao.
56 * Neu anh sang phong doi nhieu theo gio, do lai nen bang 'b' va hoc lai
57 * mau bang 'c'.
58 *
59 * DAU DAY - MODULE GY-33 ("TCS34725 I2C UART") cua ban co cac chan:
60 * VCC, GND, DR, CT, SCL, SDA, INT, NC, S0, S1, G (KHONG co chan LED)
61 *
62 * VCC -> 3.3V
63 * GND -> GND chung (chan 'G' con lai cung la GND, dau them cho chac)
64 * SCL -> PB10 (I2C2_SCL)
65 * SDA -> PB11 (I2C2_SDA)
66 * DR / CT -> DE TRONG (I2C cua MCU phu tren module, dia chi 0x5A)
67 * S0 / S1 -> DE TRONG (noi GND se bat mode MCU_IIC -> MCU phu tranh bus)
68 * INT / NC -> DE TRONG
69 * Module KHONG co chan LED: 4 den trang tren module SANG THUONG TRUC khi
70 * co dien 3.3V. Firmware KHONG dieu khien den (khong con chan/ lenh nao).
71 * Dia chi I2C: 0x29 (7-bit) = 0x52/0x53 khi tinh ca bit R/W.
72 *
73 * ⚠ GY-33 CO HAI DUONG I2C RIENG - phai cam dung cap chan:
74 * SCL/SDA -> noi THANG vao chip TCS34725, dia chi 0x29 <== DUNG CAI NAY
75 * CT/DR -> noi vao MCU PHU tren module, dia chi 0x5A <== KHONG dung
76 * Go lenh 'p' de quet bus: phai thay 0x29. Neu thay 0x5A nghia la dang
77 * cam nham sang CT/DR.
78 ******************************************************************************
79 */
80

82#define __TCS34725_H

106/* THOI GIAN TICH PHAN: t = (256 - ATIME) x 2.4ms, tran dem = (256-ATIME)x1024
107 (chan tren 65535). Cang dai cang NHAY -> nhin duoc CANG XA.
108 0xFF = 2.4ms tran 1024
109 0xF6 = 24 ms tran 10240
110 0xEB = 50 ms tran 21504
111 0xD5 = 103 ms tran 44032
112 0xC0 = 154 ms tran 65535 <== MAC DINH: nhay nhat, cho khoang 10-15cm */
113/* ★★★★ SUA 14/08/2026: 0xFF -> 0xC0. LAM THEO SO DO THAT, khong phong doan.
114 SO DO tren rig SAU khi lap man che, o 1x + 0xFF (2.4ms), bang tai TRONG:
115 C = 2 (tran dem cua 2.4ms la 1024) => 0.2% thang do
116 Cung cau hinh do, phien 03/08/2026 KHI CHUA CO MAN CHE doc duoc C = 528.
117 Tuc anh sang toi cam bien da TUT 264 LAN. Voi C = 2 thi khong con phep do
118 nao co nghia: mot don vi dem thay doi lam van mau lat hoan toan.
119 0xC0 = 154ms = 64 lan thoi gian tich phan cu, tran dem 65535.
120 Du kien: C ~ 2 x 64 x 60(gain) = ~7700, tuc ~12% thang do - vung lanh manh.
121 ⚠ Nang ATIME lam moi phep do ton 154ms thay vi 2.4ms. Voi TCS_SO_MAU_LOC=1
122 thi mot lan doc ~160ms, phoi phai nam trong tam nhin >= ~350ms de dat
123 XAC_NHAN_XANH=2. Bang tai chay nhanh thi ha ve 0xD5 (103ms). */
124#define TCS_ATIME_MAC_DINH 0xC0u /* SUA 14/08/2026: 154ms (cu 0xFF=2.4ms) */
125 
126/* DO KHUECH DAI (AGAIN): 0=1x 1=4x 2=16x 3=60x.
127 Vat cach 10-15cm + chi co den module => PHAI dung 60x (muc 3).
128 Neu sau nay ha cam bien xuong con 2-3cm thi ha ve 16x (muc 2) cho khoi
129 bao hoa. Lenh 'a' doi duoc ngay khi dang chay. */
130/* ★★★★ SUA 14/08/2026: 0 (1x) -> 3 (60x). Cung mot ly do voi ATIME o tren:
131 man che giay cat gan het anh sang, C do duoc chi con 2/1024.
132 Nguoi dung da chot "dua ve mac dinh roi hieu chinh len tu tu" - day CHINH
133 LA buoc hieu chinh len, va so do noi ro phai len HET NAC chu khong tung
134 buoc: tu C=2 len muc dung duoc can nhan them ~3800 lan, ma ATIME chi cho
135 64 lan, nen bat buoc phai lay them 60 lan cua gain.
136 ⚠ AN TOAN VE BAO HOA: main.c goi TCS_ChongBaoHoa() ngay sau khi nap hai so
137 nay - ham do TU HA gain neu cham tran. Nen dat 60x khong the ket o trang
138 thai bao hoa; neu man che bi thao ra thi gain se tu tut ve muc hop ly. */
139#define TCS_GAIN_MAC_DINH 3u /* SUA 14/08/2026: 60x (cu 0 = 1x) */
140 
141/* SAN TOI: kenh CLEAR duoi muc nay => KHONG co vat truoc cam bien (cam bien
142 dat ngang, phia sau la khoang trong / toi).
143 Gia tri nay PHU THUOC anh sang phong va phai do lai tai cho: go lenh 'b'
144 luc bang tai TRONG, firmware se tu tinh va ghi de.

146#define TCS_SAN_TOI_MAC_DINH 0u /* RESET 03/08/2026: cu 300. 0 = khong co san toi,

150/* He so an toan khi HOC san toi bang lenh 'b':

152 Phai cao hon muc trong mot chut de nhieu khong lam bao "co vat" oan,
153 nhung dung cao qua keo theo vat mau toi bi bo sot. */
154#define TCS_HS_SAN_TOI 160u /* % cua muc do duoc luc trong */
155#define TCS_CONG_SAN_TOI 80u /* cong them mot luong tuyet doi */

157/* ★★★★ NHAN "CO VAT" BANG DO SANG - DAO CHIEU (them 03/08/2026, ban V2_1) ★★★★
158 ==========================================================================
159 VI SAO PHAI DAO CHIEU:
160 Code goc gia dinh vat SANG hon nen (`C < san_toi` => khong co vat). Rig nay
161 NGUOC LAI: cam bien nhin vao nen SANG, phoi CHE bot no di nen vat TOI hon.
162 So do that:
163 NEN trong : C = 528 / 566 (va 313 khi anh sang phong khac)
164 PHOI XANH : C = 190
165 PHOI DO : C = 155 / 224..236
166 
167 VI SAO KHONG DUNG TRUC MAU DUOC NUA (ly do bat buoc phai doi):
168 Vi phoi toi hon nen, van mau chuan hoa cua phoi bi nen ap dao => phoi do
169 ra r=88 g=87 b=78, gan nhu khong con dac trung mau. Do that:
170 NEN : b-r = -9 .. -10 b-g = -11/-12
171 PHOI DO: b-r = -10 .. -6 b-g = -9/-10
172 HAI CUM CHONG NHAU tren b-r (deu cham -10). KHONG mot nguong nao tren truc
173 mau tach duoc chung. Da thu siet cua so nen tu bam 10->4 va bien nen 4->2,
174 VAN THAT BAI: phoi do keo moc nen tu -9 sang -6 roi tu bien thanh "TRONG".
175 
176 Trong khi do C tach bach tuyet doi: co vat 155..236, nen 313..567.
177 
178 VI SAO DUNG TI LE CHU KHONG DUNG SO TUYET DOI:
179 Anh sang phong CO DOI (do duoc nen 313 va 566 o hai thoi diem). Nguong
180 tuyet doi se sai ngay khi den doi. Nen lay theo TI LE cua muc nen dang
181 theo doi:
182 co_vat khi C < C_nen * TCS_PT_CO_VAT / 100
183 Ti le do duoc cua vat/nen: 155/528 = 0.29 | 190/528 = 0.36 | 235/566 = 0.42
184 Dat 70% => cach vat (42%) 28 diem va cach nen (100%) 30 diem. Can hai phia.
185 
186 ★★ SUA 04/08/2026: 70 -> 50, theo yeu cau "tang nguong khong khi len".
187 LY DO: khi BANG TAI CHAY THAT, mat bang rung + moi noi + vet ban lam C tut
188 tung luc. Voi 70%, nen chi can tut 30% (599 -> 418) la bao "co vat" oan =>
189 sau 400ms on dinh la chu trinh DO kich => servo quet oan lien tuc.
190 Voi 50% (nen 599 -> nguong 300): nen phai tut MOT NUA do sang moi bi nham.
191 So do that luc dat: nen 599 | phoi do 177 | phoi xanh 190
192 nguong 300 cach nen 299 don vi, cach phoi 110-123 don vi. */
193/* ★★★★ RESET 14/08/2026: 50 -> 70 (tra ve gia tri THIET KE GOC).
194 Ly do: nguoi dung vua lam MAN CHE ANH SANG bang giay cho cam bien. Con so

196 Co man che thi muc nen doi hoan toan, moi so hieu chinh cu deu vo nghia.
197 70 la diem xuat phat trung tinh de DO LAI, khong phai gia tri cuoi cung. */
198/* ★★★★★ SUA 14/08/2026 (lan 2): 70 -> 85. BAKE TU SO DO THAT sau khi THAO

200 NEN trong : C = 14480

202 PHOI DO : C = 8865 (do luc phoi SAT cam bien - xem canh bao duoi)
203 
204 ⚠⚠ VI SAO PHAI DOI GAP: voi 70% thi nguong = 14480 x 0.70 = 10136, ma phoi
205 XANH nam o 10050 - chi cach nguong 86 DON VI, tuc 0.85%. Muc nen troi
206 xuong duoi 14357 (chi can -0.9%) la nguong tut xuong duoi 10050 va PHOI
207 XANH BI KET LUAN LA "TRONG" => BO SOT HOAN TOAN, servo khong gat gi ca.
208 Day dung la trieu chung "phoi xanh chay qua ma log im lang" da canh bao o
209 khoi TCS_PT_CO_VAT_SANG ben duoi.

218 + vach ngan) thi PHAI DO LAI ca ba muc va tinh lai - man che cu lam ba cum
219 dinh chum vao nhau hoan toan. */
220#define TCS_PT_CO_VAT 85u /* % cua C nen; duoi muc nay = CO VAT */

222/* ★★★★★ CONG TAC DAO CHIEU SANG/TOI - THEM 14/08/2026 ★★★★★
223 ==========================================================================
224 VI SAO PHAI CO CONG TAC NAY:
225 Buoc "co vat hay khong" cua ban V2_1 dua HOAN TOAN vao DO SANG, va no gia
226 dinh mot chieu co dinh: NEN SANG (anh sang phong roi vao cam bien), PHOI
227 CHE bot nen di nen PHOI TOI HON. Do la su that cua rig KHI CHUA CO MAN CHE:
228 NEN 528..599 | PHOI XANH 190 | PHOI DO 155..236
229 
230 MAN CHE GIAY (lam ngay 14/08/2026) CAT DUONG ANH SANG PHONG. Sau khi che,
231 nguon sang gan nhu duy nhat con lai la 4 den LED trang tren module GY-33.
232 Luc do RAT CO THE quan he lat nguoc: bang tai (nen) o xa den nen TOI, con
233 phoi nam gan hon va phan xa den LED ve nen SANG HON NEN.
234 
235 ⚠ NEU LAT MA CODE VAN GIU CHIEU CU thi trieu chung se la: cam bien bao
236 TRONG VINH VIEN, khong bao gio nhan ra phoi nao ca, log im lang hoan toan
237 (khong co ca dong PHOI XANH lan PHOI DO). KHONG phai loi nguong mau.
238 
239 CACH DUNG:
240 1 = VAT TOI HON NEN (chieu cu, chua co man che) -> co vat khi C THAP
241 0 = VAT SANG HON NEN (chieu moi, co the sau khi che) -> co vat khi C CAO
242 
243 ⚠ SAU KHI DO XONG chi can doi DUNG MOT DONG nay. Ba cho phu thuoc no

249 C_phoi < C_nen -> de 1
250 C_phoi > C_nen -> de 0 */
251#define TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN 1u

253/* ★★★★★ CONG TAC CHON TRUC NHAN DANG - THEM 14/08/2026 (lan 3) ★★★★★

255 Buoc "co vat hay khong" co HAI cach lam, va rig nay da phai doi qua doi lai
256 giua chung theo dieu kien chieu sang. Day la cong tac chon.

258 1 = nhan bang DO SANG (kenh C so voi muc nen dang bam)
259 0 = nhan bang VAN MAU (b-r, b-g co bam sat diem nen khong)

262 03/08/2026: dat = DO SANG. Luc do KHONG CO man che, nen la anh sang phong

268 14/08/2026: dat = VAN MAU. Nguoi dung lap MAN CHE moi (giay, co vach ngan).
269 SO DO THAT sau khi lap:
270 truc DO SANG: NEN C=7380 | DO C=7303 (-1.0%) | XANH C=6930 (-6.1%)
271 -> PHOI DO chi toi hon nen 1.0%. KHONG nguong nao tach duoc.
272 Them nua, moc nen tu bam da bi ket o C=10953 (nguong 9310)

274 "co vat". Bo bam ha 1 don vi moi 16 lan doc nen phai mat hon

286#define TCS_NHAN_VAT_BANG_DO_SANG 0u

288/* Nguong CO VAT cho CHIEU 0 (vat SANG hon nen): co vat khi
289 C > C_nen * TCS_PT_CO_VAT_SANG / 100
290 Phai LON HON 100 (vat sang hon nen bao nhieu % moi tinh la co vat).
291 200 = vat phai sang GAP DOI nen. Do that xong thi dat vao khoang giua
292 C_nen va C_phoi giong het cach da lam voi TCS_PT_CO_VAT o chieu 1.
293 ⚠ Chi co tac dung khi TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN = 0. */
294#define TCS_PT_CO_VAT_SANG 200u

296/* ⚠⚠ GIOI HAN DA BIET CUA CON SO 70 - DOC TRUOC KHI GO LOI "BO SOT PHOI XANH":
297 Ti le vat/nen KHONG on dinh theo anh sang, vi hai nguon sang khac nhau:
298 VAT duoc chieu bang LED CUA MODULE -> gan nhu co dinh
299 NEN la anh sang PHONG -> thay doi
300 Den phong toi di thi NEN tut manh, VAT gan nhu dung yen => ti le trach LEN.
301 Do that cua cung mot phoi xanh o hai phien:
302 nen 528, xanh 190 -> ti le 0.36 (den sang)
303 nen 272, xanh 170 -> ti le 0.625 (den toi hon)
304 ★★★ VOI NGUONG 50% (dat tu 04/08/2026) RANH GIOI NAY CHAT HON HAN:
305 Phoi xanh bi bo sot khi C_xanh >= 50% x C_nen, tuc khi C_nen <= 380.
306 Muc den luc do ban vao: C_nen = 599 -> con bien 219 don vi (37%)
307 Muc den phien truoc : C_nen = 272 -> DA DUOI 380 => BO SOT HOAN TOAN
308 Nghia la: ban 50% CHI DUNG o muc den hien tai. Neu phong toi lai ve muc cu
309 (nen ~272, phoi xanh ~170 -> ti le 0.625) thi phoi xanh KHONG BAO GIO duoc
310 nhan nua - khong phai gat oan, ma la KHONG GAT GI CA.
311 ⚠ DAU HIEU NHAN RA: phoi xanh chay qua ma log im lang hoan toan (khong co
312 ca dong PHOI XANH lan PHOI DO). Luc do go 'd' o ban V2_1 doc C cua nen:
313 neu C_nen tut duoi 380 thi phai NANG TCS_PT_CO_VAT len lai, hoac lam den
314 chieu on dinh hon.
315 (Ranh gioi cu voi nguong 70%: bo sot khi C_nen <= 271 - rong rai hon nhieu,
316 doi lai la de bi gat oan khi bang tai chay.)
317 Nghiem thu 03/08/2026 o muc den luc do: nen C=271..273 (du 80 don vi tren
318 nguong 191), phoi xanh C=169..171 (du 20), phoi do C=154..155 (du 37).
319 Ca ba deu dung, nhung bien cua PHOI XANH la mong nhat.
320 
321 ⚠ TRIEU CHUNG khi con so nay khong con du: phoi xanh bi doc thanh
322 "NEN/TRONG" (khong phai "MAU KHAC") vi C cua no da vuot nguong co vat.
323 CACH CHUA: nang so nay len 80 (da tinh: bien phoi xanh 20 -> 47, chiu duoc
324 den toi them 22%; bien nen 80 -> 53, van rat rong vi nen dao dong chi +-1).
325 ⚠ DA DE XUAT NANG LEN 80 NGAY 03/08/2026 - user chon GIU 70 vi dang chay
326 dung. Khong tu y doi, nhung day la cho can sua DAU TIEN neu bo sot phoi. */

328/* Muc C cua NEN luc khoi dong. Chi la DIEM XUAT PHAT - bo theo doi ben duoi
329 se tu keo ve muc that sau vai lan doc.
330 ★ RESET 14/08/2026: bo so bake 566 (do khi CHUA co man che, gain 60x).
331 Nay dat theo CHIEU dang chon, va co y dat LECH VE PHIA XA de bo bam luon
332 phai di ve phia muc nen that (khong bao gio xuat phat sai phia):
333 - Chieu 1 (nen SANG): xuat phat THAP -> bo bam se leo LEN toi nen that.
334 - Chieu 0 (nen TOI) : xuat phat CAO -> bo bam se tut XUONG toi nen that.
335 ⚠ 61 chu khong phai 60: TCS_C_NEN_TOI_THIEU = 60 la san chan duoi, xuat
336 phat dung tren san thi nhanh "xuong cham" bi chan ngay, khong tut duoi 60. */
337#if (TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN != 0u)
338#define TCS_C_NEN_MAC_DINH 61u /* nen SANG -> xuat phat thap, leo len */
339#else
340#define TCS_C_NEN_MAC_DINH 1024u /* nen TOI -> xuat phat cao, tut xuong */
341#endif

343/* THEO DOI MUC NEN: LEN NGAY, XUONG CHAM.
344 - Len ngay: vat luon TOI hon nen, nen khong bao gio keo muc nen len duoc.
345 Den sang len thi bat duoc ngay lap tuc.
346 - Xuong cham: moi TCS_C_NEN_GIAM_MOI lan doc moi ha 1 don vi. De mot vat
347 nam lau truoc cam bien khong keo tut duoc muc nen xuong ngang no.
348 O ~50 lan doc/giay va he so 16 => ha ~3 don vi/giay: phoi di qua trong
349 1 giay chi keo tut 3 don vi (khong dang ke), con den phong toi di tu
350 566 xuong 313 thi bat kip sau khoang 80 giay. */

352#define TCS_C_NEN_TOI_THIEU 60u /* khong cho muc nen tut duoi day */

354/* ============ NGUONG NHAN MAU XANH DUONG (tren van mau da chuan hoa) =======
355 ⚠ DA HIEU CHINH THEO SO LIEU DO THAT tren rig (xem bang duoi).
356 
357 d_br = b - r : TRUC QUYET DINH CHINH.
358 d_bg = b - g : CHI la CHOT CHAN, khong phai truc phan biet.
359 
360 VI SAO d_bg KHONG DUNG LAM TRUC CHINH DUOC:
361 Kenh GREEN cua TCS34725 co dai pho RONG, duoi dap ung phu sang vung 450nm
362 cua xanh duong. Nghia la mot vat XANH DUONG van lam kenh green dem duoc
363 rat nhieu. Do thuc te tren rig: g LUON dinh o 97-98 du vat xanh dam hay
364 nhat, trong khi b chi 99-109 => d_bg chi con +1..+11. Bien do do QUA NHO,
365 khong the lam nguong (dat 7 la chan mat nua so lan doc dung).
366 Nguoc lai d_br rat khoe: +43..+62 tren vat xanh duong that.
367 
368 SO LIEU DO THAT (cam bien ngang, gain 60x, 154ms) - CUNG MOT vat xanh duong,
369 chi khac KHOANG CACH / goc dat:
370 Rat gan, goc tot : r=47 g= 98 b=108 -> d_br=+61 d_bg=+10
371 Gan, goc xau : r=56 g= 98 b= 99 -> d_br=+43 d_bg= +1
372 XA : r=59 g=101 b= 94 -> d_br=+35 d_bg= -7
373 XA hon : r=62 g=100 b= 91 -> d_br=+29 d_bg= -9
374 
375 ⚠ NHAN XET QUAN TRONG TU BANG TREN:
376 Kenh g DUNG YEN o 97-101 trong MOI truong hop - gan, xa, dam, nhat. Do la
377 dau hieu cua mot THANH PHAN CONG NEN co dinh (anh sang phong + dap ung nen
378 cua chip). Cang XA thi anh sang phan xa THAT cang nho so voi cai nen do,
379 nen van mau bi "bac" dan: r bo len, b tut xuong, b chui xuong DUOI g.
380 => Chot chan "b >= g" SAI khi vat o xa. Phai noi ra am.
381 => Cach trieu de la TRU NEN (xem TCS_BatTruNen) - bo han phan cong nen di
382 thi vat xa van cho van mau dam nhu vat gan.
383 
384 d_br van la truc dang tin duy nhat: giam deu tu +61 xuong +14 theo khoang
385 cach, nhung khong bao gio doi dau. Vat XANH LA cho d_br AM hoac gan 0
386 (nen bang tai xanh la do duoc chi +5) => d_br mot minh du de loai.
387 
388 ---- QUET RANH GIOI o ~10cm (do that, dua vat ra xa dan tung buoc) ----
389 b-r=+45 b-g= -1 | b-r=+38 b-g= -6 | b-r=+35 b-g= -7
390 b-r=+30 b-g= -8 | b-r=+27 b-g=-10 | b-r=+26 b-g=-10
391 b-r=+25 b-g=-11 | b-r=+24 b-g=-11 | b-r=+22 b-g=-11
392 b-r=+15 b-g=-10 | b-r=+14 b-g=-10
393 
394 ---- ⚠⚠ HAI CUM TACH BACH (do that tren rig, dung de dat nguong) ----
395 NEN (bang tai + khong khi, KHONG co vat):
396 r=64 g=102 b=88 -> b-r = +24 b-g = -14 (dung yen tuyet doi)
397 VAT XANH DUONG o khoang cach lam viec:
398 r=58 g=103 b=93 -> b-r = +35 b-g = -10
399 r=60 g=102 b=92 -> b-r = +32 b-g = -10

401 ★★★ SO LIEU CHUAN - CA BA MAU DO TRONG CUNG MOT PHIEN, cung khoang cach,
402 khong tat nguon, khong xe dich cam bien. ĐAY la bo so dung de bake: ★★★
403 
404 TRONG (bang tai + khong khi) : r=60 g=104 b=90 -> b-r=+30 b-g=-14
405 XANH DUONG : r=55 g=104 b=95 -> b-r=+40 b-g= -9
406 DO : r=75 g= 96 b=83 -> b-r= +8 b-g=-13
407 
408 ⚠ TRAT TU TREN TRUC b-r: DO (+8) < TRONG (+30) < XANH (+40)
409 TRONG NAM O GIUA. Day la diem mau chot: khong the dung mot nguong duy nhat
410 "lon hon thi xanh, nho hon thi khac" duoc. Phai la BA VUNG:
411 b-r >= 35 => XANH DUONG (nen +30, cong bien 5)
412 26 <= b-r <= 34 => TRONG (nen +30, +-4)
413 b-r <= 25 => MAU KHAC (nen -5, DO o +8 cach rat xa)
414 
415 Bien do duoc:
416 Xanh cach nen 10 don vi -> nguong 35 chia doi, con 5 moi phia
417 Do cach nen 22 don vi -> rat thoai mai
418 Khoang cach nhan dien cua DO va XANH bang nhau vi ca hai deu phai roi
419 khoi nen dung 5 don vi (mot ben len, mot ben xuong). */
420/* ★★★ SO DO THAT TREN RIG NGAY 03/08/2026 (lenh 'c', phoi xanh duong dat dung

439/* Bien dat TREN muc nen khi go lenh 'b' (hoc nen):
440 nguong_br = d_br_cua_nen + TCS_BIEN_TREN_NEN

445/* ============ NHAN DIEN NEN (bang tai trong) ==============================
446 ⚠ NEN KHONG PHAI "mau khac can loai ra" - no la KHONG CO VAT.
447 Cam bien dat ngang nen luc trong no van thay bang tai + khong khi, cho
448 C ~ 9000 (thua san toi) => neu chi xet do sang thi luc nao cung bao "co
449 vat" va co cau gat se chay oan khi bang tai trong.
450 
451 Cach nhan ra nen: van mau cua nen RAT ON DINH va nam o mot diem co dinh
452 tren hai truc (b-r, b-g). Neu so doc bam sat diem do thi ket luan TRONG,
453 khong xet gi them.
454 |d_br - d_br_nen| <= TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR VA
455 |d_bg - d_bg_nen| <= TCS_BIEN_NEN_BG => TRONG
456 
457 ⚠ THU TU XET: XANH DUONG duoc xet TRUOC vung nen (xem tcs34725.c). Neu xet
458 nen truoc thi vung nen (trai ve ca hai phia cua diem nen) se nuot mat mep
459 duoi cua dai xanh duong.
460 
461 ⚠ BIEN PHAI CAN HAI PHIA - day la yeu cau "khoang cach nhan dien cua cac
462 mau khac PHAI BANG xanh duong":
463 Vat XANH DUONG duoc nhan khi d_br VUOT LEN +TCS_BIEN_TREN_NEN (=4)
464 Vat MAU KHAC duoc nhan khi d_br TUT XUONG -TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR-1 (=-4)
465 Hai con so bang nhau => hai loai vat ROI KHOI NEN o cung mot muc lech,
466 tuc la duoc phat hien tu cung mot khoang cach.
467 Vi vay TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR = TCS_BIEN_TREN_NEN - 1. Sua mot cai thi sua ca hai.
468 
469 Kiem chung voi BO SO CHUAN (nen = +30, -14):
470 TRONG (+30,-14): lech (0,0) -> TRONG ✓
471 XANH (+40, -9): >= 35 -> BLUE (xet truoc, khong vao vung nen) ✓
472 DO ( +8,-13): lech (22,1) -> 22 > 4 -> MAU KHAC ✓
473 Trang ( 0, 0): lech (30,14) -> MAU KHAC ✓ */

554/* ⚠ CHAN CUNG cho muc NHA tin hieu: du tre co lon den dau, muc nha KHONG BAO
555 GIO duoc tut xuong duoi (d_br_cua_nen + TCS_BIEN_NHA_TOI_THIEU).
556 LY DO (loi that da gap): moc nen troi giua cac lan do (+29 -> +24 -> +19).
557 Khi nen troi LEN toi ngang muc nha thi CHINH CAI NEN du giu tin hieu BLUE
558 mai khong nha: quet xong mot vat xanh la ket luan dinh luon o XANH DUONG,
559 vat ra khoi vung cung khong quay ve TRONG, va vat mau khac phai ap that
560 sat moi keo noi d_br xuong du thap de thoat. */
561#define TCS_BIEN_NHA_TOI_THIEU 3
562 
563/* So lan doc LIEN TIEP khac voi ket luan dang chot thi BUOC phai doi theo,
564 ke ca khi chua co 2 lan doc lien tiep giong nhau. Chong ket luan bi ket
565 cung khi so doc dao dong qua lai giua hai nhom. */
566#define TCS_SO_LAN_EP_DOI 4u
567 
568/* Ti le giu lai cho truc CHINH khi HOC bang lenh 'c':
569 nguong_br = d_br_do_duoc * TCS_HS_HOC / 100
570 60% => vat xanh duong o goc xau / xa hon van lot (do thuc te d_br tut toi
571 30% khi doi goc, nen khong duoc de ti le nay cao qua). */
572#define TCS_HS_HOC 60u
573 
574/* Truc PHU khi hoc: nguong_bg = d_bg_do_duoc - TCS_BIEN_HOC_BG, chan duoi
575 TCS_NGUONG_BG_MAC_DINH. Tru mot bien RONG (25) vi d_bg lao doc theo khoang
576 cach: cung mot vat cho +10 luc gan va -9 luc xa. */
577#define TCS_BIEN_HOC_BG 25
578 
579/* ================== TRU NEN - CACH MUA THEM TAM XA ========================
580 Van de: cam bien luon thu them mot luong anh sang NEN co dinh (den phong,
581 anh sang tan xa, dap ung nen cua chip). Luong nay KHONG mang mau cua vat.
582 Vat cang XA thi anh sang phan xa that cang yeu, ti le nen/tin hieu cang
583 lon, van mau cang bi keo ve trung tinh => d_br teo dan, d_bg doi dau.
584 
585 Cach chua: do so dem THO cua canh TRONG (lenh 'b'), roi moi lan doc lay
586 r_hieu_qua = raw_r - nen_r (chan duoi 0)
587 tuong tu cho g va b, XONG MOI chuan hoa. Phan con lai la anh sang do CHINH
588 VAT phan xa ve => van mau dam nhu luc vat o gan, tam xa tang dang ke.
589 
590 ⚠⚠ CHI DUNG DUOC KHI NEN THUC SU TOI. Neu nen SANG ngang voi vat (cam bien
591 ngang nhung phia sau co vach/ban/nen sang phan xa ve), tru nen se an gan
592 het tin hieu, con lai vai don vi nhieu => van mau ra rac (r=0 g=0 b=0 hoac
593 b-r=+249). Do la ly do driver TU DANH GIA nen truoc khi bat:
594 C_trong < TCS_NGUONG_NEN_TOI -> nen toi that -> BAT tru nen
595 C_trong >= TCS_NGUONG_NEN_TOI -> nen sang -> KHONG bat, va sàn
596 toi cung ha ve muc nhieu (xem duoi)
597 Con mot luoi an toan nua ngay trong lua tinh: neu sau khi tru ma tong 3
598 kenh tut duoi 1/8 tong goc thi COI NHU tru nen dang pha tin hieu va tu
599 dong quay ve dung so lieu THO cho lan doc do.
600 
601 ⚠ Doi hoi: luc go 'b' phai KHONG co vat, va anh sang phong khong doi nhieu
602 sau do. Anh sang doi (bat/tat den, troi toi) thi go 'b' lai.
603 Tat/bat tay bang lenh 'z'. */
604#define TCS_HS_TRU_NEN 95u /* tru 95% muc nen, chua 5% cho nen troi nhe */
605 
606/* Muc C cua canh TRONG ma tren do coi la "NEN SANG" - khong duoc tru nen,
607 va do sang cung khong con dung de biet co vat hay khong. */
608#define TCS_NGUONG_NEN_TOI 0u /* RESET 03/08/2026: cu 2000. 0 = LUON coi la nen sang
609 -> KHONG BAO GIO tru nen (an toan nhat, tranh tru
610 nen an mat tin hieu). Muon dung tru nen thi phai do
611 C_trong that (lenh 'b') roi dat so nay tren no. */
612 
613/* Khi nen SANG: san toi chi con la muc chan NHIEU (C_trong / he so nay),
614 khong phai muc "phan biet co vat". Viec co vat hay khong se do MAU quyet
615 dinh. Chan duoi TCS_SAN_TOI_TOI_THIEU. */
616#define TCS_CHIA_SAN_TOI_NEN_SANG 8u
617#define TCS_SAN_TOI_TOI_THIEU 100u
618 
619/* ============ CHAN RAC KHI HOC NGUONG BANG LENH 'c' ======================
620 Van mau chuan hoa co tong 255. Mot vat xanh duong THAT o rig nay cho
621 d_br = +14..+62. Neu do ra tren +120 nghia la so lieu da hong (thuong do
622 tru nen an mat hai kenh kia) => TU CHOI hoc, dung de nguong rac ghi de. */
623#define TCS_HOC_BR_TOI_DA 120
624 
625/* Chan tren/duoi cho nguong hoc duoc - khong cho hoc ra so vo ly.
626 ★★★ SUA 31/07/2026: TCS_NGUONG_BR_MIN 8 -> 0.
627 SO LIEU DO DUOC TREN RIG (log UART that):
628 - Nen bang tai : b-r = -7 / -8, dai dao dong 0 don vi (30/30 lan doc)
629 - Phoi xanh, tai DUNG : b-r = +10 ... +19
630 - Phoi xanh, tai CHAY : b-r = +8 <== SAT NGUONG, bien bang KHONG
631 Con so 8 cu la DI SAN cua lan hieu chinh truoc, khi nen cua rig do duoc la
632 +31. Nay nen da ve -8 nhung chan duoi 8 van con, khien:
633 nguong tu hoc = nen + TCS_BIEN_TREN_NEN = -8 + 5 = -3
634 bi KEP NGUOC LEN thanh +8 => viec tu hoc nen bi VO HIEU HOA am tham.
635 Hau qua: bien chong gat oan thua 16 don vi, con bien BAT DUOC PHOI chi con
636 2 don vi (va bang 0 khi bang tai chay) => BO SOT PHOI, dung loi dang gap.
637 Ha ve 0 thi nguong thuc te do lop ung dung quyet dinh:
638 nen(-8) + BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN(7) = -1
639 => cach nen 7 don vi (nen dao dong 0) va cach phoi xanh 9 don vi. Can bang.
640 ⚠⚠ DA THU HA VE 0 NGAY 01/08/2026 VA BI HOI QUY - DA TRA LAI 8.
641 Hau qua khi de 0: "vua cap nguon, CHUA co phoi ma servo gat/giat lien tuc"
642 - dung lai trieu chung ma khoi comment BIEN_GAT_AN_TOAN trong main.c da mo
643 ta. Ha chan duoi lam nguong tu hoc tut xuong sat nen (nen -8 -> nguong -3),
644 nen chi can nen troi vai don vi la ket luan nham XANH.
645 => Bien chong gat oan QUAN TRONG HON bien bat duoc phoi: gat oan lam hong
646 me phan loai va co the ket co cau, con bo sot phoi chi la mot phoi di thang.
647 Muon noi bien bat phoi thi phai DO TRUOC (lenh 'w' khi bang tai DANG CHAY
648 de biet dai nhieu that cua nen), roi ha TUNG BUOC 8 -> 6 -> 4, moi buoc
649 chay thu it nhat 5 phut khong co phoi de chac chan khong tu kich. */
650#define TCS_NGUONG_BR_MIN 0 /* RESET 03/08/2026: cu 8. Ha ve 0 theo yeu cau de
651 nguong tu hoc khong bi kep nguoc len. XEM KHOI
652 COMMENT NGAY TREN: da tung bi hoi qui ngay
653 01/08/2026 voi trieu chung servo giat khi chua co
654 phoi. Lan nap dau tien phai canh chung. */
655#define TCS_NGUONG_BR_MAX 60
656 
657/* ★★★★★ HAI CHOT CHAN SO LIEU RAC - THEM 14/08/2026 ★★★★★
658 ==========================================================================
659 LOI DA XAY RA THAT (log 14/08/2026, bang tai TRONG, khong co vat):
660 C=2 | r=0 g=0 b=254 | b-r=+254 b-g=+254 | XANH DUONG <== TIN HIEU
661 Lap lai y het 9 lan lien tiep. Neu luc do servo khong bi khoa thi no da
662 gat lien tuc vao khong khi.
663 
664 HAI CAI SAI RIENG BIET trong mot dong do:
665 
666 (1) TIN HIEU QUA YEU. Chuan hoa chia cho TONG 3 kenh, nen khi tong be thi
667 MOT don vi dem tho bi phong dai thanh ca chuc don vi chuan hoa:
668 tong = 3 -> 1 dem = 85 don vi chuan hoa
669 tong = 255 -> 1 dem = 1 don vi
670 Muon d_br dang tin toi +-3 don vi thi can 255/tong <= 3, tuc
671 tong >= 85. Lay tron 100 cho chac. Duoi muc do, van mau CHI LA NHIEU
672 LUONG TU HOA - khong duoc phep dem ra ket luan mau.
673 
674 (2) SO LIEU VO LY VE VAT LY. Kenh CLEAR khong co kinh loc nen luon thu
675 duoc NHIEU photon hon bat ky kenh mau nao. Dong log tren co C=2 trong
676 khi suy nguoc ra kenh B phai o khoang 510 - tuc kenh mau LON GAP 255
677 LAN kenh trong. Dieu do khong the xay ra o mot cam bien lanh lan:
678 chac chan la BYTE BI HONG tren duong I2C (I2C khong co CRC cho phan
679 du lieu, nen mot bit loi van duoc HAL bao HAL_OK).
680 Chot: neu kenh mau lon nhat > C x TCS_HS_C_HOP_LE thi BO lan doc do.
681 
682 Ca hai chot deu tra ve TCS_KHONG_RO - CO Y, vi TCS_KHONG_RO khong thuoc
683 ca ba chuoi dem (TRONG / XANH / MAU KHAC) trong gy33_quet(), nen so lieu
684 rac se KHONG kich duoc chu trinh xanh LAN chu trinh do.
685 
686 ⚠ Neu sau nay bo sot phoi TOI MAU that su vi chot (1): dung ha con so nay,
687 ma NANG DO NHAY len (ATIME truoc, roi gain) cho tong vuot 100 mot cach
688 lanh manh. Ha nguong xuong chi lam he tin vao nhieu. */
689#define TCS_TONG_TIN_TOI_THIEU 100u /* tong R+G+B toi thieu de tin van mau */
690#define TCS_HS_C_HOP_LE 4u /* kenh mau > C x 4 => du lieu hong */
691 
692/* Muc bao hoa: kenh C HOAC bat ky kenh R/G/B nao vuot ti le nay cua tran dem
693 thi coi nhu chay het thang (phan xa guong cua NHUA BONG hay gap) => so
694 lieu mau khong con dung => phai ha gain hoac ha thoi gian tich phan. */
695#define TCS_MUC_BAO_HOA_PT 90u /* % cua tran dem */
696 
697/* SO LAN DOC LAY TRUNG VI cho moi phep do mau (chong phan xa GUONG cua nhua
698 bong VA chong nhieu khi tin hieu yeu o xa).
699 Phai la so LE (1,3,5). 1 = tat loc. Cang lon cang cham:
700 moi lan +1 chu ky tich phan (154ms o ATIME mac dinh).
701 
702 ⚠ MAC DINH = 1 vi VAT CHAY LIEN TUC qua cam bien:
703 3 mau -> ~460ms/lan doc -> vat di qua nhanh la khong kip doc
704 1 mau -> ~160ms/lan doc -> kip ~6 lan doc moi giay
705 Ha so mau KHONG lam giam do nhay (tich phan van 154ms), chi bot kha nang
706 chong dot bien. So lieu do that tren rig lap lai y het nhieu chu ky lien
707 nen gan nhu khong can loc. Neu thay so nhay lung tung thi tang bang 'f'. */
708#define TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAC_DINH 1u
709#define TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX 5u
710 
711/* ================= NEN TU BAM (auto-track) ================================
712 Van de da gap: moc nen troi giua cac lan do (+29 -> +24 -> +19) vi anh
713 sang phong doi, cam bien xe dich, bang tai doi vi tri. Moc nen sai lam
714 ca ba nhom ket luan sai theo.
715 
716 Cach chua: firmware TU CAP NHAT moc nen khi so doc DUNG YEN lien tuc mot
717 khoang du dai. Ly le: vat chay lien tuc qua cam bien thi chi nam trong
718 tam nhin duoi 1 giay; con dung yen nhieu giay lien nghia la bang tai dang
719 TRONG => cai dang nhin chinh la nen.
720 
721 ⚠⚠ LOI DA GAP (phai hieu ro truoc khi noi long lai cac so duoi):
722 Khi hieu chuan bang tay, nguoi dung GIU vat do dung yen truoc cam bien
723 rat lau. Ban dau cac chan an toan qua long nen moc nen "bo" theo vat do
724 qua cac gia tri trung gian: +30 -> +22 -> +15 -> +8. Ket qua la vat DO bi
725 ket luan la TRONG. Bon chan an toan duoi day chan dung kieu bo dan do:
726 
727 1) Bo qua nhung lan doc ket luan la XANH DUONG (vat xanh dung yen truoc
728 cam bien se khong bi bien thanh nen).
729 2) CUA SO TUNG LAN DOC: chi tinh nhung lan doc nam trong khoang
730 +-TCS_NEN_BAM_CUA_SO quanh moc HIEN TAI. Vat do o +8 cach moc +30 toi
731 22 don vi => bi loai ngay tu dau, khong bao gio thanh ung vien.
732 3) TRAN TONG: moc khong duoc phep di xa qua TCS_NEN_BAM_TONG so voi NEO
733 (gia tri bake, hoac gia tri do duoc lan go 'b' gan nhat). Day la
734 chan cuoi cung chong "bo dan": du co bo tung buoc nho bao nhieu lan
735 cung khong vuot duoc khoi tran nay.
736 4) Khi doi moc, nguong xanh duoc doi theo dung bang ay don vi, nen moi
737 hieu chinh tay bang lenh 'e' van duoc giu nguyen. */
738#define TCS_NEN_BAM_SO_LAN 30u /* so lan doc dung yen thi nhan moc moi
739 (~5 giay o 154ms/lan) */
740#define TCS_NEN_BAM_DAO_DONG 2 /* +-bao nhieu van coi la "dung yen" */
741/* ★★★ SUA 03/08/2026 (V2_1) - LOI DA XAY RA THAT, khong phai phong doan:
742 Giu phoi DO dung yen truoc cam bien ~44 lan doc thi moc nen BI KEO tu -9
743 len ~0, va nguong xanh bi doi theo dung +9 (16 -> 25). Hau qua: phoi xanh
744 o +28 chi con cach nguong 3 don vi => BO SOT PHOI XANH.
745 Ca ba chot deu thung vi chung duoc dat cho rig CU (phoi do cach nen 22
746 don vi). Rig hien tai phoi do chi cach nen 9 don vi:
747 chot 1: raw_c(155) < s_san_toi(0) -> sai -> khong chan
748 chot 2: |0-(-9)| = 9 <= 10 -> lot
749 chot 3: |0-(-9)| = 9 <= 12 -> lot
750 Ha ca hai ve 4 thi:
751 moc dung yen o -9 : phoi do o -2 cach 7 > 4 -> bi loai o chot 2 ✓
752 neu moc troi toi -5 (bien toi da): phoi do o -2 cach 3 <= 4, lot chot
753 2, nhung chot 3 chan lai vi |-2-(-9)| = 7 > 4 ✓
754 Nen dao dong 0 don vi nen cua so 4 van du rong de bam anh sang troi that. */
755/* ★★★★★ SUA 14/08/2026 (lan 4): 4 -> 6 CA HAI. LOI THIET KE CO HE THONG.
756 ==========================================================================
757 SO DO THAT 14/08/2026, cung mot cai nen, chi khac motor:
758 motor DUNG : C=7380 r=1846 g=3143 b=2705 -> b-r = +28
759 motor CHAY : C=7148 r=1668 g=2993 b=2576 -> b-r = +32
760 KHONG PHAI NHIEU: 7/7 lan doc khi motor chay deu dung +32, dao dong 0.
761 Day la mot TRANG THAI ON DINH KHAC, dich deu +4 don vi.
762 Kenh do tut 9.6% trong khi luc va lam tut 4.8% - anh sang khong chi YEU DI
763 ma con DOI MAU (xanh hon). Phu hop voi gia thuyet: motor keo dong lam sut
764 nhe duong nguon, den LED tren module mo di va pho dich ve phia xanh.
765 
766 ⚠⚠ VI SAO DAY LA LOI CO HE THONG chu khong phai mot con so can chinh:
767 TCS_HocSanToi() chay LUC KHOI DONG, ma luc khoi dong motor LUON DUNG (day
768 la yeu cau an toan, xem MOTOR_TAT o dau main()). Nghia la moc nen LUON duoc
769 hoc o trang thai motor DUNG (+28), roi he chay that o trang thai motor CHAY
770 (+32). Sai so 4 don vi nay duoc NUONG VAO he ngay tu dau va khong bao gio
771 tu mat di. Truoc day khong lo ra vi trai gia tri rong 49 don vi; nay man
772 che lam trai chi con 18 don vi thi 4 don vi la mot phan tu ngan sach.
773 
774 CACH CHUA: de bo NEN TU BAM di theo. Nhung voi cua so 4 thi buoc dich dung
775 bang 4 - nam DUNG TREN mep nen bam khong chac chan. Noi ca hai len 6.
776 
777 ⚠ DA KIEM TRA AN TOAN TRUOC KHI NOI (day la ly do chon 6 chu khong phai 8):
778 TCS_NEN_BAM_TONG gioi han moc di xa NEO bao nhieu. Neo = +28 (hoc luc khoi
779 dong). Voi 6 thi moc chi chay duoc trong [+22, +34].
780 PHOI DO nam o +20 (motor dung) - THAP HON can duoi +22.
781 => Moc KHONG BAO GIO tut xuong ngoi len phoi do duoc, ke ca khi giu
782 phoi do dung yen truoc cam bien vo han.
783 Neu dat 8 thi khoang la [+20, +36] - CHAM DUNG phoi do, tai phat dung loi
784 "giu phoi do dung yen thi moc nen bo theo" da ghi o tren. VI VAY LA 6. */
785#define TCS_NEN_BAM_CUA_SO 6 /* SUA 14/08/2026: 4 -> 6 (dich motor) */
786#define TCS_NEN_BAM_TONG 6 /* SUA 14/08/2026: 4 -> 6 (dich motor) */
787 
788/* ========================================================================== */
789 
790typedef enum
791{
792 TCS_TRONG = 0, /* khong co vat truoc cam bien (duoi san toi) */
793 TCS_XANH_DUONG, /* CO VAT + mau XANH DUONG => RA TIN HIEU */
794 TCS_MAU_KHAC, /* CO VAT nhung khong phai xanh duong => loai ra */
795 TCS_KHONG_RO /* bao hoa - so lieu khong tin duoc */
796} TCS_Mau_t;
797 
798typedef struct
799{
800 uint16_t raw_c; /* kenh CLEAR - do sang tong (so dem tho) */
801 uint16_t raw_r; /* kenh DO */
802 uint16_t raw_g; /* kenh XANH LA */
803 uint16_t raw_b; /* kenh XANH DUONG */
804 uint8_t r; /* ti le 0..255, chuan hoa theo TONG 3 kenh => r+g+b=255 */
805 uint8_t g;
806 uint8_t b;
807 int16_t d_br; /* b - r : truc chinh nhan xanh duong */
808 int16_t d_bg; /* b - g : truc phu (b phai la kenh lon nhat)*/
809 uint8_t co_vat; /* 1 = co vat truoc cam bien (C vuot san toi)*/
810 uint8_t la_xanh; /* 1 = ket luan XANH DUONG (chua qua bo loc) */
811 uint8_t bao_hoa; /* 1 = C hoac 1 kenh cham tran, so lieu khong tin */
812 TCS_Mau_t mau; /* ket luan cuoi (da qua bo loc on dinh 2 lan doc) */
813} TCS_KetQua_t;
814 
815/* Ket qua tu kiem tra ket noi - chay truoc khi quet mau */
816typedef struct
817{
818 uint8_t so_tb; /* so thiet bi tra loi tren bus I2C */
819 uint8_t dia_chi[8]; /* danh sach dia chi 7-bit tim duoc */
820 uint8_t id; /* thanh ghi ID doc duoc (0x44 / 0x4D) */
821 uint16_t c, r, g, b; /* mot lan doc thu */
822 uint16_t tran; /* tran dem theo ATIME hien tai */
823 uint8_t ok_bus; /* [1] co thiet bi ACK o 0x29 */
824 uint8_t ok_id; /* [2] ID dung cua TCS34725 */
825 uint8_t ok_doc; /* [3] bat PON+AEN va doc duoc so lieu moi */
826 uint8_t ok_sang; /* [4] du sang (C khong qua thap) */
827 uint8_t ok_khong_bh; /* [5] khong bao hoa */
828 uint8_t ok_tach_mau; /* [6] 3 kenh khong dinh nhau y het */
829 uint8_t du_dieu_kien; /* 1 = du dieu kien de bat dau quet mau */
830} TCS_ChanDoan_t;
831 
832/* Khoi tao: nho handle I2C, PON+AEN, nap ATIME/AGAIN mac dinh (60x/154ms).
833 Tra ve 1 = tim thay va cau hinh duoc cam bien, 0 = khong thay. */
834uint8_t TCS_Init(I2C_HandleTypeDef *hi2c);
835 
836/* ★★ HAM PHUC VU trong luc CHO CAM BIEN DO XONG.
837 Mot phep do ton tron mot chu ky tich phan (154ms o cau hinh mac dinh) va
838 trong suot thoi gian do driver chi ngoi cho co AVALID. Neu khong lam gi
839 thi ca he thong dung hinh 154ms: servo khong duoc truot tiep (GIAT rat ro),
840 nut nhan co the bi bo sot, IR khong duoc quet.
841 Dang ky mot ham o day thi driver se goi no lien tuc trong luc cho.
842 Ham nay PHAI ngan va KHONG duoc goi lai bat ky ham TCS_* nao. */
843typedef void (*TCS_HamPhucVu_t)(void);
844void TCS_DatHamPhucVu(TCS_HamPhucVu_t ham);
845 
846/* QUET TOAN BO BUS I2C (0x08..0x77) - in ra dia chi nao co thiet bi.
847 Cong cu so 1 khi cam bien "khong noi chuyen": biet ngay la dut day
848 (khong dia chi nao) hay cam nham module MCU phu (chi thay 0x5A).
849 Tra ve so thiet bi tim duoc, ket qua nam trong cd->dia_chi[]. */
850uint8_t TCS_QuetBus(TCS_ChanDoan_t *cd);
851 
852/* TU KIEM TRA KET NOI - chay truoc khi quet mau. Ton khoang 0.5 s.
853 Tra ve 1 = du dieu kien quet mau, 0 = con loi day / cau hinh. */
854uint8_t TCS_KiemTraKetNoi(TCS_ChanDoan_t *cd);
855 
856/* DOC NHANH CHI KENH CLEAR (do sang tong) - 1 chu ky, KHONG loc trung vi.
857 Dung de RINH nhanh / do muc sang nen. */
858uint16_t TCS_DocClear(void);
859 
860/* Doc 1 chu ky day du - CA 4 KENH CUNG MOT LUC (khong nhoe nhu TCS3200),
861 co LOC TRUNG VI TCS_SO_MAU_LOC lan de chong nhieu khi tin hieu yeu.
862 Tu tinh d_br, d_bg, co_vat, bao_hoa va ket luan mau.
863 Tra ve 1 = OK, 0 = loi I2C / khong co so lieu moi. */
864uint8_t TCS_Doc(TCS_KetQua_t *kq);
865 
866/* 1 = ket luan hien tai la XANH DUONG (tien loi cho phan dieu khien) */
867uint8_t TCS_LaXanhDuong(const TCS_KetQua_t *kq);
868 
869/* Ten ket luan dang chuoi de in ra UART */
870const char *TCS_TenMau(TCS_Mau_t m);
871 
872/* HOC SAN TOI (lenh 'b') - chay khi KHONG co vat truoc cam bien, cam bien
873 dang nhin dung cai canh se thay luc chay that (khoang trong / toi).
874 Do muc CLEAR luc trong roi dat:
875 san_toi = C_trong * TCS_HS_SAN_TOI / 100 + TCS_CONG_SAN_TOI
876 ⚠ PHAI khong co vat nao truoc cam bien khi goi.
877 Tra ve 1 = OK, ket qua do nam trong *kq. */
878uint8_t TCS_HocSanToi(TCS_KetQua_t *kq);
879 
880/* Doc / dat san toi bang tay (so dem kenh CLEAR) */
881uint16_t TCS_LaySanToi(void);
882void TCS_DatSanToi(uint16_t san);
883 
884/* Muc CLEAR do duoc trong lan HOC SAN TOI gan nhat (0 = chua hoc lan nao) */
885uint16_t TCS_LayCTrong(void);

3 * @file tcs34725.c
4 * @brief Driver TCS34725 - doc 4 kenh RGBC 16-bit qua I2C.
5 * BAN "CHI NHAN XANH DUONG": XANH DUONG => co tin hieu,
6 * moi mau khac => TCS_MAU_KHAC (phan loai ra), khong co vat => TRONG.
7 * Cau hinh cho cam bien DAT NGANG, vat cach 10-15 cm.

15 (kieu AUTO-INCREMENT) thi doc nhieu byte lien tiep duoc trong 1 lan. */
