113static void tcs_nap_lai_cau_hinh(void);
114 
115/* ========================= TU CUU BUS I2C ================================= */
116 
117/* ★ Cho 'ms' mili giay nhung VAN tra quyen dieu khien ve ung dung trong luc
118 cho, thay vi chan cung nhu HAL_Delay.
119 Them 03/08/2026 cung dot chua loi "servo giat va cham": duong cuu bus I2C
120 truoc day chan cung khoang 30ms (18 nhip SCL + 3ms + 8ms khoi tao lai),
121 va no chay DUNG LUC servo dang quay (mat I2C xay ra khi servo rut dong).
122 Viec truot servo nay da chuyen vao ngat TIM3 nen khong con bi anh huong,
123 nhung IR va nut bam van doc bang vong lap chinh - nen van phai nha quyen
124 dieu khien ra o day. */
125static void tcs_cho_phuc_vu(uint32_t ms)
126{
127 uint32_t moc = HAL_GetTick();
128 
129 while ((HAL_GetTick() - moc) < ms)
130 {
131 if (s_ham_phuc_vu != NULL) { s_ham_phuc_vu(); }
132 }
133}
134 
135/* Nghi 1ms giua hai muc SCL - cuu bus chay ~500Hz, cham nhung chac an. */
136static void tcs_nhip(void)
137{
138 tcs_cho_phuc_vu(1u);
139}
140 
141uint8_t TCS_CuuBus(void)
142{
143 GPIO_InitTypeDef gi = {0};
144 uint8_t i;
145 uint8_t id = 0u;
146 
147 if (s_hi2c == NULL) { return 0u; }
148 
149 s_so_lan_cuu++;
150 
151 /* 1. Tra hai chan ve GPIO open-drain, ca hai o muc CAO (nha bus) */
152 HAL_I2C_DeInit(s_hi2c);
153 
154 gi.Pin = TCS_SCL_Pin | TCS_SDA_Pin;
155 gi.Mode = GPIO_MODE_OUTPUT_OD;
156 gi.Pull = GPIO_NOPULL;
157 gi.Speed = GPIO_SPEED_FREQ_HIGH;
158 HAL_GPIO_Init(TCS_SCL_GPIO_Port, &gi);
159 HAL_GPIO_WritePin(TCS_SCL_GPIO_Port, TCS_SCL_Pin, GPIO_PIN_SET);
160 HAL_GPIO_WritePin(TCS_SDA_GPIO_Port, TCS_SDA_Pin, GPIO_PIN_SET);
161 tcs_nhip();
162 
163 /* 2. Danh 9 xung SCL de slave dang ket giua byte nha SDA ra. */
164 for (i = 0u; i < 9u; i++)
165 {
166 HAL_GPIO_WritePin(TCS_SCL_GPIO_Port, TCS_SCL_Pin, GPIO_PIN_RESET);
167 tcs_nhip();
168 HAL_GPIO_WritePin(TCS_SCL_GPIO_Port, TCS_SCL_Pin, GPIO_PIN_SET);
169 tcs_nhip();
170 }
171 
172 /* 3. Tao dieu kien STOP. */
173 HAL_GPIO_WritePin(TCS_SDA_GPIO_Port, TCS_SDA_Pin, GPIO_PIN_RESET);
174 tcs_nhip();
175 HAL_GPIO_WritePin(TCS_SCL_GPIO_Port, TCS_SCL_Pin, GPIO_PIN_SET);
176 tcs_nhip();
177 HAL_GPIO_WritePin(TCS_SDA_GPIO_Port, TCS_SDA_Pin, GPIO_PIN_SET);
178 tcs_nhip();
179 
180 /* 4. Khoi tao lai khoi I2C (MSP tu tra hai chan ve AF open-drain). */
181 if (HAL_I2C_Init(s_hi2c) != HAL_OK)
182 {
183 return 0u;
184 }
185 
186 /* 5. Nap lai cau hinh cam bien - chip co the da mat trang thai. */
187 s_dang_cuu = 1u;
188 tcs_nap_lai_cau_hinh();
189 (void)HAL_I2C_Mem_Read(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
190 (uint16_t)(CMD | REG_ID), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
191 &id, 1u, I2C_TIMEOUT);
192 s_dang_cuu = 0u;
193 
194 return ((id == ID_TCS34725) || (id == ID_TCS34727)) ? 1u : 0u;
195}
196 
197/* Goi khi mot giao dich that bai: dem loi, va neu chua o trong thu tuc cuu
198 thi chay cuu bus mot lan. Tra ve 1 = da cuu xong, dang goi co the thu lai. */
199static uint8_t tcs_xu_ly_loi(void)
200{
201 s_so_loi++;
202 
203 if (s_dang_cuu) /* dang cuu ma con loi -> khong de quy */
204 {
205 return 0u;
206 }
207 return TCS_CuuBus();
208}
209 
210/* ========================= LOP I2C MUC THAP ===============================
211 Ba ham duoi deu theo mot khuon: thu mot lan; that bai thi cuu bus roi
212 THU LAI DUNG MOT LAN nua. */
213 
214static uint8_t tcs_ghi8(uint8_t reg, uint8_t gt)
215{
216 if (s_hi2c == NULL) { return 0u; }
217 
218 if (HAL_I2C_Mem_Write(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
219 (uint16_t)(CMD | reg), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
220 &gt, 1u, I2C_TIMEOUT) == HAL_OK)
221 {
222 return 1u;
223 }
224 if (tcs_xu_ly_loi() == 0u) { return 0u; }
225 
226 return (HAL_I2C_Mem_Write(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
227 (uint16_t)(CMD | reg), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
228 &gt, 1u, I2C_TIMEOUT) == HAL_OK) ? 1u : 0u;
229}
230 
231static uint8_t tcs_doc8(uint8_t reg, uint8_t *gt)
232{
233 if (s_hi2c == NULL) { return 0u; }
234 
235 if (HAL_I2C_Mem_Read(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
236 (uint16_t)(CMD | reg), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
237 gt, 1u, I2C_TIMEOUT) == HAL_OK)
238 {
239 return 1u;
240 }
241 if (tcs_xu_ly_loi() == 0u) { return 0u; }
242 
243 return (HAL_I2C_Mem_Read(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
244 (uint16_t)(CMD | reg), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
245 gt, 1u, I2C_TIMEOUT) == HAL_OK) ? 1u : 0u;
246}
247 
248/* ★ CHU KY CUA LOI TRUYEN da do duoc 14/08/2026: byte cao == byte thap, tuc
249 gia tri chia het cho 0x0101 = 257. Xem tcs_doc_rgbc(). */
250static uint8_t hai_byte_giong_nhau(uint16_t v)
251{
252 return (uint8_t)(((v >> 8) == (v & 0x00FFu)) ? 1u : 0u);
253}
254 
255/* ★ DUONG DU PHONG: doc 8 byte C,R,G,B bang 8 GIAO DICH RIENG LE.
256 Cham hon cum 8 byte nhung khong co pha "hai byte cuoi" nen khong dinh loi
257 lap byte da do duoc ngay 14/08/2026. Xem giai thich trong tcs_doc_rgbc(). */
258static uint8_t tcs_doc_rgbc_tung_byte(uint16_t *c, uint16_t *r,
259 uint16_t *g, uint16_t *b)
260{
261 uint8_t d[8];
262 uint8_t i;
263 
264 for (i = 0u; i < 8u; i++)
265 {
266 if (tcs_doc8((uint8_t)(REG_CDATAL + i), &d[i]) == 0u)
267 {
268 return 0u;
269 }
270 }
271 *c = (uint16_t)((uint16_t)d[1] << 8) | d[0];
272 *r = (uint16_t)((uint16_t)d[3] << 8) | d[2];
273 *g = (uint16_t)((uint16_t)d[5] << 8) | d[4];
274 *b = (uint16_t)((uint16_t)d[7] << 8) | d[6];
275 return 1u;
276}
277 
278/* Doc lien 8 byte C,R,G,B (moi kenh 2 byte, byte THAP truoc) */
279static uint8_t tcs_doc_rgbc(uint16_t *c, uint16_t *r, uint16_t *g, uint16_t *b)
280{
281 uint8_t d[8];
282 
283 if (s_hi2c == NULL) { return 0u; }
284 
285 if (HAL_I2C_Mem_Read(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
286 (uint16_t)(CMD_INC | REG_CDATAL), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
287 d, 8u, I2C_TIMEOUT) != HAL_OK)
288 {
289 if (tcs_xu_ly_loi() == 0u) { return 0u; }
290 
291 if (HAL_I2C_Mem_Read(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
292 (uint16_t)(CMD_INC | REG_CDATAL), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
293 d, 8u, I2C_TIMEOUT) != HAL_OK)
294 {
295 return 0u;
296 }
297 }
298 *c = (uint16_t)((uint16_t)d[1] << 8) | d[0];
299 *r = (uint16_t)((uint16_t)d[3] << 8) | d[2];
300 *g = (uint16_t)((uint16_t)d[5] << 8) | d[4];
301 *b = (uint16_t)((uint16_t)d[7] << 8) | d[6];
302 
303 /* ★★★★★ CHOT KIEM TRA + DUONG DOC DU PHONG - THEM 14/08/2026 ★★★★★
304 ==========================================================================
305 LOI DA DO DUOC THAT (log 14/08/2026, 24/24 lan doc lien tiep):
306 b_tho = 50115, 50372, 47545, 49087, 51400, 58853, 52685, ...
307 TAT CA deu chia het cho 257 = 0x0101, du 0. Mot so chia het cho 0x0101
308 nghia la BYTE CAO BANG BYTE THAP: d[7] == d[6]. Xac suat 24 lan lien tiep
309 ngau nhien la (1/256)^24 - coi nhu bang khong.
310 Dong thoi b_tho (~50000) lon gap ~6.6 lan kenh CLEAR (~7568), ma CLEAR
311 khong co kinh loc nen KHONG THE nho hon kenh mau. Hai dau hieu cung chi
312 ve mot ket luan: BYTE CUOI CUA CUM 8 BYTE bi lap lai byte truoc no.
313 
314 Hang so trong file NAY da duoc kiem tra va DUNG (CMD_INC = 0xA0 = COMMAND
315 + auto-increment, REG_CDATAL = 0x14 -> 8 byte la 0x14..0x1B = C,R,G,B),
316 nen loi KHONG nam o lua chon thanh ghi ma o TANG TRUYEN.
317 
318 DUONG DU PHONG: doc lai bang 8 GIAO DICH RIENG LE, moi lan mot byte.
319 Doc mot byte khong co pha "hai byte cuoi" nen mien nhiem voi loi tren.
320 Ton them ~8 x 60us = 0.5ms, khong dang ke so voi 154ms tich phan.
321 
322 ⚠ DAY LA MIENG VA, KHONG PHAI THUOC CHUA. Bo dem s_so_lan_doc_lai cho
323 biet no phai va bao nhieu lan; con so nay lon nghia la duong I2C dang
324 that su co van de va PHAI xu ly phan cung (xem huong dan dau file ve
325 chan S0/S1 va tro keo len). */
326 /* ★★ SUA 14/08/2026 (lan 2) - CHOT "> C x 4" MOT MINH LA KHONG DU.
327 SO DO THAT sau khi thao man che, mau phoi xanh:
328 C=10034 b_tho=10023 -> b NHO HON C nen chot cu KHONG bat duoc
329 C=10025 b_tho= 9509 -> lot luon, va bi ket luan "XANH DUONG"
330 Nhung 10023 = 39 x 257 va 9509 = 37 x 257: VAN LA loi hai byte giong
331 nhau, chi khac la lan nay gia tri rac roi dung vao vung "trong nhu that".
332 Loi da chuyen tu LIEN TUC sang THI THOANG (2/8 lan doc), nen kiem tra
333 theo do lon khong con dung - phai bat DUNG CHU KY cua no.
334 
335 CHU KY: byte cao == byte thap, tuc v = X<<8 | X, tuc v chia het 0x0101.
336 ⚠ Mot gia tri THAT cung co the tinh co co hai byte giong nhau (xac suat
337 1/256). Vi vay KHONG loai thang, ma DOC LAI bang duong tung byte roi
338 dung so doc lai. Doc lai ton ~0.5ms, xay ra ~1/256 lan voi so liệu lanh
339 - khong dang ke so voi 154ms tich phan. */
340 if ((*r > *c) || (*g > *c) || (*b > *c) ||
341 hai_byte_giong_nhau(*c) || hai_byte_giong_nhau(*r) ||
342 hai_byte_giong_nhau(*g) || hai_byte_giong_nhau(*b))
343 {
344 uint16_t c2, r2, g2, b2;
345 
346 if (tcs_doc_rgbc_tung_byte(&c2, &r2, &g2, &b2) != 0u)
347 {
348 /* ★★ SUA 14/08/2026 (lan 3) - BO DEM TRUOC DAY DEM SAI, LAM HOANG BAO.
349 Ban dau tang bo dem NGAY KHI nghi ngo. Nhung chot "hai byte giong
350 nhau" tu no da bat nham mot cach LANH TINH: mot gia tri THAT cung co
351 1/256 co hoi trung hai byte, va co TOI 4 KENH duoc soi, nen ~1.6% so
352 lan doc se bi nghi oan. Log 14/08/2026 bao "590 lan I2C hong" trong
353 khi phan lon la nghi oan - con so do lam nguoi doc tuong duong bus
354 hong nang hon thuc te rat nhieu.
355 NAY chi dem khi so doc lai THUC SU KHAC so cua cum 8 byte, tuc la da
356 chung minh duoc cum 8 byte sai. Con so moi la bang chung that. */
357 if ((c2 != *c) || (r2 != *r) || (g2 != *g) || (b2 != *b))
358 {
359 s_so_lan_doc_lai++;
360 }
361 
362 /* Chi nhan so lieu doc lai khi no HOP LE VE VAT LY. Neu doc rieng cung
363 ra so vo ly thi van de nam o CHINH CAM BIEN / module (vi du MCU phu
364 cua GY-33 dang tranh bus), khong phai o cum 8 byte - luc do giu so
365 cu de hai chot chan trong tcs_phan_loai() bat duoc va bao KHONG RO. */
366 if ((r2 <= c2) && (g2 <= c2) && (b2 <= c2))
367 {
368 *c = c2; *r = r2; *g = g2; *b = b2;
369 }
370 }
371 }
372 return 1u;
373}
374 
375uint32_t TCS_LaySoLanDocLai(void)
376{
377 return s_so_lan_doc_lai;
378}
379 
380void TCS_LayThongKeLoi(uint32_t *so_loi, uint32_t *so_lan_cuu)
381{
382 if (so_loi != NULL) { *so_loi = s_so_loi; }
383 if (so_lan_cuu != NULL) { *so_lan_cuu = s_so_lan_cuu; }
384}
385 
386void TCS_XoaThongKeLoi(void)
387{
388 s_so_loi = 0u;
389 s_so_lan_cuu = 0u;
390}
391 
392/* Ep cam bien do LAI TU DAU roi cho ket qua MOI.
393 Tat AEN rồi bat lai => AVALID bi xoa, chu ky tich phan bat dau lai tu
394 thoi diem nay. Neu khong, doc phai so lieu cu (con dong tren bang tai
395 la doc nham vi tri phoi truoc do). */
396static uint8_t tcs_do_moi(void)
397{
398 uint32_t t0, han;
399 uint8_t st = 0u;
400 
401 if (tcs_ghi8(REG_ENABLE, EN_PON) == 0u) { return 0u; }
402 if (tcs_ghi8(REG_ENABLE, (uint8_t)(EN_PON | EN_AEN)) == 0u) { return 0u; }
403 
404 /* Cho AVALID, han = 3 lan thoi gian tich phan + 10ms.
405 Tru theo HIEU so tick de khong sai khi bo dem tick tran. */
406 t0 = HAL_GetTick();
407 han = (uint32_t)TCS_ThoiGianTichPhan_ms() * 3u + 10u;
408 while ((HAL_GetTick() - t0) < han)
409 {
410 /* ★ Trong luc cho (toi 154ms), tra quyen dieu khien ve cho ung dung de
411 no truot servo / quet nut / quet IR. Khong co buoc nay thi servo dung
412 hinh giua hanh trinh moi lan do mau => GIAT thay ro. */
413 if (s_ham_phuc_vu != NULL) { s_ham_phuc_vu(); }
414 
415 if (tcs_doc8(REG_STATUS, &st) == 0u) { return 0u; }
416 if (st & ST_AVALID) { return 1u; }
417 }
418 return 0u; /* qua han - cam bien treo */
419}
420 
421/* Trung vi cua mot mang nho (n = 1,3,5) - sap xep chen roi lay phan tu giua. */
422static uint16_t tcs_trung_vi(uint16_t *v, uint8_t n)
423{
424 uint8_t i, j;
425 for (i = 1u; i < n; i++)
426 {
427 uint16_t x = v[i];
428 j = i;
429 while ((j > 0u) && (v[j - 1u] > x)) { v[j] = v[j - 1u]; j--; }
430 v[j] = x;
431 }
432 return v[n / 2u];
433}
434 
435/* Doc CA 4 KENH co LOC TRUNG VI TCS_SO_MAU_LOC lan. Chong phan xa GUONG cua
436 nhua bong: mot lan doc co the vot dot bien, trung vi loai duoc diem la. */
437static uint8_t tcs_doc_rgbc_loc(uint16_t *c, uint16_t *r, uint16_t *g, uint16_t *b)
438{
439 uint16_t vc[TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX], vr[TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX];
440 uint16_t vg[TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX], vb[TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX];
441 uint8_t n = 0u, i;
442 
443 for (i = 0u; i < s_so_mau; i++)
444 {
445 if (tcs_do_moi() == 0u) { break; }
446 if (tcs_doc_rgbc(&vc[n], &vr[n], &vg[n], &vb[n]) == 0u) { break; }
447 n++;
448 }
449 if (n == 0u) { return 0u; }
450 
451 *c = tcs_trung_vi(vc, n);
452 *r = tcs_trung_vi(vr, n);
453 *g = tcs_trung_vi(vg, n);
454 *b = tcs_trung_vi(vb, n);
455 return 1u;
456}
457 
458/* ====================== TINH TOAN / PHAN LOAI ============================= */
459 
460/* Chuan hoa 3 kenh ve thang 0..255 theo TONG 3 KENH.
461 KHONG chia cho kenh CLEAR (CLEAR con thu ca hong ngoai). Chia cho tong
462 R+G+B cho ra thanh phan mau thuan tuy, khong phu thuoc do sang lan khoang
463 cach. Ket qua luon thoa R+G+B = 255, mau trung tinh cho 85/85/85. */
464/* Tru mot muc nen ra khoi so dem tho, chan duoi 0.
465 Chi tru TCS_HS_TRU_NEN % de con chua cho nen troi len mot chut (den phong
466 nhap nhay, nhiet do chip doi) - tru het 100% de bi am/ve 0 lien tuc. */
467static uint16_t tcs_tru(uint16_t tho, uint16_t nen)
468{
469 uint32_t n = ((uint32_t)nen * TCS_HS_TRU_NEN) / 100u;
470 
471 return (tho > (uint16_t)n) ? (uint16_t)(tho - (uint16_t)n) : 0u;
472}
473 
474static void tcs_chuan_hoa(TCS_KetQua_t *kq)
475{
476 uint16_t er = kq->raw_r, eg = kq->raw_g, eb = kq->raw_b;
477 uint32_t tong;
478 uint32_t tong_tho = (uint32_t)kq->raw_r + kq->raw_g + kq->raw_b;
479 
480 /* TRU NEN truoc khi chuan hoa - day la buoc mua them tam xa. Phan con lai
481 la anh sang do CHINH VAT phan xa ve, khong lan anh sang nen. */
482 if (s_tru_nen && s_da_hoc_nen)
483 {
484 uint32_t tong_tru;
485 
486 er = tcs_tru(er, s_nen_tho_r);
487 eg = tcs_tru(eg, s_nen_tho_g);
488 eb = tcs_tru(eb, s_nen_tho_b);
489 
490 /* ⚠ LUOI AN TOAN: neu tru xong ma chi con duoi 1/8 tin hieu goc thi nen
491 da an gan het phan huu ich (nen sang ngang vat, hoac nen do sai luc
492 con vat trong tam nhin). Van mau con lai chi la nhieu - se sinh ra
493 nhung so vo ly kieu r=0 g=0 b=0 hoac b-r=+249.
494 Truong hop do: BO qua tru nen cho lan doc nay, dung so lieu THO. */
495 tong_tru = (uint32_t)er + eg + eb;
496 if (tong_tru < (tong_tho / 8u))
497 {
498 er = kq->raw_r;
499 eg = kq->raw_g;
500 eb = kq->raw_b;
501 }
502 }
503 
504 tong = (uint32_t)er + eg + eb;
505 
506 if (tong == 0u)
507 {
508 kq->r = 0u; kq->g = 0u; kq->b = 0u;
509 kq->d_br = 0; kq->d_bg = 0;
510 return;
511 }
512 kq->r = (uint8_t)(((uint32_t)er * 255u) / tong);
513 kq->g = (uint8_t)(((uint32_t)eg * 255u) / tong);
514 kq->b = (uint8_t)(((uint32_t)eb * 255u) / tong);
515 
516 /* Hai truc quyet dinh. Chuan hoa theo TONG 3 kenh nen d_br/d_bg KHONG doi
517 khi vat xa dan (chi do sang giam), day la ly do chon cach nay thay vi
518 dung so dem tho. */
519 kq->d_br = (int16_t)kq->b - (int16_t)kq->r;
520 kq->d_bg = (int16_t)kq->b - (int16_t)kq->g;
521}
522 
523/* Nguong do sang de ket luan CO VAT: C DUOI muc nay = co vat.
524 Lay theo TI LE cua muc nen dang theo doi chu KHONG dung so tuyet doi, vi
525 anh sang phong co doi (do duoc nen 313 va 566 o hai thoi diem khac nhau). */
526static uint16_t tcs_nguong_co_vat(void)
527{
528 uint32_t ng;
529 
530 /* ★ 14/08/2026: hai chieu dung hai he so khac nhau - xem TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN.
531 chieu 1 (vat TOI hon nen) : he so < 100, co vat khi C DUOI nguong
532 chieu 0 (vat SANG hon nen): he so > 100, co vat khi C TREN nguong */
533#if (TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN != 0u)
534 ng = ((uint32_t)s_c_nen * (uint32_t)TCS_PT_CO_VAT) / 100u;
535#else
536 ng = ((uint32_t)s_c_nen * (uint32_t)TCS_PT_CO_VAT_SANG) / 100u;
537#endif
538 if (ng > 65535u) { ng = 65535u; }
539 return (uint16_t)ng;
540}
541 
542/* Bam theo muc sang cua NEN: LEN NGAY, XUONG CHAM.
543 - Len ngay an toan tuyet doi: vat luon TOI hon nen tren rig nay nen khong
544 co vat nao keo muc nen LEN duoc. Den sang len thi bat kip ngay.
545 - Xuong cham: moi TCS_C_NEN_GIAM_MOI lan doc moi ha 1 don vi, de mot vat
546 nam lau truoc cam bien khong keo tut muc nen xuong ngang chinh no. */
547static void tcs_bam_c_nen(uint16_t raw_c, uint8_t bao_hoa)
548{
549 uint16_t tran = TCS_TranDem();
550 
551 /* ★★★★ SUA 04/08/2026 - LOI THAT: "chay 3-4 san pham roi servo im han".
552 ==========================================================================
553 Ban dau nhanh "len" la: if (raw_c > s_c_nen) s_c_nen = raw_c; - LEN NGAY,
554 KHONG kiem tra gi. Chi mot lan doc loi (nhieu I2C tra ve so rac - da xay
555 ra that, log co day dong MAT LIEN LAC luc servo quay) la s_c_nen bi keo
556 vot len va KET O DO:
557 s_c_nen nhay len 2000 -> nguong = 1000 -> nen that 599 < 1000
558 => moi lan doc deu ra "CO VAT" => KHONG BAO GIO ra TRONG
559 => g_da_roi_xanh khong bao gio bat => may trang thai KET O KHOA
560 => servo im han.
561 Tu thoat mat gan 8 phut (giam 3 don vi/giay); neu so rac la 65535 thi coi
562 nhu khong bao gio. Dung la trieu chung nguoi dung bao.
563 
564 BA CHOT MOI:
565 1) Bo qua lan doc BAO HOA - so lieu khong tin duoc.
566 2) Bo qua raw_c > tran dem cua ATIME hien tai: vuot tran la VO LY VE
567 VAT LY, chac chan la so rac tu bus loi.
568 3) Muon LEN thi phai thay HAI lan doc LIEN TIEP cao hon moc, va chi len
569 toi gia tri NHO HON trong hai lan do. Mot xung dot bien don le
570 khong con keo duoc moc nen di. */
571 if (bao_hoa) { s_c_len_dem = 0u; return; }
572 if (raw_c > tran) { s_c_len_dem = 0u; return; }
573 
574 /* ★★★★ 14/08/2026 - HAI CHIEU, XEM TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN TRONG tcs34725.h.
575 Nguyen tac chung: bo bam duoc phep di NHANH ve phia KHONG CO VAT NAO,
576 va phai di CHAM ve phia co the la vat. Doi chieu sang/toi thi hai phia
577 nay doi cho cho nhau, nen ca ham phai lat theo - neu chi lat phep so
578 sanh o tcs_phan_loai() thi bo bam se BO THEO VAT va moc nen chay mat. */
579#if (TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN != 0u)
580 /* --- Chieu 1: nen la cai SANG => LEN nhanh, XUONG cham --- */
581 if (raw_c > s_c_nen)
582 {
583 if (s_c_len_dem == 0u)
584 {
585 s_c_len_dem = 1u;
586 s_c_len_tam = raw_c; /* ung vien - cho lan doc thu hai xac nhan */
587 return;
588 }
589 /* Da co hai lan lien tiep: len toi gia tri NHO HON (bao thu) */
590 s_c_nen = (raw_c < s_c_len_tam) ? raw_c : s_c_len_tam;
591 s_c_len_dem = 0u;
592 s_c_nen_le = 0u;
593 return;
594 }
595 
596 s_c_len_dem = 0u; /* chuoi len bi dut */
597 
598 if (s_c_nen <= (uint16_t)TCS_C_NEN_TOI_THIEU) { return; }
599 
600 s_c_nen_le++;
601 if (s_c_nen_le >= (uint8_t)TCS_C_NEN_GIAM_MOI)
602 {
603 s_c_nen_le = 0u;
604 s_c_nen--;
605 }
606#else
607 /* --- Chieu 0: nen la cai TOI => XUONG nhanh, LEN cham ---
608 Guong doi xung cua nhanh tren, tung dong mot:
609 - "xuong nhanh" cung phai doi HAI lan doc lien tiep thap hon moc, va
610 chi xuong toi gia tri LON HON trong hai lan (bao thu y het ban goc).
611 - "len cham" bi chan tren boi TRAN DEM thay vi chan duoi boi
612 TCS_C_NEN_TOI_THIEU. */
613 if (raw_c < s_c_nen)
614 {
615 if (s_c_len_dem == 0u)
616 {
617 s_c_len_dem = 1u;
618 s_c_len_tam = raw_c; /* ung vien - cho lan doc thu hai xac nhan */
619 return;
620 }
621 s_c_nen = (raw_c > s_c_len_tam) ? raw_c : s_c_len_tam;
622 s_c_len_dem = 0u;
623 s_c_nen_le = 0u;
624 return;
625 }
626 
627 s_c_len_dem = 0u; /* chuoi xuong bi dut */
628 
629 if (s_c_nen >= tran) { return; } /* khong cho moc nen vuot tran dem */
630 
631 s_c_nen_le++;
632 if (s_c_nen_le >= (uint8_t)TCS_C_NEN_GIAM_MOI)
633 {
634 s_c_nen_le = 0u;
635 s_c_nen++;
636 }
637#endif
638}
639 
640/* Phan loai 3 muc: TRONG / XANH DUONG / MAU KHAC.
641 Buoc 1: co vat khong - do DO SANG quyet dinh (dao chieu, xem trong ham).
642 Buoc 2: neu co vat -> doi HAI dieu kien xanh duong.
643 Buoc 3: co vat ma khong xanh -> MAU KHAC. */
644static TCS_Mau_t tcs_phan_loai(TCS_KetQua_t *kq)
645{
646 /* Bao hoa: mot kenh cham tran => moi mau deu keo ve trang, khong ket luan. */
647 if (kq->bao_hoa)
648 {
649 kq->co_vat = 1u; /* chac chan co gi do rat sang truoc cam bien */
650 kq->la_xanh = 0u;
651 return TCS_KHONG_RO;
652 }
653 
654 /* ★★★★ BUOC 0 - HAI CHOT CHAN SO LIEU RAC (them 14/08/2026).
655 Phai dat TRUOC MOI buoc khac: neu so lieu khong dang tin thi khong duoc
656 phep ket luan BAT CU dieu gi tu no - ke ca ket luan "TRONG".
657 Xem khoi giai thich day du o TCS_TONG_TIN_TOI_THIEU trong tcs34725.h. */
658 {
659 uint32_t tong_tho = (uint32_t)kq->raw_r + kq->raw_g + kq->raw_b;
660 uint16_t kenh_lon = kq->raw_r;
661 
662 if (kq->raw_g > kenh_lon) { kenh_lon = kq->raw_g; }
663 if (kq->raw_b > kenh_lon) { kenh_lon = kq->raw_b; }
664 
665 /* (1) Tin hieu qua yeu - chuan hoa se phong dai nhieu luong tu hoa. */
666 if (tong_tho < (uint32_t)TCS_TONG_TIN_TOI_THIEU)
667 {
668 kq->co_vat = 0u;
669 kq->la_xanh = 0u;
670 s_dang_xanh = 0u;
671 return TCS_KHONG_RO;
672 }
673 
674 /* (2) Kenh mau lon hon han kenh CLEAR => byte hong tren duong I2C.
675 CLEAR khong co kinh loc nen KHONG THE nho hon kenh mau nhieu lan. */
676 if ((uint32_t)kenh_lon > ((uint32_t)kq->raw_c * (uint32_t)TCS_HS_C_HOP_LE))
677 {
678 kq->co_vat = 0u;
679 kq->la_xanh = 0u;
680 s_dang_xanh = 0u;
681 return TCS_KHONG_RO;
682 }
683 }
684 
685 /* --- Buoc 1: CO VAT hay khong - QUYET DINH BANG DO SANG C ---
686 ★★★ DAO CHIEU 03/08/2026 (V2_1). Truoc day: `C < san_toi` => TRONG,
687 tuc gia dinh vat SANG hon nen. Rig nay NGUOC LAI - cam bien nhin vao nen
688 sang, phoi CHE bot no nen vat TOI hon nen:
689 NEN 528/566 | PHOI XANH 190 | PHOI DO 155..236
690 Nay: C >= nguong => TRONG (dang nhin thang vao nen)
691 C < nguong => CO VAT
692 Nguong lay theo TI LE muc nen dang theo doi, khong phai so tuyet doi,
693 vi anh sang phong co doi (nen do duoc 313 va 566 o hai thoi diem).
694 Xem khoi giai thich day du o TCS_PT_CO_VAT trong tcs34725.h.
695 ★ 14/08/2026: phep so sanh nay LAT theo TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN. Dat cong
696 tac do = 0 (sau khi lam man che, neu do ra phoi SANG hon nen) thi cau
697 duoi tu doi thanh "C THAP ngang nen => TRONG".
698 ★★ 14/08/2026 (lan 3): CA KHOI NAY bi bo qua khi
699 TCS_NHAN_VAT_BANG_DO_SANG = 0 - xem khoi giai thich trong tcs34725.h.
700 Voi man che moi, phoi DO chi toi hon nen 1.0% nen truc do sang da chet. */
701#if (TCS_NHAN_VAT_BANG_DO_SANG != 0u)
702#if (TCS_VAT_TOI_HON_NEN != 0u)
703 if (kq->raw_c >= tcs_nguong_co_vat()) /* sang ngang nen => dang nhin nen */
704#else
705 if (kq->raw_c <= tcs_nguong_co_vat()) /* toi ngang nen => dang nhin nen */
706#endif
707 {
708 kq->co_vat = 0u;
709 kq->la_xanh = 0u;
710 s_dang_xanh = 0u; /* vat da di khoi tam nhin -> nha tre */
711 return TCS_TRONG;
712 }
713#endif /* TCS_NHAN_VAT_BANG_DO_SANG */
714 /* --- Buoc 2: co phai XANH DUONG khong --- (XET TRUOC KHI XET NEN!)
715 d_br = b - r >= nguong_br : TRUC QUYET DINH (hut manh anh sang do)
716 d_bg = b - g >= nguong_bg : CHOT CHAN, chi de loai vat XANH LA.
717 Kenh green cua chip nay dem rat nhieu ngay ca tren vat xanh duong, nen
718 d_bg CHI dung lam chot chan chu KHONG lam truc phan biet - xem so lieu
719 do that ghi trong tcs34725.h.
720 
721 ⚠ THU TU QUAN TRONG: phai xet XANH DUONG TRUOC vung nen. Vung nen trai
722 rong ve ca hai phia cua diem nen, nen neu xet nen truoc thi phan MEP
723 DUOI cua dai xanh duong (nam trong vung nen) se bi nuot thanh TRONG va
724 cam bien chi nhan duoc xanh duong tu tren cao han. Xet xanh truoc thi
725 hai vung khong con the chong nhau duoc nua.
726 
727 TRE: neu lan doc truoc da la xanh duong thi noi nguong truc chinh ra
728 TCS_TRE_BR don vi, de vat nam sat ranh gioi khong nhap nhay. */
729 {
730 int16_t nguong_br_hd = s_dang_xanh
731 ? (int16_t)(s_nguong_br - TCS_TRE_BR)
732 : s_nguong_br;
733 int16_t muc_nha_toi_thieu = (int16_t)(s_d_br_nen + TCS_BIEN_NHA_TOI_THIEU);
734 
735 /* ⚠ CHAN CUNG: muc giu tin hieu khong bao gio duoc tut xuong ngang NEN.
736 Neu khong, khi moc nen troi len (da gap: +19 -> +24 -> +29) thi chinh
737 cai nen se du giu BLUE mai khong nha => quet xong vat xanh la dinh
738 luon, vat ra khoi vung khong quay ve TRONG, va vat mau khac phai ap
739 sat moi thoat ra duoc. */
740 if (nguong_br_hd < muc_nha_toi_thieu)
741 {
742 nguong_br_hd = muc_nha_toi_thieu;
743 }
744 
745 if ((kq->d_br >= nguong_br_hd) && (kq->d_bg >= s_nguong_bg))
746 {
747 kq->co_vat = 1u;
748 kq->la_xanh = 1u;
749 s_dang_xanh = 1u;
750 return TCS_XANH_DUONG;
751 }
752 }
753 s_dang_xanh = 0u;
754 
755 /* --- Buoc 3: co vat nhung khong phai xanh duong => MAU KHAC ---
756 ★★★ DA BO 03/08/2026 (V2_1): truoc day o day co mot buoc nua, xet xem
757 van mau co bam sat diem NEN khong (|d_br - d_br_nen| <= TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR
758 va |d_bg - d_bg_nen| <= TCS_BIEN_NEN_BG) thi ket luan TRONG.
759 Buoc do BUOC PHAI BO vi tren rig nay no SAI:
760 NEN : b-r = -9 .. -10 b-g = -11/-12
761 PHOI DO : b-r = -10 .. -6 b-g = -9 /-10
762 Hai cum CHONG NHAU (deu cham -10) => phoi do bi ket luan la TRONG. Da thu
763 siet TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR tu 4 xuong 2 va cua so nen tu bam tu 10 xuong 4,
764 van that bai vi day khong phai van de kich thuoc cua so - hai phan bo
765 that su trung nhau tren truc mau.
766 Nay viec "co vat hay khong" da do DO SANG quyet dinh o buoc 1 (nen 566
767 so voi vat 155..236 - tach bach tuyet doi), nen buoc nay thua va co hai.
768 TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR / TCS_BIEN_NEN_BG gio KHONG con duoc dung o dau nua.
769 
770 ★★★★★ DUNG LAI 14/08/2026 (lan 3) - CO DIEU KIEN ★★★★★
771 Ly do bo no ngay 03/08 la "PHOI DO va NEN chong nhau tren truc mau".
772 Dieu do DUNG voi rig luc do (khong man che). Voi MAN CHE MOI thi so do
773 that da khac han:
774 DO b-r=+20 | NEN b-r=+28 | XANH b-r=+38
775 Ba cum tach bach, va nen dung yen tuyet doi (20/20 lan doc deu +28).
776 Trong khi truc DO SANG thi phoi do chi cach nen 1.0% - da chet.
777 => Dao lai: truc mau song, truc do sang chet. Buoc nay duoc dung lai khi
778 TCS_NHAN_VAT_BANG_DO_SANG = 0.
779 ⚠ Van giu nguyen THU TU: XANH DUONG da duoc xet O TREN, truoc buoc nay.
780 Neu xet vung nen truoc thi mep duoi cua dai xanh se bi nuot thanh
781 TRONG - da giai thich o dau buoc 2. */
782#if (TCS_NHAN_VAT_BANG_DO_SANG == 0u)
783 {
784 int16_t lech_br = (int16_t)(kq->d_br - s_d_br_nen);
785 int16_t lech_bg = (int16_t)(kq->d_bg - s_d_bg_nen);
786 
787 if (lech_br < 0) { lech_br = (int16_t)(-lech_br); }
788 if (lech_bg < 0) { lech_bg = (int16_t)(-lech_bg); }
789 
790 if ((lech_br <= (int16_t)TCS_BIEN_NEN_BR) &&
791 (lech_bg <= (int16_t)TCS_BIEN_NEN_BG))
792 {
793 kq->co_vat = 0u;
794 kq->la_xanh = 0u;
795 s_dang_xanh = 0u; /* vat da di khoi tam nhin -> nha tre */
796 return TCS_TRONG;
797 }
798 }
799#endif
800 
801 kq->co_vat = 1u;
802 kq->la_xanh = 0u;
803 return TCS_MAU_KHAC;
804}
805 
806/* ===================== NEN TU BAM (auto-track) ============================
807 Goi sau moi lan phan loai. Y tuong: vat CHAY LIEN TUC qua cam bien chi nam
808 trong tam nhin duoi 1 giay; con so doc DUNG YEN nhieu giay lien nghia la
809 bang tai dang TRONG => cai dang nhin chinh la nen.
810 Xem day du ba chan an toan trong tcs34725.h. */
811static void tcs_bam_nen(const TCS_KetQua_t *kq, TCS_Mau_t mau_moi)
812{
813 int16_t l_br, l_bg;
814 
815 if (s_bam_nen == 0u) { return; }
816 
817 /* Chan an toan 1 - SIET LAI 03/08/2026 (V2_1): CHI hoc tu nhung lan doc da
818 ket luan la TRONG.
819 Truoc day chot nay chi loai XANH_DUONG / KHONG_RO / qua toi, nen mot vat
820 ket luan MAU KHAC van duoc phep tro thanh ung vien moc nen. Do chinh la
821 duong ma PHOI DO da di de keo moc nen tu -9 sang -6 (loi that, 03/08).
822 Nay "TRONG hay khong" do DO SANG quyet dinh (nen 566 so voi vat 155..236,
823 tach bach tuyet doi) nen dieu kien nay vua chat vua dang tin: moi vat
824 nam truoc cam bien deu bi loai khoi qua trinh hoc, bat ke mau gi. */
825 if (mau_moi != TCS_TRONG)
826 {
827 s_uv_co = 0u;
828 s_uv_dem = 0u;
829 return;
830 }
831 
832 /* Chan an toan 2 - CUA SO TUNG LAN DOC: chi xet nhung lan doc bam quanh
833 moc HIEN TAI. Vat do o +8 cach moc +30 toi 22 don vi => bi loai ngay,
834 khong bao gio tro thanh ung vien. Day la cho chan "bo dan theo vat". */
835 {
836 int16_t xa = (int16_t)(kq->d_br - s_d_br_nen);
837 if (xa < 0) { xa = (int16_t)-xa; }
838 if (xa > TCS_NEN_BAM_CUA_SO)
839 {
840 s_uv_co = 0u;
841 s_uv_dem = 0u;
842 return;
843 }
844 }
845 
846 /* Con dung yen quanh ung vien hien tai khong? */
847 if (s_uv_co)
848 {
849 l_br = (int16_t)(kq->d_br - s_uv_br);
850 l_bg = (int16_t)(kq->d_bg - s_uv_bg);
851 if (l_br < 0) { l_br = (int16_t)-l_br; }
852 if (l_bg < 0) { l_bg = (int16_t)-l_bg; }
853 
854 if ((l_br > TCS_NEN_BAM_DAO_DONG) || (l_bg > TCS_NEN_BAM_DAO_DONG))
855 {
856 s_uv_co = 0u; /* dong roi -> bo ung vien, lam lai */
857 }
858 }
859 
860 if (s_uv_co == 0u)
861 {
862 s_uv_br = kq->d_br;
863 s_uv_bg = kq->d_bg;
864 s_uv_co = 1u;
865 s_uv_dem = 1u;
866 return;
867 }
868 
869 s_uv_dem++;
870 if (s_uv_dem < TCS_NEN_BAM_SO_LAN) { return; }
871 
872 /* Da dung yen du lau => day la nen. */
873 s_uv_dem = 0u;
874 s_uv_co = 0u;
875 
876 if (s_uv_br == s_d_br_nen) { return; } /* khong doi gi -> thoi */
877 
878 /* Chan an toan 3 - TRAN TONG: moc khong duoc di xa qua TCS_NEN_BAM_TONG so
879 voi NEO (gia tri bake, hoac gia tri do duoc lan go 'b' gan nhat).
880 Day la chan CUOI CUNG chong kieu "bo dan": du bo tung buoc nho bao nhieu
881 lan, tong duong di van bi giu trong tran nay. Khong co no thi vat dung
882 yen lau truoc cam bien se keo moc nen di theo minh. */
883 l_br = (int16_t)(s_uv_br - s_d_br_neo);
884 if (l_br < 0) { l_br = (int16_t)-l_br; }
885 if (l_br > TCS_NEN_BAM_TONG) { return; }
886 
887 /* Chan an toan 3: doi HAI nguong theo dung luong moc dich, de moi hieu
888 chinh tay bang lenh 'e' van duoc giu nguyen. */
889 s_nguong_br = (int16_t)(s_nguong_br + (s_uv_br - s_d_br_nen));
890 s_d_br_nen = s_uv_br;
891 s_d_bg_nen = s_uv_bg;
892 s_so_lan_bam++;
893}
894 
895/* ================================ API ==================================== */
896 
897uint16_t TCS_ThoiGianTichPhan_ms(void)
898{
899 uint32_t b = (uint32_t)(256u - s_atime);
900 return (uint16_t)(((b * 24u) + 9u) / 10u); /* (256-ATIME)x2.4ms, lam tron len */
901}
902 
903uint16_t TCS_TranDem(void)
904{
905 uint32_t tran = (uint32_t)(256u - s_atime) * 1024u;
906 return (tran > 65535u) ? 65535u : (uint16_t)tran;
907}
908 
909uint8_t TCS_LayGain(void) { return s_gain; }
910uint8_t TCS_LayTichPhan(void) { return s_atime; }
911 
912void TCS_DatSoMauLoc(uint8_t n)
913{
914 if (n < 1u) { n = 1u; }
915 if (n > TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX) { n = TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX; }
916 if ((n % 2u) == 0u) { n++; } /* phai la so LE */
917 if (n > TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX) { n = TCS_SO_MAU_LOC_MAX; }
918 s_so_mau = n;
919}
920 
921uint8_t TCS_LaySoMauLoc(void) { return s_so_mau; }
922 
923void TCS_BatBamNen(uint8_t bat)
924{
925 s_bam_nen = (uint8_t)(bat != 0u);
926 s_uv_co = 0u;
927 s_uv_dem = 0u;
928}
929 
930uint8_t TCS_LayBamNen(void) { return s_bam_nen; }
931uint16_t TCS_LaySoLanBamNen(void) { return s_so_lan_bam; }
932 
933const char *TCS_TenGain(uint8_t gain)
934{
935 switch (gain & 0x03u)
936 {
937 case 0u: return "1x";
938 case 1u: return "4x";
939 case 2u: return "16x";
940 default: return "60x";
941 }
942}
943 
944uint8_t TCS_DatGain(uint8_t gain)
945{
946 gain &= 0x03u;
947 if (tcs_ghi8(REG_CONTROL, gain) == 0u) { return 0u; }
948 s_gain = gain;
949 return 1u;
950}
951 
952uint8_t TCS_DatTichPhan(uint8_t atime)
953{
954 if (tcs_ghi8(REG_ATIME, atime) == 0u) { return 0u; }
955 s_atime = atime;
956 return 1u;
957}
958 
959/* Nap lai ATIME / AGAIN / ENABLE sau khi cuu bus. Ghi THANG bang HAL, khong
960 qua tcs_ghi8, de khong lam khoi dong lai chinh thu tuc cuu bus. */
961static void tcs_nap_lai_cau_hinh(void)
962{
963 uint8_t gt;
964 
965 if (s_hi2c == NULL) { return; }
966 
967 gt = s_atime;
968 (void)HAL_I2C_Mem_Write(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
969 (uint16_t)(CMD | REG_ATIME), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
970 &gt, 1u, I2C_TIMEOUT);
971 gt = s_gain;
972 (void)HAL_I2C_Mem_Write(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
973 (uint16_t)(CMD | REG_CONTROL), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
974 &gt, 1u, I2C_TIMEOUT);
975 gt = (uint8_t)(EN_PON | EN_AEN);
976 (void)HAL_I2C_Mem_Write(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1),
977 (uint16_t)(CMD | REG_ENABLE), I2C_MEMADD_SIZE_8BIT,
978 &gt, 1u, I2C_TIMEOUT);
979}
980 
981void TCS_DatHamPhucVu(TCS_HamPhucVu_t ham)
982{
983 s_ham_phuc_vu = ham;
984}
985 
986uint8_t TCS_Init(I2C_HandleTypeDef *hi2c)
987{
988 uint8_t id = 0u;
989 
990 s_hi2c = hi2c;
991 
992 /* Bat nguon noi bo truoc, cho 3ms (yeu cau cua chip) roi bat bo do */
993 if (tcs_ghi8(REG_ENABLE, EN_PON) == 0u) { return 0u; }
994 tcs_cho_phuc_vu(3u); /* 03/08/2026: cu la HAL_Delay chan cung */
995 
996 if (tcs_doc8(REG_ID, &id) == 0u) { return 0u; }
997 if ((id != ID_TCS34725) && (id != ID_TCS34727)) { return 0u; }
998 
999 if (TCS_DatTichPhan(TCS_ATIME_MAC_DINH) == 0u) { return 0u; }
1000 if (TCS_DatGain(TCS_GAIN_MAC_DINH) == 0u) { return 0u; }
1001 if (tcs_ghi8(REG_WTIME, 0xFFu) == 0u) { return 0u; }
1002 
1003 if (tcs_ghi8(REG_ENABLE, (uint8_t)(EN_PON | EN_AEN)) == 0u) { return 0u; }
1004 tcs_cho_phuc_vu((uint32_t)TCS_ThoiGianTichPhan_ms() + 5u);
1005 
1006 return 1u;
1007}
1008 
1009uint8_t TCS_QuetBus(TCS_ChanDoan_t *cd)
1010{
1011 uint8_t addr;
1012 
1013 if ((s_hi2c == NULL) || (cd == NULL)) { return 0u; }
1014 
1015 cd->so_tb = 0u;
1016 for (addr = 0x08u; addr <= 0x77u; addr++)
1017 {
1018 if (HAL_I2C_IsDeviceReady(s_hi2c, (uint16_t)(addr << 1), 2u, 5u) == HAL_OK)
1019 {
1020 if (cd->so_tb < 8u)
1021 {
1022 cd->dia_chi[cd->so_tb] = addr;
1023 }
1024 cd->so_tb++;
1025 }
1026 }
1027 return cd->so_tb;
1028}
1029 
1030uint8_t TCS_KiemTraKetNoi(TCS_ChanDoan_t *cd)
1031{
1032 uint16_t lon, nho;
1033 
1034 if ((s_hi2c == NULL) || (cd == NULL)) { return 0u; }
1035 
1036 cd->id = 0u;
1037 cd->c = 0u; cd->r = 0u; cd->g = 0u; cd->b = 0u;
1038 cd->tran = TCS_TranDem();
1039 cd->ok_id = 0u; cd->ok_doc = 0u; cd->ok_sang = 0u;
1040 cd->ok_khong_bh = 0u; cd->ok_tach_mau = 0u;
1041 
1042 /* --- [1] Bus: co thiet bi nao ACK o 0x29 khong --- */
1043 TCS_QuetBus(cd);
1044 cd->ok_bus = (HAL_I2C_IsDeviceReady(s_hi2c,
1045 (uint16_t)(TCS_DIA_CHI_7BIT << 1), 3u, 10u) == HAL_OK)
1046 ? 1u : 0u;
1047 if (cd->ok_bus == 0u)
1048 {
1049 cd->du_dieu_kien = 0u;
1050 return 0u;
1051 }
1052 
1053 /* --- [2] Dung chip TCS34725 khong (thanh ghi ID) --- */
1054 if (tcs_doc8(REG_ID, &cd->id))
1055 {
1056 cd->ok_id = ((cd->id == ID_TCS34725) || (cd->id == ID_TCS34727)) ? 1u : 0u;
1057 }
1058 
1059 /* --- [3] Bat bo do va doc duoc mot chu ky moi --- */
1060 if (tcs_do_moi())
1061 {
1062 cd->ok_doc = tcs_doc_rgbc(&cd->c, &cd->r, &cd->g, &cd->b);
1063 }
1064 
1065 if (cd->ok_doc)
1066 {
1067 /* --- [4] Du sang: C phai vuot han muc nhieu nen --- */
1068 cd->ok_sang = (cd->c > 50u) ? 1u : 0u;
1069 
1070 /* --- [5] Khong bao hoa --- */
1071 cd->ok_khong_bh =
1072 (cd->c < (uint16_t)(((uint32_t)cd->tran * TCS_MUC_BAO_HOA_PT) / 100u))
1073 ? 1u : 0u;
1074 
1075 /* --- [6] 3 kenh phai khac nhau it nhat mot chut --- */
1076 lon = cd->r; nho = cd->r;
1077 if (cd->g > lon) { lon = cd->g; } if (cd->g < nho) { nho = cd->g; }
1078 if (cd->b > lon) { lon = cd->b; } if (cd->b < nho) { nho = cd->b; }
1079 cd->ok_tach_mau = (lon > nho) ? 1u : 0u;
1080 }
1081 
1082 /* Ba muc dau la bat buoc; 4-5-6 chi la canh bao. */
1083 cd->du_dieu_kien = (uint8_t)(cd->ok_bus && cd->ok_id && cd->ok_doc);
1084 return cd->du_dieu_kien;
1085}
1086 
1087uint16_t TCS_DocClear(void)
1088{
1089 uint16_t c = 0u, r = 0u, g = 0u, b = 0u;
1090 
1091 if (tcs_do_moi() == 0u) { return 0u; }
1092 if (tcs_doc_rgbc(&c, &r, &g, &b) == 0u) { return 0u; }
1093 return c;
1094}
1095 
1096uint8_t TCS_Doc(TCS_KetQua_t *kq)
1097{
1098 static TCS_Mau_t mau_truoc = TCS_TRONG; /* bo loc on dinh 2 lan doc */
1099 static TCS_Mau_t mau_chot = TCS_TRONG;
1100 static uint8_t dem_khac = 0u; /* so lan lien tiep khac mau_chot */
1101 uint16_t nguong_bh, lon;
1102 TCS_Mau_t mau_moi;
1103 
1104 if (s_xoa_loc) /* TCS_XoaLoc() da duoc goi -> quen vat cu */
1105 {
1106 mau_truoc = TCS_TRONG;
1107 mau_chot = TCS_TRONG;
1108 dem_khac = 0u;
1109 s_dang_xanh = 0u; /* nha luon TRE de do vat moi cho sach */
1110 s_xoa_loc = 0u;
1111 }
1112 
1113 if ((s_hi2c == NULL) || (kq == NULL)) { return 0u; }
1114 
1115 if (tcs_doc_rgbc_loc(&kq->raw_c, &kq->raw_r, &kq->raw_g, &kq->raw_b) == 0u)
1116 {
1117 return 0u;
1118 }
1119 
1120 /* Bao hoa: xet CA kenh C LAN kenh mau lon nhat (nhua bong co the lam mot
1121 kenh cham tran truoc ca C). */
1122 nguong_bh = (uint16_t)(((uint32_t)TCS_TranDem() * TCS_MUC_BAO_HOA_PT) / 100u);
1123 lon = kq->raw_r;
1124 if (kq->raw_g > lon) { lon = kq->raw_g; }
1125 if (kq->raw_b > lon) { lon = kq->raw_b; }
1126 kq->bao_hoa = ((kq->raw_c >= nguong_bh) || (lon >= nguong_bh)) ? 1u : 0u;
1127 
1128 tcs_chuan_hoa(kq);
1129 
1130 /* Bam muc sang cua nen TRUOC khi phan loai: nguong "co vat" o buoc 1 duoc
1131 tinh tu muc nay. Vat toi hon nen nen no chi keo muc nen xuong, va rat
1132 cham - xem tcs_bam_c_nen(). */
1133 tcs_bam_c_nen(kq->raw_c, kq->bao_hoa);
1134 
1135 mau_moi = tcs_phan_loai(kq);
1136 tcs_bam_nen(kq, mau_moi); /* tu cap nhat moc nen khi bang tai trong */
1137 
1138 if (mau_moi == mau_chot)
1139 {
1140 dem_khac = 0u;
1141 }
1142 else
1143 {
1144 dem_khac++;
1145 /* Doi ket luan khi: 2 lan doc lien tiep giong nhau (nhanh, nhu cu), HOAC
1146 da co TCS_SO_LAN_EP_DOI lan lien tiep khac voi ket luan dang chot.
1147 Ve sau chong duoc truong hop so doc dao dong qua lai giua hai nhom lam
1148 ket luan ket cung mai o gia tri cu (vat da di khoi vung ma van bao
1149 XANH DUONG). */
1150 if ((mau_moi == mau_truoc) || (dem_khac >= TCS_SO_LAN_EP_DOI))
1151 {
1152 mau_chot = mau_moi;
1153 dem_khac = 0u;
1154 }
1155 }
1156 mau_truoc = mau_moi;
1157 kq->mau = mau_chot;
1158 
1159 return 1u;
1160}
1161 
1162uint8_t TCS_LaXanhDuong(const TCS_KetQua_t *kq)
1163{
1164 return ((kq != NULL) && (kq->mau == TCS_XANH_DUONG)) ? 1u : 0u;
1165}
1166 
1167const char *TCS_TenMau(TCS_Mau_t m)
1168{
1169 switch (m)
1170 {
1171 case TCS_XANH_DUONG: return "XANH DUONG (BLUE) <== TIN HIEU";
1172 case TCS_MAU_KHAC: return "MAU KHAC (co vat, loai ra) ";
1173 case TCS_KHONG_RO: return "KHONG RO (bao hoa) ";
1174 default: return "NEN/TRONG (khong co vat) ";
1175 }
1176}
1177 
1178void TCS_DoNhayToiDa(void)
1179{
1180 /* Vat cach 10-15cm + chi co 4 den nho tren module => luon can do nhay cao
1181 nhat. Khong "tu do" gain nhu ban cu: o day gain THAP dong nghia voi mat
1182 tin hieu o xa. */
1183 (void)TCS_DatTichPhan(TCS_ATIME_MAC_DINH); /* 154 ms */
1184 (void)TCS_DatGain(3u); /* 60x */
1185}
1186 
1187uint8_t TCS_ChongBaoHoa(void)
1188{
1189 /* CHI ha gain khi thuc su cham tran (vat qua gan / phong qua sang).
1190 Khong bao gio tu tang gain. */
1191 uint32_t nguong_bh = ((uint32_t)TCS_TranDem() * TCS_MUC_BAO_HOA_PT) / 100u;
1192 uint8_t lan;
1193 
1194 for (lan = 0u; lan < 3u; lan++)
1195 {
1196 uint16_t c = TCS_DocClear();
1197 
1198 if ((c >= (uint16_t)nguong_bh) && (s_gain > 0u))
1199 {
1200 TCS_DatGain((uint8_t)(s_gain - 1u));
1201 continue;
1202 }
1203 break;
1204 }
1205 return s_gain;
1206}
1207 
1208uint8_t TCS_HocSanToi(TCS_KetQua_t *kq)
1209{
1210 uint32_t san;
1211 uint8_t tru_cu = s_tru_nen;
1212 
1213 if (kq == NULL) { return 0u; }
1214 
1215 /* Do canh "TRONG" that (khong co vat) - co loc trung vi cho on dinh.
1216 TAT tru nen trong luc do, neu khong se do canh trong SAU KHI da tru
1217 chinh no => ra so vo nghia. */
1218 s_tru_nen = 0u;
1219 TCS_XoaLoc();
1220 if (TCS_Doc(kq) == 0u)
1221 {
1222 s_tru_nen = tru_cu;
1223 return 0u;
1224 }
1225 
1226 s_c_trong = kq->raw_c;
1227 
1228 /* Ghi nho so dem THO cua nen -> dung cho tru nen. */
1229 s_nen_tho_r = kq->raw_r;
1230 s_nen_tho_g = kq->raw_g;
1231 s_nen_tho_b = kq->raw_b;
1232 s_da_hoc_nen = 1u;
1233 
1234 /* ⚠ DANH GIA NEN truoc khi quyet dinh lam gi voi no. */
1235#if (TCS_NGUONG_NEN_TOI == 0u)
1236 /* RESET 03/08/2026: nguong = 0 nghia la CHUA HIEU CHINH -> LUON coi la nen
1237 sang -> khong bao gio tru nen. Viet bang #if thay vi so sanh de tranh
1238 canh bao #186-D (so sanh unsigned voi 0 luon dung). Dat lai
1239 TCS_NGUONG_NEN_TOI khac 0 thi doan duoi tu dong duoc dung lai. */
1240 (void)s_c_trong;
1241 s_nen_sang = 1u;
1242#else
1243 s_nen_sang = (uint8_t)(s_c_trong >= TCS_NGUONG_NEN_TOI);
1244#endif
1245 
1246 /* ★★ QUAN TRONG NHAT: ghi nho d_br CUA CHINH CANH TRONG, roi dat nguong
1247 nam TREN no mot bien an toan. Nen trong o rig nay da cho b-r = +29 (khong
1248 he trung tinh - cam bien co thien lech mau san). Nguong ma nam duoi so
1249 do thi cam bien bao XANH DUONG ngay ca khi khong co gi truoc mat.
1250 Hoc theo cach nay thi nguong luon dung voi rig + anh sang hien tai,
1251 khong phu thuoc hang so bake trong tcs34725.h. */
1252 s_d_br_nen = kq->d_br;
1253 s_d_bg_nen = kq->d_bg;
1254 s_d_br_neo = kq->d_br; /* dat lai NEO cho nen tu bam */
1255 s_uv_co = 0u; /* bo ung vien dang theo doi dang do */
1256 s_uv_dem = 0u;
1257 {
1258 int32_t ng = (int32_t)s_d_br_nen + (int32_t)TCS_BIEN_TREN_NEN;
1259 
1260 if (ng < TCS_NGUONG_BR_MIN) { ng = TCS_NGUONG_BR_MIN; }
1261 if (ng > TCS_NGUONG_BR_MAX) { ng = TCS_NGUONG_BR_MAX; }
1262 s_nguong_br = (int16_t)ng;
1263 }
1264 
1265 if (s_nen_sang)
1266 {
1267 /* NEN SANG (vach/ban/nen phia sau phan xa manh, sang ngang vat).
1268 - KHONG tru nen: se an gan het tin hieu cua vat.
1269 - Do sang KHONG con phan biet duoc co vat hay khong (nen va vat cho C
1270 xap xi nhau). San toi ha xuong chi con lam muc chan NHIEU, viec co
1271 vat hay khong de cho MAU quyet dinh. */
1272 uint32_t san_nhieu = (uint32_t)s_c_trong / TCS_CHIA_SAN_TOI_NEN_SANG;
1273 
1274 if (san_nhieu < TCS_SAN_TOI_TOI_THIEU) { san_nhieu = TCS_SAN_TOI_TOI_THIEU; }
1275 s_san_toi = (uint16_t)san_nhieu;
1276 s_tru_nen = 0u;
1277 
1278 TCS_XoaLoc();
1279 TCS_Doc(kq);
1280 return 1u; /* khong chay tiep phan tinh san toi duoi */
1281 }
1282 
1283 s_tru_nen = 1u; /* nen toi that -> tru nen an toan, bat len */
1284 
1285 /* San toi phai NAM TREN muc trong mot bien an toan, nhung khong qua cao
1286 keo theo vat mau toi bi bo sot. */
1287 san = (((uint32_t)s_c_trong * TCS_HS_SAN_TOI) / 100u) + TCS_CONG_SAN_TOI;
1288 if (san > 65535u) { san = 65535u; }
1289 s_san_toi = (uint16_t)san;
1290 
1291 /* Doc lai chinh canh trong => phai ra TCS_TRONG. */
1292 TCS_XoaLoc();
1293 TCS_Doc(kq);
1294 
1295 return 1u;
1296}

1367 that). Chan duoi -60 vi duoi nua la bo han chot chan chong xanh la. */

1373uint8_t TCS_HocXanhDuong(int16_t d_br, int16_t d_bg)
1374{
1375 int32_t br, bg;
1376 
1377 /* Vat hoc phai THUC SU la xanh duong tren TRUC CHINH: b hon han r.
1378 KHONG doi d_bg duong - tren chip nay vat xanh duong o XA cho d_bg AM
1379 (-7..-11 do that) vi kenh green dem nhieu. Chi xet truc chinh. */
1380 if (d_br <= 0)
1381 {
1382 return 0u;
1383 }
1384 
1385 /* ⚠ CHAN RAC: vat xanh duong that o rig nay cho d_br = +14..+62. Tren
1386 +120 nghia la so lieu da hong (thuong do tru nen an mat hai kenh kia,
1387 con lai b gan het 255). Hoc cai do se ghi de nguong bang so vo ly va
1388 lam cam bien khong con nhan duoc gi. */
1389 if (d_br > TCS_HOC_BR_TOI_DA)
1390 {
1391 return 0u;
1392 }
1393 
1394 br = ((int32_t)d_br * (int32_t)TCS_HS_HOC) / 100;
1395 if (br < TCS_NGUONG_BR_MIN) { br = TCS_NGUONG_BR_MIN; }
1396 if (br > TCS_NGUONG_BR_MAX) { br = TCS_NGUONG_BR_MAX; }
1397 
1398 /* Truc phu chi la chot chan: lay d_bg do duoc tru mot bien RONG, vi d_bg
1399 lao doc theo khoang cach (+10 luc gan -> -9 luc xa tren cung mot vat). */
1400 bg = (int32_t)d_bg - (int32_t)TCS_BIEN_HOC_BG;
1401 if (bg > (int32_t)TCS_NGUONG_BG_MAC_DINH) { bg = (int32_t)TCS_NGUONG_BG_MAC_DINH; }
1402 if (bg < -60) { bg = -60; }
1403 
1404 s_nguong_br = (int16_t)br;
1405 s_nguong_bg = (int16_t)bg;
1406 return 1u;
1407}

1/**
2 ******************************************************************************
3 * @file servo_mg996r.h
4 * @brief Driver servo RC MG996R cho STM32F103C8T6 (TIM3_CH1 = PA6)
5 *
6 * NGUYEN LY: servo RC nhan xung PWM chu ky 20ms (50Hz). Do rong xung quyet
7 * dinh goc truc: ~1000us = 0 do, ~1500us = 90 do, ~2000us = 180 do.
8 * TIM3 duoc dat Prescaler=71 => 1 tick = 1us, Period=19999 => chu ky 20ms,
9 * nen thanh ghi CCR1 chinh la do rong xung tinh bang micro giay.
10 *
11 * CHONG GIAT DONG (SLEW): KHONG dat goc dot ngot. Moi lan goi Servo_Task()
12 * do rong xung chi doi toi da SERVO_BUOC_US, nho vay servo quay muot va
13 * khong rut dong dot bien lam sut nguon (loi da gap o cac khoi truoc).

15 * ⚠ NGUON: MG996R co dong chet may ~2.5A - PHAI cap nguon rieng
16 * (LM2596 ha 24V xuong 6V), TUYET DOI khong lay 5V tu ST-Link.
17 * Chi noi chung GND voi STM32.

32/* Gioi han do rong xung (us).
33 ⚠⚠ SUA 31/07/2026 - LOI DA GAP: "cap nguon servo quay loan / dung im, an
34 nut RESET cung khong ve home".
35 NGUYEN NHAN: dai cu 600-2400us anh xa 0-180 do, nen GOC_NGHI_DO=180 (home)
36 sinh ra xung 2400us. MG996R thuc te CHAM CHAN CO KHI o khoang 2000-2100us.
37 Ra lenh 2400us la bat servo EP vao chan co va GIU MAI o do => rut dong chet
38 may ~2.5A lien tuc => sut ap 6V => servo rung giat (nhin nhu "quay loan"),
39 LM2596 vao bao ve hoac servo vao bao ve nhiet (nhin nhu "dung im"), va
40 sau khi RESET firmware lai phat dung 2400us nen lap lai y het - dung la
41 trieu chung "an reset khong ve home".
42 Thao canh gat chay khong tai VAN loi, vi servo ep vao chan co BEN TRONG.
43 
44 DAI MOI 1000-2000us = chuan RC pho thong, nam GON trong tam co khi:
45 goc 90 do -> 1500us (KHONG DOI so voi ban cu - vi tri GAT giu nguyen)
46 goc 180 do -> 2000us (truoc la 2400us - het ep chan co)
47 ⚠ Vi tri HOME se dich mot chut so voi truoc. Neu canh gat lech, chinh
48 GOC_NGHI_DO trong main.c (vd 170 hoac 175) chu KHONG nang lai so 2000. */
49#define SERVO_XUNG_MIN_US 1000u
50#define SERVO_XUNG_MAX_US 2000u
51 
52/* ★★★★ SUA 03/08/2026 - VIEC TRUOT DA CHUYEN VAO NGAT UPDATE CUA TIM3 ★★★★
53 ==========================================================================
54 TRIEU CHUNG DA GAP: servo "giat nhe va bi cham" khi quay.
55 NGUYEN NHAN THAT: Servo_Task() truot theo kieu HOP TAC, chay nho vong lap
56 chinh. Khi vong lap bi chan (doc I2C, va nang nhat la TCS_CuuBus() voi cac
57 HAL_Delay chan cung), servo khong duoc phuc vu. Co che bu thoi gian thuc
58 lai bi KEP o SERVO_BU_TOI_DA = 3 buoc, nen phan qua han BI MAT LUON:
59 vong lap bi chan 200ms -> dang le di 400us -> chi duoc di 60us
60 => hanh trinh keo dai ra (CHAM), va vi bi chan khong deu nen nhich-dung-
61 nhich (GIAT). Ca hai trieu chung cung mot goc.
62 
63 NAY: TIM3 dang phat PWM 50Hz (khung 20ms). Dung luon NGAT UPDATE cua no de
64 truot DUNG MOT BUOC MOI KHUNG PWM. Mien nhiem hoan toan voi vong lap bi
65 chan - ke ca luc dang cuu bus I2C.
66 Cap nhat do rong xung dung 1 lan moi khung cung la cach DUNG ve ban chat:
67 servo chi "nhin thay" mot xung moi 20ms, cap nhat day hon la vo nghia.
68 
69 SERVO_BUOC_US LA "us MOI KHUNG PWM 20ms" (truoc ban ngat la moi 10ms).
70 
71 ★★ SUA 04/08/2026: 40 -> 25. LOI DA GAP THAT: "gat xong, luc VE HOME thi
72 servo giat nhe" - o CA hai chu trinh xanh va do.
73 NGUYEN NHAN (tinh ra bang so, khong phai doan):
74 40us/khung / 20ms = 7.2 do moi khung = 360 do/giay
75 MG996R o 6V quay toi da khoang 430 do/giay KHI KHONG TAI. Tuc la lenh
76 360 do/giay chi con ~16% bien - va bien do BIEN MAT khi co tai canh gat.
77 Servo bi lenh di nhanh hon kha nang thuc thi vong dieu khien ben trong no
78 bao hoa: vi tri thuc tut lai roi giat len bam kip => "giat nhe".
79 VI SAO CHI CHIEU VE MOI GIAT: toc do toi da THUC phu thuoc huong so voi
80 tai. Chieu 180->0 duoc trong luc/quan tinh giup nen con bam kip; chieu
81 0->180 phai keo canh gat nguoc lai nen tut duoi nguong va lo ra.
82 
83 Da thu ha xuong 25us (=225 do/giay, ~800ms ca dai) thi HET GIAT, nhung
84 nguoi dung thay CHAM QUA nen 04/08/2026 DA TRA LAI 40us.
85 ⚠⚠ VI VAY: giat nhe luc ve home la HE QUA DA BIET va DA CHAP NHAN cua
86 viec chay 500ms, KHONG phai loi moi. Dung di tim nguyen nhan khac.
87 Muon het giat ma van gat nhanh thi cach dung la TACH TOC DO THEO CHIEU:
88 giu 40us cho chieu di gat (180->0, duoc trong luc giup nen bam kip) va
89 dung ~25us cho chieu ve home (0->180, phai keo nguoc nen hay tut). Xem
90 truot_mot_buoc() trong servo_mg996r.c - cho do da biet huong roi.
91 
92 ★★ SUA 04/08/2026: so nay CHI CON LA GIA TRI KHOI DONG. Tu ban nay, toc do
93 duoc dat RIENG CHO TUNG CHANG bang Servo_DatTocDo() - xem bang BUOC_* trong
94 main.c. Ly do: nguoi dung muon chu trinh XANH em ca hai chieu, con chu
95 trinh DO thi ra 0 do NHANH nhung ve home tu tu.
96 
97 Bang quy doi (moi khung PWM = 20ms, ca dai 180 do = 1000us):
98 15us -> ~1.3s | 20us -> ~1.0s | 25us -> ~0.8s (em, da kiem chung)
99 40us -> ~0.5s (nhanh, CO giat nhe o chieu phai keo nguoc tai)
100 ⚠ Quay nhanh hon = dong dinh cao hon = de lam sut ap gay mat I2C hon.
101 ⚠ So buoc PHAI chia het 1000 thi buoc cuoi moi rot DUNG goc dich, khong du
102 mot chut nao: 10/20/25/40/50/100. Tranh cac so nhu 30 hay 35. */
103#define SERVO_BUOC_US 25u
104 
105/* ⚠ CHI CON DUNG cho duong DU PHONG trong Servo_Task() (khi ngat TIM3 vi ly
106 do nao do khong chay). Duong chinh khong dung so nay nua.
107 Dat 20 (= mot khung PWM) de duong du phong chay DUNG BANG toc do cua
108 duong chinh: 40us moi 20ms. De 10 nhu cu thi luc chay du phong servo se
109 di nhanh GAP DOI thiet ke. */

114 ⚠ PHAI NHO. Tran cu la 20 => 20 x 20us = 400us mot nhip, ma hanh trinh
115 gat chi 900us => servo nhay gan nua quang duong trong mot chu ky PWM,
116 nhin nhu QUAY LOAN. Day dung la loi da gap tren rig.
117 3 buoc = 60us: van bu duoc nhung khuc chan ngan, khong the nhay thay ro.
118 Bi chan lau hon thi servo chi di CHAM hon chu khong nhay coc. */
119#define SERVO_BU_TOI_DA 3u

30static uint16_t goc_sang_xung(uint8_t goc)
31{

33 {
34 goc = 180u;
35 }
36 return (uint16_t)(SERVO_XUNG_MIN_US +
37 (((uint32_t)goc * (SERVO_XUNG_MAX_US - SERVO_XUNG_MIN_US)) / 180u));

41static void xuat_xung(uint16_t us)

44 if (us > SERVO_XUNG_MAX_US) { us = SERVO_XUNG_MAX_US; }
45 s_xung_ht = us;
46 __HAL_TIM_SET_COMPARE(s_htim, s_channel, us);
47}

49/* ---------- truot DUNG MOT BUOC ve phia goc dich ----------

56 doi giua chung */
57 
58 if (s_xung_ht == s_xung_dich)
59 {
60 return;
61 }
62 
63 if (s_xung_dich > s_xung_ht)
64 {
65 con_lai = (uint16_t)(s_xung_dich - s_xung_ht);
66 if (con_lai > buoc) { con_lai = buoc; }
67 xuat_xung((uint16_t)(s_xung_ht + con_lai));
68 }
69 else
70 {
71 con_lai = (uint16_t)(s_xung_ht - s_xung_dich);
72 if (con_lai > buoc) { con_lai = buoc; }
73 xuat_xung((uint16_t)(s_xung_ht - con_lai));
74 }
75}
76 
77/* ---------- NGAT UPDATE CUA TIM3 - moi khung PWM 20ms mot lan ----------
78 ⚠ Ham nay dat o day (trong driver servo) chu KHONG o stm32f1xx_it.c, vi
79 ngat TIM3 KHONG duoc bat trong .ioc nen CubeMX khong sinh ra no. Nho vay
80 sinh lai code tu CubeMX cung khong ghi de mat.
81 ⚠ Neu sau nay co ai bat "TIM3 global interrupt" trong CubeMX thi se co
82 HAI dinh nghia TIM3_IRQHandler -> loi linker "multiply defined". Luc do
83 XOA ham nay va goi truot_mot_buoc() tu handler cua CubeMX. */
84void TIM3_IRQHandler(void)
85{
86 if (s_htim == NULL)
87 {
88 return;
89 }
90 
91 if ((__HAL_TIM_GET_FLAG(s_htim, TIM_FLAG_UPDATE) != RESET) &&
92 (__HAL_TIM_GET_IT_SOURCE(s_htim, TIM_IT_UPDATE) != RESET))
93 {
94 __HAL_TIM_CLEAR_IT(s_htim, TIM_IT_UPDATE);
95 s_ngat_moc = HAL_GetTick();
96 s_ngat_song = 1u;
97 truot_mot_buoc();
98 }
99}
100 
101/* ================================ API ==================================== */
102 
103void Servo_Init(TIM_HandleTypeDef *htim, uint32_t channel, uint8_t goc_ban_dau)
104{
105 s_htim = htim;
106 s_channel = channel;
107 
108 /* ⚠ PHAI kep o day GIONG Servo_DatGoc(). goc_sang_xung() co kep ben trong
109 nen do rong xung luon dung, nhung neu khong kep bien s_goc_dich thi
110 Servo_GocDich() se bao ra mot goc KHONG khop voi xung dang phat - so
111 lieu chan doan in ra terminal se noi doi. */
112 if (goc_ban_dau > 180u)
113 {
114 goc_ban_dau = 180u;
115 }
116 
117 s_goc_dich = goc_ban_dau;
118 s_xung_dich = goc_sang_xung(goc_ban_dau);
119 
120 /* Dat xung TRUOC khi bat PWM: xung dau tien phat ra da dung goc nghi,
121 servo khong quat manh luc cap nguon. */
122 xuat_xung(s_xung_dich);
123 HAL_TIM_PWM_Start(s_htim, s_channel);
124 
125 s_tick = HAL_GetTick();
126 
127 /* ★ Bat ngat update TIM3 de viec truot chay trong ngat, khong phu thuoc
128 vong lap chinh nua. Lam bang code chu KHONG qua CubeMX, nen .ioc giu
129 nguyen va sinh lai code cung khong mat.
130 Do uu tien 5: THAP hon cac ngat he thong nhung CAO hon SysTick (15) -
131 than ngat chi vai chuc chu ky nen khong lam troi dong ho HAL. */
132 __HAL_TIM_CLEAR_IT(s_htim, TIM_IT_UPDATE);
133 __HAL_TIM_ENABLE_IT(s_htim, TIM_IT_UPDATE);
134 HAL_NVIC_SetPriority(TIM3_IRQn, 5u, 0u);
135 HAL_NVIC_EnableIRQ(TIM3_IRQn);
136}
137 
138void Servo_DatGoc(uint8_t goc)
139{
140 if (goc > 180u)
141 {
142 goc = 180u;
143 }
144 s_goc_dich = goc;
145 s_xung_dich = goc_sang_xung(goc);
146}
147 
148void Servo_DatTocDo(uint8_t buoc_us)
149{
150 s_buoc_us = (buoc_us == 0u) ? 1u : buoc_us; /* 0 se lam servo dung han */
151}
152 
153void Servo_Task(void)
154{
155 uint32_t now;
156 uint32_t buoc_lan_nay; /* luon duoc gan trong khoi tinh bu ben duoi */
157 
158 if (s_htim == NULL)
159 {
160 return;
161 }
162 
163 now = HAL_GetTick();
164 
165 /* ★★ DUONG CHINH GIO LA NGAT TIM3 (xem TIM3_IRQHandler o tren).
166 Neu ngat dang song thi ham nay KHONG lam gi - de no truot tiep se thanh
167 truot HAI LAN moi khung, servo di nhanh gap doi so thiet ke.
168 Coi la "con song" neu co ngat trong vong 100ms gan day (khung PWM la
169 20ms nen 100ms la rat rong rai).
170 Phan con lai ben duoi CHI chay khi ngat chet - duong DU PHONG, giu lai
171 de mot loi cau hinh ngat khong lam servo dung han. */
172 if (s_ngat_song && ((now - s_ngat_moc) < 100u))
173 {
174 return;
175 }
176 
177 if ((now - s_tick) < SERVO_CHU_KY_MS)
178 {
179 return;
180 }
181 
182 /* BU THEO THOI GIAN THUC: vong lap chinh co the bi chan hang tram ms
183 (doc cam bien mau ton 33-130ms). Neu moi lan goi chi truot 1 buoc thi
184 servo nhich roi dung, nhin nhu GIAT. Tinh du so buoc dang le phai di
185 trong khoang thoi gian da troi qua. */
186 {
187 uint32_t so_buoc = (now - s_tick) / SERVO_CHU_KY_MS;
188 s_tick += so_buoc * SERVO_CHU_KY_MS;
189 
190 /* ⚠⚠ LOI DA GAP - "servo quay loan":
191 Tran cu la 20 buoc = 20 x 20us = 400us don vao MOT chu ky PWM. Hanh
192 trinh gat (180 do -> 90 do) chi co 900us, nen mot lan bu nhu vay lam
193 servo nhay gan NUA quang duong trong mot nhip => giat manh, nhin nhu
194 quay loan. Chinh co che chong giat tu gay ra giat.
195 Xay ra dung luc IR kich: vong lap bi chan boi in UART + bat dau chu
196 ky do mau 154ms, roi Servo_Task duoc goi lai va don het mot cuc.
197 Tran 3 buoc (60us) van bu duoc nhung khuc chan ngan ma khong the lam
198 servo nhay thay ro. Bi chan lau hon thi servo chi don gian di CHAM
199 hon - an toan hon nhieu so voi nhay coc. */
200 if (so_buoc > SERVO_BU_TOI_DA) { so_buoc = SERVO_BU_TOI_DA; }
201 buoc_lan_nay = (uint32_t)s_buoc_us * so_buoc; /* theo toc do CHANG */
202 }
203 
204 if (s_xung_ht == s_xung_dich)
205 {
206 return;
207 }
208 
209 /* Truot toi da SERVO_BUOC_US moi chu ky - khong nhay mot phat toi dich */
210 if (s_xung_dich > s_xung_ht)
211 {
212 uint32_t con_lai = (uint32_t)(s_xung_dich - s_xung_ht);
213 xuat_xung((uint16_t)(s_xung_ht +
214 ((con_lai > buoc_lan_nay) ? buoc_lan_nay : con_lai)));
215 }
216 else
217 {
218 uint32_t con_lai = (uint32_t)(s_xung_ht - s_xung_dich);
219 xuat_xung((uint16_t)(s_xung_ht -
220 ((con_lai > buoc_lan_nay) ? buoc_lan_nay : con_lai)));
221 }
222}
223 
224uint8_t Servo_DenNoi(void)
225{
226 return (uint8_t)(s_xung_ht == s_xung_dich);
227}
228 
229uint8_t Servo_GocDich(void)
230{
231 return s_goc_dich;
232}
233 
234uint16_t Servo_XungHienTai(void)
235{
236 return s_xung_ht;
237}

1/**

3 * @file led7seg_595.h
4 * @brief Hai bo dem 7 doan DOC LAP, moi board mot bo chan rieng
5 *
6 * ★★★ SUA 11/08/2026 - BO HAN KIEU NOI TIEP (daisy-chain).
7 * ==========================================================================
8 * BAN CU: hai board dung CHUNG SH_CP va ST_CP, board #1 day tiep du lieu
9 * sang board #2 qua chan Q7'. Tiet kiem chan nhung co ba diem yeu:
10 * - phai co mot soi day LECH CHAN (chan 1 board #1 -> chan 6 board #2),
11 * rat de cam nham thanh 1<->1 va dau hai dau ra choi nhau;
12 * - thu tu hai byte gui di phai dung, sai la hai den doi cho nhau;
13 * - mot xung nhieu tren duong chung lam sai CA HAI den cung luc.
14 *
15 * BAN NAY: moi board mot bo BA chan rieng, khong dung chung mot chan nao.
16 * Giua hai board CHI CON day nguon (J2/J3), khong con day tin hieu nao.
17 * Doi lai ton them 3 chan cua STM32 - deu la chan dang de trong.
18 *
19 * PHAN CHAN:
20 * Board #1 - dem phoi DO Board #2 - dem phoi XANH DUONG
21 * PB12 -> ST_CP (IC chan 12) PB3 -> ST_CP (IC chan 12)
22 * PB13 -> SH_CP (IC chan 11) PB4 -> SH_CP (IC chan 11)
23 * PB15 -> DS (IC chan 14) PB5 -> DS (IC chan 14)
24 * Ca hai board: MR (chan 10) len VCC, OE (chan 13) xuong GND - bang hai
25 * jumper ngay tren J1 (chan 2 <-> chan 8 va chan 5 <-> chan 7).
26 * Chan Q7' (J1 chan 1) cua CA HAI board deu BO TRONG.
27 *
28 * ⚠ PB3, PB4, PB5 von la chan JTAG (JTDO / NJTRST / JTDI-lan-can). Chung da
29 * duoc giai phong boi __HAL_AFIO_REMAP_SWJ_NOJTAG() trong HAL_MspInit, lenh
30 * do chay VO DIEU KIEN ngay trong HAL_Init nen luon co hieu luc. Nap va
31 * debug bang ST-Link KHONG bi anh huong (SWD chi dung PA13/PA14).
32 *
33 * ⚠ KHONG CON DUNG SPI2. Ban nay dap chan bang phan mem cho ca hai board:
34 * - hai kenh doc lap can hai xung clock rieng, ma STM32F103C8 chi co mot
35 * SPI2 (SPI1 thi chan PA5/PA7 nam ke chan servo PA6 - de cam nham);
36 * - day 8 bit het khoang 16us, khong dang ke so voi cac moc thoi gian cua
37 * gat_task (250 / 800 / 2000 / 5000 ms);
38 * - do rong xung tu chinh duoc qua LED595_TRE_XUNG, co ich vi day tin
39 * hieu di ngoai troi canh bo day dong luc 24V.
40 * Nho vay PB14 (SPI2_MISO) cung duoc tra lai thanh chan trong.
41 *
42 * ★ HAI BO DEM HOAN TOAN DOC LAP - KHONG phai mot so hai chu so.
43 * Moi den chay 0 -> 9 roi QUAY VE 0, tuyet doi khong co nho (carry) sang
44 * den ben canh. Vi vay trong file nay chi co phep chia lay DU (% 10),
45 * KHONG co bat ky phep / 10 nao de tinh hang chuc.
46 ******************************************************************************
47 */
48 
49#ifndef __LED7SEG_595_H
50#define __LED7SEG_595_H
51

53extern "C" {
54#endif
55 
56#include "main.h"

58/* ====================== THONG SO CAN HIEU CHINH ============================ */
59 
60/* ★★★ CONG TAC KHOANH VUNG LOI.
61 1 = khoi hien thi hoat dong binh thuong (mac dinh).
62 0 = TAT HAN khoi hien thi: moi ham thanh rong, KHONG cham vao chan nao,
63 khong ton mot chu ky CPU nao. Firmware quay ve y het ban
64 BangTai_PhanLoai_V2_1_NoUART goc.
65 DUNG KHI NAO: he co trieu chung la (nut khong an, servo lung tung, bang
66 tai khong chay...) va can biet CHAC no co phai do khoi hien thi gay ra hay
67 khong. Dat 0, dich lai, nap:
68 - Loi BIEN MAT => nguyen nhan nam trong khoi hien thi.
69 - Loi VAN CON => nguyen nhan nam cho khac.
70 Do xong nho dat lai 1. */
71#define LED595_BAT 1u
72 
73/* ★★★ CHIEU PHAN CUC CUA LED 7 DOAN - DOI DUNG MOT SO NAY NEU DEN KHONG SANG.
74 0 = theo dung ban ve Proteus / PCB hien tai: chan chung CA1/CA2 di XUONG
75 GND qua R1, tuc la chan chung dong vai tro CATHODE. Doan sang khi dau
76 ra 595 o muc CAO. Ma so dung bang 0x3F, 0x06, 0x5B...
77 1 = LED ANODE CHUNG that (chan chung noi len +5V). Doan sang khi dau ra
78 595 o muc THAP. Ma so la gia tri DAO BIT: 0xC0, 0xF9, 0xA4...
79 ⚠⚠ Cach thu 30 giay TRUOC KHI HAN: de dong ho o thang do DIODE, que DO vao
80 chan chung, que DEN cham lan luot cac chan doan - neu doan sang thi LED la
81 ANODE CHUNG => dat so nay = 1. */
82#define LED595_ANODE_CHUNG 1u
83 
84/* ★★★★ ANH XA DOAN -> BIT. SUA O DAY KHI DEN HIEN "NET DUT".
85 ==========================================================================
86 TRIEU CHUNG: bat ca 7 doan (so 8) thi ra so 8 LIEN NET, nhung cac so khac
87 deu bi gay net va khong doc ra so may.
88 NGUYEN NHAN: con LED that co thu tu chan doan KHAC voi footprint tren PCB
89 (moi hang danh chan mot kieu). Moi chu so bi xao thanh, rieng so 8 thi bat
90 het 7 doan nen xao kieu gi cung van ra so 8 dung.
91 
92 BANG NAY tra loi cau hoi: "doan X duoc lai boi BIT nao cua byte gui ra".
93 LED595_BIT_A = so bit dieu khien doan A (thanh ngang tren cung)
94 LED595_BIT_B = so bit dieu khien doan B (thanh doc tren ben phai)
95 ... va tuong tu.
96 
97 CACH DO RA BANG NAY: nap ban TEST_LED595_2BOARD voi SOI_TUNG_DOAN = 1.
98 Firmware bat lan luot bit0..bit7, moi bit 1.5 giay, va in ra dang bat bit
99 nao. Ghi lai THANH NAO thuc su sang o moi buoc, roi dien vao day.
100 Vi du do duoc "bit 0 lam sang thanh ngang duoi cung (doan D)" thi dat
101 LED595_BIT_D = 0.
102 
103 ⚠ Tam gia tri 0..7 va KHONG duoc trung nhau. Mac dinh 0..7 la dung theo
104 ban ve Proteus (Q0->A, Q1->B, ... Q6->G, Q7->DP). */
105#define LED595_BIT_A 0u
106#define LED595_BIT_B 1u
107#define LED595_BIT_C 2u
108#define LED595_BIT_D 3u
109#define LED595_BIT_E 4u
110/* ★★ DO THUC TE 12/08/2026 tren board that: hai doan F va G bi TRAO NHAU so
111 voi ban ve. Q5 chay doan G, Q6 chay doan F. Ket luan tu 10/10 phep do
112 khop nhau, khong sot truong hop nao - xem bang doi chieu trong bao cao. */
113#define LED595_BIT_F 6u
114#define LED595_BIT_G 5u
115#define LED595_BIT_DP 7u
116 
117/* Do rong nua chu ky xung SH_CP, tinh bang so lan lap NOP o 72MHz.
118 ★★★ SUA 12/08/2026: 8 -> 40 (tuc ~2.2us moi nua chu ky, 1 bit ~4.4us, ca
119 hai board day xong het ~70us). Cung mot ly do voi viec ha toc do chan
120 xuong 2MHz: dap DOI XUNG tren day cam breadboard dai. Xung rong ra thi
121 moi dao dong con sot lai co du thoi gian tat han truoc suon ke tiep.
122 ⚠ Neu VAN dut net, tang tiep len 100 roi 200. Cham lai khong mat gi -
123 vong lap chinh quay 174ms mot lan, 70us hay 350us deu khong dang ke.
124 Khi day da di gon gang, on dinh roi thi ha lai ve 8 cung duoc. */
125#define LED595_TRE_XUNG 40u
126 
127/* Chu ky day lai toan bo noi dung (ms), ke ca khi so khong doi.
128 Muc dich: tu chua khi ghi dich bi nhieu lam sai - bu cho viec 74HC595 chay
129 5V nhung nhan tin hieu 3.3V (V_IH = 3.5V). Dat 0 de tat. */
130#define LED595_LAM_TUOI_MS 500u
131 
132/* ============================ GIAO DIEN ==================================== */
133

135 hai den. PHAI goi SAU MX_GPIO_Init() va SAU MX_TIM4_Init(), tuc la trong
136 khoi USER CODE 2 cua main.c. */
137void LED595_Init(void);
138 
139/* Dat so cho hai den. Gia tri >9 se tu duoc lay phan du cho 10.
140 Ham TU SO SANH voi gia tri dang hien: chi day xung khi that su doi, nen
141 goi moi vong lap cung khong ton gi. */
142void LED595_DatSo(uint8_t so_do, uint8_t so_xanh);
143 
144/* Goi trong vong lap chinh. Cu moi LED595_LAM_TUOI_MS thi day lai mot lan. */
145void LED595_Task(uint32_t now);
146 
147/* Tat het ca hai den (khong doan nao sang). */
148void LED595_Tat(void);

150/* Tu kiem tra phan cung: sang het 7 doan, roi chay 0..9 tren CA HAI den, roi
151 tra ve gia tri dang dem. Chay MOT LAN luc khoi dong.
152 ⚠ CHAN khoang 1.7 giay (dung HAL_Delay) nen CHI duoc goi trong USER CODE 2,
153 TUYET DOI khong goi trong vong lap chinh. */
154void LED595_TuKiemTra(void);

157 bit0=A bit1=B bit2=C bit3=D bit4=E bit5=F bit6=G bit7=DP, bit=1 la SANG.
158 Ham tu lo phan dao bit khi LED595_ANODE_CHUNG=1.
159 Dung de soi TUNG DOAN khi den hien "net dut": xem doan nao that su sang
160 ung voi bit nao, tu do biet anh xa Q0..Q6 -> A..G co dung khong.
161 ⚠ Noi dung tho chi ton tai toi lan LED595_Task() ke tiep. */

Bit-banged output to two independent shift registers

Device
STM32F103C8T6
Clock
72 MHz
Lines

1/**
2 ******************************************************************************
3 * @file led7seg_595.c
4 * @brief Hai bo dem 7 doan DOC LAP, moi board mot bo chan rieng
5 *
6 * Xem toan bo giai thich phan cung o led7seg_595.h.

12#if (LED595_BAT == 0u)
13/* ===== KHOI HIEN THI DA BI TAT bang LED595_BAT = 0 (xem led7seg_595.h) =====
14 Toan bo than file duoc thay bang cac ham rong. Khong dong vao chan nao.

26/* ==================== BO CHAN CUA MOI BOARD ================================
27 Ca 6 chan deu nam tren GPIOB nen dat/xoa duoc bang mot lenh ghi BSRR.
28 ⚠ NEU DOI CHAN: chi sua hai dong khoi tao ben duoi, KHONG phai sua cho nao
29 khac trong file. Nhung chan moi BAT BUOC phai van thuoc GPIOB - toan bo
30 lop duoi cua file nay ghi thang vao GPIOB. */
31typedef struct
32{
33 uint8_t chot; /* ST_CP - IC chan 12 */
34 uint8_t sck; /* SH_CP - IC chan 11 */
35 uint8_t dat; /* DS - IC chan 14 */
36} LED595_Chan_t;
37 
38/* Board #1 = den dem phoi DO. Giu nguyen bo chan cu de khong phai thao day. */
39static const LED595_Chan_t s_board1 = { 12u, 13u, 15u };
40/* Board #2 = den dem phoi XANH DUONG. Ba chan lien nhau tren hang chan phai
41 cua Blue Pill, cam mot khoi 3 chan la xong. */
42static const LED595_Chan_t s_board2 = { 3u, 4u, 5u };
43 
44/* ==================== BANG MA 7 DOAN (Muc 5.3 cua tai lieu) ================
45 Anh xa bit theo phan cung: bit0=A(Q0) bit1=B(Q1) bit2=C(Q2) bit3=D(Q3)
46 bit4=E(Q4) bit5=F(Q5) bit6=G(Q6) bit7=DP(Q7).
47 Chan Q7 (dau cham DP) KHONG duoc dau tren board nen bit7 luon = 0. */
48static const uint8_t g_ma7[10] =
49{
50 0x3Fu, /* 0 : A B C D E F */
51 0x06u, /* 1 : B C */
52 0x5Bu, /* 2 : A B D E G */
53 0x4Fu, /* 3 : A B C D G */
54 0x66u, /* 4 : B C F G */
55 0x6Du, /* 5 : A C D F G */
56 0x7Du, /* 6 : A C D E F G */
57 0x07u, /* 7 : A B C */
58 0x7Fu, /* 8 : A B C D E F G */
59 0x6Fu /* 9 : A B C D F G */
60};
61 
62/* ==================== TRANG THAI ========================================== */
63static uint8_t s_da_init; /* 1 = da cau hinh xong 6 chan */
64static uint8_t s_so_do = 0u; /* so DANG hien tren den board #1 */
65static uint8_t s_so_xanh = 0u; /* so DANG hien tren den board #2 */
66static uint32_t s_moc_lam_tuoi; /* tick lan day du lieu gan nhat */
67 
68/* ==================== LOP DUOI: dieu khien chan ========================== */
69 
70static void dat_cao(uint8_t chan)
71{
72 GPIOB->BSRR = (uint32_t)(1uL << chan);
73}
74 
75static void dat_thap(uint8_t chan)
76{
77 GPIOB->BSRR = (uint32_t)(1uL << ((uint32_t)chan + 16u));
78}
79 
80/* Nua chu ky xung. Dung __NOP() (lenh assembly) de trinh dich khong toi uu
81 mat vong lap nay. */
82static void tre_ngan(void)
83{
84 uint32_t n;
85 
86 for (n = 0u; n < (uint32_t)LED595_TRE_XUNG; n++)
87 {
88 __NOP();
89 }
90}
91 
92/* Dat MOT chan cua GPIOB thanh Output push-pull 2MHz.
93 ★★★ SUA 12/08/2026 - HA TU 10MHz XUONG 2MHz.
94 TRIEU CHUNG DA GAP: so 8 hien HOAN CHINH nhung moi so khac deu dut net.
95 Ma cua so 8 la 0x7F - TOAN BIT 1, khong co mot chuyen muc nao tren duong
96 du lieu. Loi anh xa chan thi lam hong so 8 y het cac so khac. Chi co MOT
97 loai loi tha cho so 8: du lieu hong luc TRUYEN.
98 NGUYEN NHAN: suon xung o toc do 10MHz rat doc (~5ns). Di tren day cam
99 breadboard dai, khong co duong GND chay kem, day thanh mot mach LC va bi
100 DOI (ringing). Moi cai doi la mot suon len GIA -> 74HC595 dich them nhip
101 -> bit nam sai cho. Chuoi toan 1 thi dich thua bao nhieu nhip cung van ra
102 toan 1, nen rieng so 8 khong bi anh huong - dung nhu quan sat duoc.
103 CACH CHUA: 2MHz cho suon thoai hon han (~25ns), gan nhu het doi. Toc do
104 nay van thua xa nhu cau - ca hai board day xong chi ton vai chuc micro
105 giay, trong khi vong lap chinh quay 174ms mot lan.
106 
107 ⚠ CRL giu chan 0..7, CRH giu chan 8..15; moi chan chiem 4 bit. Bat buoc
108 phai doc - sua - ghi CO MAT NA, tuyet doi khong ghi de ca thanh ghi:
109 - CRL con dang giu PB6/PB7 la chan encoder TIM4;
110 - CRH con dang giu PB10/PB11 la chan I2C2 cua cam bien mau.
111 Ghi de ca thanh ghi la mat encoder hoac mat cam bien ngay lap tuc. */
112static void cau_hinh_1_chan(uint8_t chan)
113{
114 __IO uint32_t *reg;
115 uint32_t vt;
116 uint32_t v;
117 
118 reg = (chan < 8u) ? &GPIOB->CRL : &GPIOB->CRH;
119 vt = (uint32_t)((chan & 0x07u) * 4u);
120 
121 v = *reg;
122 v &= ~(0xFuL << vt);
123 v |= (0x2uL << vt); /* MODE=10 (2MHz), CNF=00 (GP push-pull) */
124 *reg = v;
125}
126 
127static void cau_hinh_bo_chan(const LED595_Chan_t *c)
128{
129 cau_hinh_1_chan(c->chot);
130 cau_hinh_1_chan(c->sck);
131 cau_hinh_1_chan(c->dat);
132 
133 dat_thap(c->chot); /* ba chan deu nghi o muc THAP */
134 dat_thap(c->sck);
135 dat_thap(c->dat);
136}
137 
138/* ==================== LOP TREN: day mot byte ra MOT board ================= */
139 
140/* ★★ HOAN VI DOAN -> BIT theo bang LED595_BIT_x trong led7seg_595.h.
141 Bang g_ma7 o tren luon viet theo quy uoc CHUAN (bit0=A ... bit6=G), con
142 ham nay doi sang thu tu chan THAT cua con LED dang dung. Mac dinh la hoan
143 vi dong nhat nen khong doi gi; chi khi con LED co chan khac footprint thi
144 moi phai sua bang trong file .h.
145 ⚠ Chay 8 vong, va chi chay khi so DOI hoac khi lam tuoi (moi 500ms) nen
146 chi phi bang khong. */
147static uint8_t hoan_vi_doan(uint8_t m)
148{
149 static const uint8_t vt[8] =
150 {
151 LED595_BIT_A, LED595_BIT_B, LED595_BIT_C, LED595_BIT_D,
152 LED595_BIT_E, LED595_BIT_F, LED595_BIT_G, LED595_BIT_DP
153 };
154 uint8_t r = 0u;
155 uint8_t i;
156 
157 for (i = 0u; i < 8u; i++)
158 {
159 if ((m & (uint8_t)(1u << i)) != 0u)
160 {
161 r |= (uint8_t)(1u << vt[i]);
162 }
163 }
164 return r;
165}
166 
167static uint8_t ma_cua(uint8_t so)
168{
169 uint8_t m = (so <= 9u) ? g_ma7[so] : 0x00u;
170 
171 m = hoan_vi_doan(m);
172 
173#if (LED595_ANODE_CHUNG != 0u)
174 m = (uint8_t)(~m); /* anode chung: sang o muc THAP => dao bit */
175#endif
176 
177 return m;
178}
179 
180/* ★★★ Day 8 bit vao MOT board roi chot.
181 Gui MSB TRUOC: bit nao vao chan DS truoc thi bi day di xa nhat, nen sau du
182 8 xung, bit7 dung o Q7 (DP) va bit0 dung o Q0 (doan A) - dung y het anh xa
183 ghi o bang ma phia tren, KHONG phai dao bit trong phan mem.
184 
185 Trinh tu moi bit: dat muc cho DS -> cho on dinh -> nhac SH_CP LEN (595 lay
186 mau dung o suon nay) -> cho -> ha SH_CP xuong.
187 Sau 8 bit moi nhac ST_CP mot lan: suon LEN cua ST_CP day noi dung ghi dich
188 ra cac chan Q. Chot GIUA CHUNG se lam den hien so dang dich do dang. */
189static void day_1_byte(const LED595_Chan_t *c, uint8_t ma)
190{
191 uint8_t i;
192 
193 for (i = 0u; i < 8u; i++)
194 {
195 if ((ma & 0x80u) != 0u) { dat_cao(c->dat); }
196 else { dat_thap(c->dat); }
197 ma = (uint8_t)(ma << 1);
198 
199 tre_ngan();
200 dat_cao(c->sck); /* suon LEN - 595 lay mau bit */
201 tre_ngan();
202 dat_thap(c->sck);
203 }
204 
205 tre_ngan();
206 dat_cao(c->chot); /* suon LEN - day noi dung ra chan Q */
207 tre_ngan();
208 dat_thap(c->chot);
209}
210 
211/* Hai board hoan toan doc lap nen day lan luot, moi board mot byte tren bo
212 chan cua rieng no. Khong con thu tu byte nao de nham, va cung khong con
213 day tin hieu nao chay giua hai board. */
214static void day_ma(uint8_t ma_b1, uint8_t ma_b2)
215{
216 day_1_byte(&s_board1, ma_b1);
217 day_1_byte(&s_board2, ma_b2);
218}
219 
220static void day_ra(uint8_t so_do, uint8_t so_xanh)
221{
222 day_ma(ma_cua(so_do), ma_cua(so_xanh));
223}
224 
225/* ==================== GIAO DIEN CONG KHAI ================================= */
226 
227void LED595_Init(void)
228{
229 __HAL_RCC_GPIOB_CLK_ENABLE();
230 
231 cau_hinh_bo_chan(&s_board1);
232 cau_hinh_bo_chan(&s_board2);
233 
234 s_da_init = 1u;
235 s_so_do = 0u;
236 s_so_xanh = 0u;
237 s_moc_lam_tuoi = HAL_GetTick();
238 
239 day_ra(0u, 0u); /* ca hai den hien so 0 ngay khi cap nguon */
240}
241 
242void LED595_DatSo(uint8_t so_do, uint8_t so_xanh)
243{
244 uint8_t d, x;
245 
246 if (s_da_init == 0u)
247 {
248 return;
249 }
250 
251 /* ★ CHI lay phan DU cho 10. Hai bo dem doc lap, khong co nho sang nhau,
252 nen o day KHONG duoc phep xuat hien phep / 10 de tinh hang chuc. */
253 d = (uint8_t)(so_do % 10u);
254 x = (uint8_t)(so_xanh % 10u);
255 
256 if ((d == s_so_do) && (x == s_so_xanh))
257 {
258 return; /* khong doi -> khong day mot xung nao */
259 }
260 
261 s_so_do = d;
262 s_so_xanh = x;
263 s_moc_lam_tuoi = HAL_GetTick();
264 
265 day_ra(d, x);
266}
267 
268void LED595_Task(uint32_t now)
269{
270#if (LED595_LAM_TUOI_MS > 0u)
271 if (s_da_init == 0u)
272 {
273 return;
274 }
275 
276 if ((now - s_moc_lam_tuoi) >= (uint32_t)LED595_LAM_TUOI_MS)
277 {
278 s_moc_lam_tuoi = now;
279 day_ra(s_so_do, s_so_xanh); /* day lai y nguyen - tu chua neu bi nhieu */
280 }
281#else
282 (void)now;
283#endif
284}
285 
286void LED595_Tat(void)
287{
288 if (s_da_init == 0u)
289 {
290 return;
291 }
292 day_ra(0xFFu, 0xFFu); /* >9 => ma_cua() tra ve "khong doan nao" */
293}
294 
295void LED595_TuKiemTra(void)
296{
297 uint8_t i;
298 
299 if (s_da_init == 0u)
300 {
301 return;
302 }
303 
304 day_ra(8u, 8u); /* sang het 7 doan tren ca hai den */
305 HAL_Delay(500u);
306 
307 for (i = 0u; i <= 9u; i++)
308 {
309 day_ra(i, i);
310 HAL_Delay(100u);
311 }
312 
313 LED595_Tat();
314 HAL_Delay(200u);
315 
316 day_ra(s_so_do, s_so_xanh); /* tra ve dung gia tri dang dem */
317 s_moc_lam_tuoi = HAL_GetTick();
318}
319 
320/* ★★ CHAN DOAN: bat thang cac doan theo mat na, khong qua bang ma 7 doan.
321 bit0=A bit1=B bit2=C bit3=D bit4=E bit5=F bit6=G bit7=DP, bit=1 la SANG
322 (ham tu lo phan dao bit khi LED595_ANODE_CHUNG=1, nguoi goi khong phai nghi).
323 Dung de soi TUNG DOAN mot khi den hien "net dut" - xem doan nao that su
324 sang ung voi bit nao, tu do biet bang ma co khop voi chan LED that khong.
325 ⚠ Noi dung tho nay chi ton tai toi lan LED595_Task() ke tiep (toi da
326 LED595_LAM_TUOI_MS) - sau do den tu tro ve gia tri dang dem. */
327void LED595_DatDoanTho(uint8_t doan_b1, uint8_t doan_b2)
328{
329 if (s_da_init == 0u)
330 {
331 return;
332 }
333 
334#if (LED595_ANODE_CHUNG != 0u)
335 doan_b1 = (uint8_t)(~doan_b1);
336 doan_b2 = (uint8_t)(~doan_b2);
337#endif
338 
339 day_ma(doan_b1, doan_b2);
340 s_moc_lam_tuoi = HAL_GetTick(); /* hoan lan lam tuoi ke tiep lai */
341}
342 
343uint8_t LED595_SoDangHienDo(void) { return s_so_do; }
344uint8_t LED595_SoDangHienXanh(void) { return s_so_xanh; }
345 
346#endif /* LED595_BAT */
